# Card DSL and effect pipeline

Rules data for cards (`mtg-cards`) and the pipeline that runs it (`mtg-engine`).

Scope: the data model, loader, validation, support records, the corpus and card assembly are implemented in `crates/mtg-cards`, with the engine's capabilities in `mtg-engine` and the card source in `mtg-server`. The pipeline core ("Execution", "Decisions the effects need", "Snapshots") is implemented in `mtg-engine` for targets, modes, cards chosen by a player and put in an order, yes or no, payments, options, drawing, discarding, sacrificing, destroying, exiling, countering, scrying, looking at, revealing, searching for, moving (onto the battlefield too) and shuffling cards, life, damage, mana, tokens and counters (put, removed and moved on permanents and players); "Triggers, replacement and layers" is implemented for triggers (detection, the queue, order, delayed triggers, the intervening "if" and the limit) and for the layer rows of continuous effects that pump power and toughness or grant a keyword (durations, timestamps, derivation); the replacement step (tokens, damage, zone changes, counters put, life gained, cards drawn and entering, with ChooseReplacement) is implemented; the other layer rows and "Tasks" are the contract of the other M3 engine tasks. Related: `engine.md`, `protocol.md`, `deck-check.md`, `arena-flow.md`.

## Summary

| Area | Rule |
|---|---|
| Printed side | Name, cost, types, power, toughness and oracle text come from the imported Scryfall record. |
| Rules file | One JSON file per oracle card: abilities per face, keyed by oracle id, with the fingerprint of the oracle text they match. |
| Vocabulary | Verbs (`Effect`, `TriggerPattern`, `CostItem`, `Modification`, `Keyword`) and values (`Ref`, `Selector`, `Amount`, `Condition`). A primitive is one enum variant. |
| Execution | An effect list runs as a journal: each step is a pure function of state that yields events or asks one `Decision`; answers are logged as events. |
| Stack | Abilities are stack objects. Targets are chosen on announcement and rechecked on resolution (CR 608.2b). |
| Triggers | Detected from events (look-back ones against the state before the event), queued in state, put on the stack in APNAP order. |
| Replacement | A step in front of the fold; the affected player orders several (CR 616.1). |
| Static abilities | Derived when characteristics are derived (CR 613); never stored as effects. |
| Keywords | Enum variants with parameters; behaviour in one engine file each. |
| Support | `Verified`, `Partial`, `Manual`, `Stale` and `Unplayable` are computed from the file, the imported text and the engine's capabilities. |

## Decisions

| # | Decision | Reason | Rejected |
|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | Format: JSON, one file per oracle card. Serde's externally tagged enums, PascalCase variants, snake_case fields; unknown fields are errors; defaults are omitted. Short forms: a mana cost is a string (`"{X}{R}"`), a fixed `Amount` a number, a mana-only `Cost` a string | The database stores JSON, the M6 compiler emits data, the web reads it, errors carry a path (`serde_path_to_error`) | RON (no jsonb, no TypeScript reader), YAML and TOML (typing traps, awkward trees), Rust builders like `vanilla.rs` (not data), a text script (needs its own parser) |
| D2 | Identity: the key is the Scryfall `oracle_id`; the file is `cards/<slug>.json` at any depth; a predefined token is `cards/tokens/<id>.json` | `cards.oracle_id` is the database key; names change and collide with face names | A slug as key (a rename breaks joins), per-printing ids (rules belong to the oracle card), a sharded layout now (sharding is a rename of files) |
| D3 | Multi-face: one file per oracle card, `faces` in Scryfall order; a whole-card property (Fuse) is a keyword on face 0 | Decks and imports name the card; faces share identity and status | One file per face, a `layout` field (a copy of import data) |
| D4 | Characteristics: the printed side always comes from the import record; the file holds abilities and a per-face `oracle_fingerprint`; a token carries its own characteristics | Cards without a file need the record anyway, so there is one source; errata surface as drift; M6 generates thousands of entries | Copying cost, types and P/T into each file (a second source, silent typos) |
| D5 | Shape: verbs and values are separate; sequences are lists; results are named (`as`, `Bound`) | Names are validated; a new mechanic is one verb over existing values | Implicit anaphora (`last created`), one flat enum, a class per card |
| D6 | Support is computed, never stored | `Verified` cannot go stale or ignore an engine that lacks a primitive; no engine run | A stored flag, running tests per lookup |
| D7 | Execution is a journal of pure steps | State stays the fold of the log; a restart asks the same question; each step is testable | Blocking callbacks per choice, unlogged resolution flushed at the end, replay from the start on every answer |
| D8 | Triggers: detection from events, a queue in state | Detection is a function of events and data; the log shows why an ability appeared; a resume keeps the queue | Detection inside the fold, a queue outside state |
| D9 | Replacement: a step in front of the fold | The fold stays dumb; the log holds what happened | A `replaced` flag on events, replacement inside each primitive, a fixed global order |
| D10 | Static abilities are derived | Characteristics are already derived; layer dependencies come from the data | Registering statics as `ContinuousEffect`s, a per-card layer table |
| D11 | Keywords: one enum, one engine file each | An exhaustive `match` flags a forgotten keyword; parallel tasks touch separate files | Keywords as strings, a shared registry file |
| D12 | Validation in three levels; all errors are collected | A file is judged where the information is | Checking when the engine uses a card, stopping at the first error |
| D13 | The compiler writes the same files; an unreadable clause becomes a `Manual` ability | One reader and one validator; a deck is never blocked | A second format, refusing decks with unimplemented cards |

## Files

| Path | Content |
|---|---|
| `crates/mtg-cards/cards/<slug>.json` | Rules of one oracle card |
| `crates/mtg-cards/cards/tokens/<id>.json` | A predefined token (Treasure, Clue, Food, Gold, Blood, Junk, Powerstone, Vibranium, Map, Mutagen) |
| `crates/mtg-cards/tests/fixtures/corpus/<slug>.json` | The imported Scryfall object of one corpus card |
| `crates/mtg-cards/tests/fixtures/reproduced.txt` | The ratchet list: the corpus cards the compiler reproduces, one name per line, sorted |

- `mtg_cards::builtin::library()` is the library of these files, embedded at build time (`build.rs`), so a server needs no directory and a deploy carries the rules it was tested with.
- Canonical text: compact JSON in serde's field order, laid out by `mtg_cards::pretty` (a value stays on one line up to 100 columns, otherwise one entry per line).
- `MTG_UPDATE_CARDS=1 cargo test -p mtg-cards corpus::` rewrites the files and their fingerprints; the test fails on a file that is not canonical.
- `cargo test -p mtg-cards hand_written::` checks every rules file as a file of its own (`Library::check_generated`) against its fixture record and against the newest `oracle-cards-*.jsonl.gz` of `MTG_REAL_DATA_DIR` or the `mtg-cli` cache; without a download it checks the fixture only and says so. An erratum fails the card it changed: fix the rules, or refresh the fingerprint when they still fit.
- `cargo test -p mtg-cards fixture_real::` compares the fixture records with the same download; without one it says so and passes.

## Adding a corpus card

No test holds a count of cards, sources, compiled sources, reproduced cards or fingerprints; each is derived from the card directory and the fixture, and a test asserts what the number stood for. A new card needs:

1. Its rules file `crates/mtg-cards/cards/<slug>.json` (the slug is the card's name lowercased with every run of non-alphanumerics as `_`, `mtg_cards::load::slug`). A `Full` or `Partial` face starts with an empty `oracle_fingerprint`.
2. Its Scryfall object as the new file `crates/mtg-cards/tests/fixtures/corpus/<slug>.json`, with the same slug: pretty-printed JSON of one object and a final newline (a multi-face or split card is one file named after the whole card name). The rules file and the fixture file share the `oracle_id`, and each card has exactly one of each. No other file changes for this step, so branches adding different cards never conflict here.
3. `MTG_UPDATE_CARDS=1 cargo test -p mtg-cards corpus::` writes the rules file in canonical form with the fingerprints of the fixture's text and adds every card the compiler reproduces to `crates/mtg-cards/tests/fixtures/reproduced.txt`. It never removes a name: a listed card that stops being reproduced fails.
4. `cargo test -p mtg-cards -p mtg-testkit`. A card the engine plays whole needs no row. A card it does not (Manual or Partial) is a row of `NOT_VERIFIED` in `crates/mtg-testkit/tests/it/corpus_cards.rs` with its state and the number of abilities it carries; a Verified card carries the abilities of its file, and each basic land type adds its mana ability.

`reproduced.txt` is a plain list in sorted order, one name per line, and the update inserts a name at its sorted position. Two branches that add different names conflict only where the names are adjacent; the resolution keeps both lines, and the update command restores the order if it is off.

The tests report a card without its record or file, a fixture file that is not named after its card or lacks the final newline, two files with one oracle id, a stale or missing fingerprint, an unlisted reproduced card and an unexpected state with the card's name, the file to add and this section. The fixture directory loads in file-name order and no test depends on that order. `tests/it/adding_a_card.rs` adds a card to a copy of the directory and the fixture directory and shows that no count or table needs an edit.

## Printed side and rules

The printed side of every card comes from the import record (`mtg_import::CardRecord`, stored in the `cards` table). The rules file adds abilities. A deck of oracle ids becomes `CardDefinition`s in the server (`CardSource`, `DeckRef::Oracle`):

| Step | Action |
|---|---|
| 1 | `mtg-db` reads one `cards` row per distinct oracle id of the deck (`CardRepo::get_many`); every id without a row is named in the error. |
| 2 | The server adapts each stored row to `mtg_cards::PrintedCard` (`mtg_server::printed_card`; strings only, `mtg-cards` does not depend on `mtg-import`). The testkit does the same from the record itself. |
| 3 | `assemble(&PrintedCard, Option<&Card>, &Caps) -> Assembled { definition, support }` reads the front face: cost (`mana_cost` without its `{X}` symbols, and `x_in_cost`), type line, power, toughness, loyalty, defense and colours (`color_indicator` only where the record's colours differ from the cost's). It gives a land the mana abilities of its basic land types (CR 305.6) and attaches the rules the support record says the engine runs. A card with no file is `Verified` when it has no rules text once reminder text (parentheses) is removed, such as a vanilla creature or a basic land, and `Manual` otherwise. |
| 4 | `PlayerSetup.library` gets one `Arc<CardDefinition>` per deck line; copies share it. `CardDefinition`, `AbilityDef` and `ManaAbility` live in `mtg-cards`; `AbilityDef::Mana` is the lowered plain `{T}: Add {X}`; every other ability the engine runs is an `Arc<Ability>` in `CardDefinition.rules`, named by its index there (`lower` gives `Lowered::Mana` or `Lowered::Rules`). The field is left out of the serde form while it is empty. |
| 5 | The recipe's deck fingerprint (`recipe/fingerprint.rs`) hashes the serde form of the definitions, so a corrected record, changed rules or a change in what the engine runs changes it and a resumed game fails as a changed deck does. Fields added to `CardDefinition` are left out of that form while unused, so `FINGERPRINT_VERSION` stays 1. |

A printed gap makes the card `Manual` and attaches no rules. `support` computes it, so the deck check and the game agree; a `Caps` name lifts it once the engine plays the value.

| Printed value | In the definition | Lifted by |
|---|---|---|
| `*`, `1+*` or `X` as power, toughness, loyalty or defense | `None` | `Card.VariableStats` |
| `{X}` in the cost | `x_in_cost`; the engine asks X as the spell is cast and the spell, its permanent, its enters trigger and the delayed triggers it sets up remember it; its cast triggers read it | `Card.XCost` |
| A second face | A split card, an Adventure, a modal double-faced card and a transforming double-faced card (`PrintedCard.layout`: `split`, `adventure`, `modal_dfc`, `transform`, `flip`, `prepare`, two faces) are played with their faces, each face assembled like a card of its own (`CardDefinition.layout` and `faces`; a split card's definition is both halves, the others their front face, and the bottom half of a flip card has the mana cost and colour of its top half; a split card with `Fuse` also carries the spell of both halves, their targets in order and the effects of the left half then the right half with its targets renumbered); a `prepare` card is its creature face and plays its spell face as a copy cast by the prepared permanent, `docs/engine.md`; any other card with a second face (meld) is only its front face and names the face | `Card.Faces`, `Card.Prepare` |
| A mana symbol the engine does not know, an unreadable cost, a type word that is no card type | No cost, or the word dropped | Nothing |

## The type tree

All types are in `crates/mtg-cards/src`. Notation: `Name { field: Type = default }`.

**Card and face**
```
Card      { oracle_id, name, impl_status: ImplStatus, faces: [Face], notes? }
Face      { name, oracle_fingerprint, abilities: [Ability] }
ImplStatus = Full | Partial | Manual | Unplayable          declared by the file
TokenFile { id, token: TokenSpec }
TokenSpec { name, colors, supertypes, types, subtypes, power?, toughness?, abilities }
```

**Abilities** (`Spell`, `Activated`, `Loyalty` and `Triggered` carry `targets` and `effects`)
```
Spell      { targets, effects }                                  instants and sorceries
Activated  { cost: Cost, timing = Instant|Sorcery, condition?, limit?, from = Battlefield, targets, effects }
Loyalty    { cost: Plus(n) | Minus(n) | Zero | MinusX, targets, effects }     planeswalkers only
Triggered  { when: TriggerPattern, intervening_if?: Condition, limit?, from = Battlefield, targets, effects }
Static     { affected: Ref, mods: [Modification], condition?, defining = false, from = Battlefield }
Rule       { rule: GameRule, condition?, from = Battlefield }   a static ability that changes a rule of the game
Replacement{ when: ReplaceWhen, by: ReplaceBy, condition?, from = Battlefield }
CostModifier{ applies_to: Selector, change: Increase|Reduce, mana: ManaCostX, per = 1, condition? }
AdditionalCost{ cost: Cost, required = false }                    "As an additional cost to cast this spell, [you may] ..."; an optional one is read as Condition AdditionalCostPaid
FreeCast{ condition? }                                             "If [condition], you may cast this spell without paying its mana cost"; an alternative cost of no mana (`CastWay.Free`) while the condition holds
CastOnly{ condition }                                              "Cast this spell only during combat[ on an opponent's turn]"; the spell is cast only while the condition holds for the caster
Keyword(Keyword)
Manual     { reason: String, span: Span }                        an unmodelled clause
```
An activated ability is a mana ability (CR 605.1a) when it has no targets and one of its effects is `AddMana`; the effects after the mana (a painland's damage, an egg's draw) are part of it. Loyalty timing (CR 606.3) is engine code. The reader gives an "Activate only if you control a Swamp." sentence to the ability as its `condition`; "{T}: Add one mana of any color in your commander's color identity." is `CommanderIdentity` and "... of any color (or type) that a land an opponent (or you) controls could produce." is `ProducedBy` (`colorless` for "type"). "Add {G} for each creature you control." is `AddMana` with `times` counted by `Amount::Count`, read and written after the mana and before the spending restriction; "Activate only once each turn." is the `limit`, which a mana ability keeps (the engine logs a use and does not offer it again that turn); "Tap an untapped creature you control", "Sacrifice a Goblin" and "Exile a card from your hand" (Cadaverous Bloom) are cost parts of a mana ability the payment picks. A `OneOf` or `OneOfLists` choice has at most six ways (`assemble::MANA_WAYS`); the validator refuses more. "Add two mana in any combination of colors" (also "of {R} and/or {G}", "of {U}, {B}, and/or {R}") is `AnyCombination{count, among}` (none listed means the five colors); a mana ability runs it when its ways (`assemble::combinations`) fit in `MANA_COMBINATIONS`, and on the stack it asks for the type of each mana.

A `CostModifier` changes the total cost of the spells `applies_to` selects (the selector is read against a spell on the stack: `Spell`, a type, `ControlledBy(You)` for "you cast") by `mana` (fixed symbols, never `{X}`) as many times as `per` counts ("for each target beyond the first" is `Sum[TargetCount(0), -1]`). `This` selects the spell with the ability, the one place a card in a hand has a static ability. Increases come before reductions, and a reduction takes generic mana only down to nothing and a colored symbol only where the cost has it (`engine.md`, Announcement). Strive ("Strive — This spell costs {2}{U} more to cast for each target beyond the first.") is this ability: the compiler reads it with its ability word, a cost of several symbols included, and keeps it only beside a spell ability it reads.

A `CastOnly` is a casting restriction of the spell (CR 601.3): "Cast this spell only during combat." is `InCombat`, "... during combat on an opponent's turn" is `All[InCombat, Not(YourTurn)]` (`compile/read/w17c_cast_only.rs`, a spell rule in `classify.rs`, tests in `tests/w17c_cast_only.rs`). "While voting, you may vote an additional time." is `GameRule::VoteAgain(You)` (`statics.rs`, tests in `tests/w17c_vote_again.rs`). `Ability.CastOnly` and `Rule.VoteAgain` are in `Caps`.

A `FreeCast` with a `cost` is the printed alternative cost "You may sacrifice a Mountain rather than pay this spell's mana cost." or "If you control a Swamp, you may pay 4 life rather than pay this spell's mana cost." (`compile/read/w14c_alt_costs.rs`): mana, life or a part the caster pays with no X and nothing the spell pays itself (the validator refuses those). `Modification::BlocksAdditional(n)` is "can block an additional creature each combat" ("an additional two creatures" for 2) for the card itself or a group (`compile/read/w14c_block_capacity.rs`). Goldens and near-misses are in `compile/read/tests/w14c_*.rs`; Crash, Snuff Out, High Ground, Night Market Guard, Two-Headed Giant of Foriys and Brave the Sands are corpus cards.

A `FreeCast` is the printed "If you control a commander, you may cast this spell without paying its mana cost." (the condition is a count of what the caster controls: `Pred::Commander` is a player's commander, whoever controls it now). While the condition holds for the caster, the card has the alternative cost `CastWay.Free` beside its mana cost in every zone it is cast for that cost from; the additional costs, the commander tax and the cost modifiers stay (CR 118.9d). The compiler reads and writes the sentence for any "you control" condition it reads.

```
            | CantGainLife(Ref) | NoLifeLossFromDamage(Ref) | LifeTotalCantChange(Ref) | PlayersHave{who,keyword} | CantDraw(Ref) | CantSearch(Ref) | VoteAgain(Ref) | SkipExtraTurns(Ref) | RevealTop(Ref) | TriggerAgain{cause: TriggerPattern}
GameRule    = DamageCantBePrevented{source?,combat?} | MustAttack(Ref) | MustAttackPlayers{who,players} | MustBlock(Ref) | AttackTax{who = You, attacked, attackers?, mana?, life?, per = 1} | CantBeCountered(Ref)
            | CantGainLife(Ref) | NoLifeLossFromDamage(Ref) | RadiationGainsLife(Ref) | LifeTotalCantChange(Ref) | PlayersHave{who,keyword} | CantDraw(Ref) | CantSearch(Ref) | VoteAgain(Ref) | SkipExtraTurns(Ref) | RevealTop(Ref) | TriggerAgain{cause: TriggerPattern}
            | MayCast{who = You, what?, from?, free?, flash?, only?, play?, once?}
            | LookAtTopAnyTime(Ref) | NoMaxHandSize(Ref) | SkipDrawStep(Ref) | AdditionalLandPlays{who,count} | CanBeCommander
            | GraveyardCardsUntargetable | CantLoseGame{who, opponents_cant_win?} | CantCastSpells{who, your_turn?} | ToughnessAssignsDamage(Ref) | CountersRemain
Keyword     = Flying | Reach | FirstStrike | ... | Shroud | Protection(Selector) | Ward(Cost) | Equip(Cost) | Enchant(Selector) | EnchantPlayer{opponent} | Kicker(Cost) | Buyback(Cost) | Entwine(Cost) | ...
            | Dredge(n) | Crew(n) | Saddle(n) | Saddled | Station | LevelUp(Cost) | Outlast(Cost) | Mutate(Cost) | Encore(Cost) | Squad(Cost) | Foretell(Cost) | Changeling | Devoid | Myriad | Infect
            | Vanishing(n) | Fading(n) | Backup{count,abilities} | Devour{counters,of} | Casualty(n) | Offspring(Cost) | Demonstrate | Hideaway(n) | Scavenge(Cost) | Transmute(Cost) | Awaken{count,cost} | Gravestorm | Sunburst | UmbraArmor | Cipher | ReadAhead
```

**Values** (evaluated when an effect runs or a pattern is matched; they produce no events)
```
Ref       = You | Opponents | EachPlayer | This | Target(i) | Triggering | Dealer | Attached | AttachedPlayer | Monarch | Defending | Attacked | MostLife | Paid | Bound(name)
          | ControllerOf(Ref) | OwnerOf(Ref) | Each(Selector)
Selector  = [Pred]                       all must hold; the zone is Battlefield unless In(zone) says otherwise (Spell means the stack); [] matches everything there
Pred      = This | Other | Is(Ref) | SharesSubtype(Ref) | SharesName(Ref) | SharesType(Ref) | SharesColor(Ref) | XInCost | Goaded | HasAttached(Selector) | ExiledWith(Ref) | EncodedWith(Ref) | Type | Supertype | Subtype | Color | Colorless | Token | Commander | Spell | Ability(Activated|Triggered) | Tapped | Untapped | Monstrous | HasAnyCounter
          | Crewed(Ref) | Saddled(Ref) | DamagedBy(Ref) | SameCard(Ref) | EnteredThisTurn | AttackedThisTurn | Attacking | Blocking | ControlledBy(Ref) | OwnedBy(Ref) | In(zone) | CastFrom(zone) | HasKeyword | HasCounter(kind) | Named
          | Compare{stat,op,to} | Not(Pred) | AnyOf([Pred])
TargetSpec{ what: AnyTarget | Player | Opponent | Object(Selector) | Either([..]) | PerPlayer{players,what}, bounds = 1..1, divide?: Amount }  // min and max are amounts: "X target creatures" is min X, max X
Amount    = n | X | Kicks | Count(Selector) | Stat{of,stat} | Life(Ref) | CommanderCasts(Ref) | Counters{on,counter} | AnyCounters(Ref) | Sum([Amount]) | Times{by,of} | Half{of,up}
          | Divided | EvenShare(Amount) | TargetCount(i) | Devotion([Color]) | Greatest{of,stat} | Total{of,stat}
          | CardTypes(Selector) | Players(Ref) | LowestLife(Ref) | HighestLife(Ref) | LowestHand(Ref) | LifeLostThisWay | Party(Ref) | Triggering
Condition = Not | All | Any | Compare{left,op,right} | Matches{subject,selector} | Kicked | KickedWith(Cost) | AdditionalCostPaid | CastWith(Flashback|Evoke|Overload|Madness|Suspend|Foretold|Escape|Dash|Bestow|Blitz|Prowl|Awaken|Free) | ManaSpent(ManaType) | YourTurn | Performed
          | Monarch(Ref) | MonarchAtTurnStart(Ref) | CitysBlessing(Ref) | IsDay | IsNight | NeitherDayNorNight | NewlyControlled(Ref) | Step(Step) | BeforeAttackers | InCombat | FirstCombat | Level{of,op,level}
```

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Shared characteristics | `SharesName`, `SharesType` and `SharesColor` hold for an object with the name, a card type or a color of one of the objects a reference names, as that object last existed if it has left; a face-down object has no name. "Target creature and all other creatures with the same name as that creature" is `AnyOf[Is(Target(0)), SharesName(Target(0))]`. `XInCost` is a card with {X} in its mana cost, `Goaded` a goaded creature on the battlefield and `HasAttached(selector)` a permanent with an attached permanent the selector matches ("that's enchanted by an Aura you control"). The selector reader reads "goaded", "with {X} in its mana cost", "that shares a color (card type) with it" and "that's enchanted or equipped" (`compile/read/w6d_shares.rs`); the whole wordings of Maelstrom Pulse, Echoing Truth, Bile Blight, Sever the Bloodline, Legions to Ashes, Cleansing Beam, Martyr's Bond and Vengeful Ancestor are in `render/oracle_only/w6d_wholes.rs` (`mtg-testkit` `w6d_shared_selectors.rs`). |
| Life of players | `Players(who)` counts the players in the game a reference names; `LowestLife(who)` and `HighestLife(who)` are the least and the greatest life total among them. Written as a comparison they say "a player" or "an opponent" has a total (`LowestLife <= n`, `HighestLife >= n`) and "each opponent" or "each player" has one (`HighestLife <= n`, `LowestLife >= n`). With no player named, `LowestLife` is the largest number and `HighestLife` the smallest, so "an opponent" is false and "each opponent" true of no one; a player who has left is not named. |
| Hands and life lost | `LowestHand(who)` is the fewest cards in a hand among the players named ("a player has one or fewer cards in hand": `LowestHand(EachPlayer) <= 1`; "an opponent has no cards in hand": `LowestHand(Opponents) <= 0`), larger than any hand when no player is named. `LifeLostThisWay` is the life the resolving spell or ability has made players lose so far ("You gain life equal to the life lost this way."), 0 outside a resolution. |
| Destroyed this way | `Destroyed(selector)` counts the permanents the resolving spell or ability has destroyed so far that matched the selector as they last existed, tokens included ("for each creature destroyed this way", "the number of creatures you controlled that were destroyed this way": `ControlledBy(You)`); one regenerated or indestructible was not destroyed. The compiler reads it after "for each" and "the number of" (`compile/read/w2c_counts.rs`); Fumigate, Kaya's Wrath, Decree of Pain, Whiptongue Hydra, Bane of Progress and Ob Nixilis, the Ascended are Verified corpus cards (`mtg-testkit` `tests/it/w2c_destroyed.rs`). |
| Exiled this way | `Exiled(selector)` counts the permanents the resolving spell or ability has exiled from the battlefield so far that matched the selector as they last existed, tokens included ("the number of creatures exiled this way", "for each artifact exiled this way"), read and written by the same words as `Destroyed`. A card exiled from another zone is not counted; "creature card exiled this way" stays the count of a bound exile. |
| Different values | `Distinct{of, stat}` counts the different values of the characteristic among the objects the selector matches: "the number of different powers among creatures you control", "for each different mana value among instant and sorcery cards in your graveyard", and coven, "if you control three or more creatures with different powers" (`Distinct >= 3`, a trigger condition). Golden Ratio, Celebrate the Harvest, Eris, Roar of the Storm, Dawnhart Wardens, Stalwart Pathlighter and Redemption Choir are Verified corpus cards (`tests/it/w2c_distinct.rs`). |
| Domain | `Distinct{of, stat: BasicLandTypes}` counts the basic land types among the lands the selector matches, a land of several types adding each: "the number of basic land types among lands you control" after "the number of", "where X is", "equal to" and in an attack tax, "for each basic land type among lands you control" (tokens, pump, cost reductions of spells and abilities), and "if there are three or more basic land types among lands you control" as a condition. Tribal Flames, Territorial Maro, Drag to the Bottom, Leyline Binding, Jodah's Codex, Prismatic Geoscope, Collective Restraint, Spore Burst, Path to the Festival and six more are Verified corpus cards (`tests/it/w7a_domain.rs`). |
| `Target(i)` | The set chosen for the i-th `TargetSpec`: one object for a plain spec, several for "any number". An effect on a set applies to each member. |
| Defaults | `who` is `You`; `source` is `This`; an `Amount` is 1; a `Bounds` is 1..1 and `max: null` is any number. |
| `Search` | The filter picks the cards of the searcher's own zone (`zone`, a library unless it says otherwise); the searcher alone is asked for between `count.min` and `count.max` of them. `count.min` does not bind in a library or a hand: a player may find fewer cards than match, or none. The cards found are bound to `as` and revealed to every seat only when `reveal` is set; a shuffle and the move to where they go are effects of their own. |
| Small enums | `ZoneKind` (Battlefield, Hand, Library, Graveyard, Stack, Exile, Command), `Step`, `Cmp`, `StatKind` (Power, Toughness, ManaValue, Loyalty, Colors: how many colors an object has), `Destination` (Hand, Graveyard, Battlefield{tapped, under?, transformed, counters}, Library{place: Top, Bottom or Shuffled, order?: Chosen or Random}), `CounterKind`, `Timing`, `Limit`, `Produce`. |

## Support records

| State | Meaning | From |
|---|---|---|
| `Verified` | Rules cover the whole text, the text is unchanged, the engine runs every primitive used; or there is no text to cover | `impl_status: Full`, equal fingerprints, `Caps` cover the primitives; or no file and no rules text on any face once reminder text is removed, and no printed gap |
| `Partial` | Rules exist except the listed clauses | `impl_status: Partial`, or `Full` with primitives the engine lacks |
| `Manual` | No rules, or the engine runs none of them | No file and rules text, `impl_status: Manual`, or no primitive covered |
| `Stale` | The oracle text or the face list changed since the rules were written; counts as `Manual` | `Full` or `Partial` with a different fingerprint |
| `Unplayable` | The engine cannot host the card | `impl_status: Unplayable` |

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Declared and computed | The file declares `impl_status` (`Full` is the database enum `full`). `support(Option<&Card>, &PrintedCard, &Caps) -> SupportRecord` computes the state. A file says `Full` only with a scenario test or compiler golden; CI enforces it. |
| Clause | An `Ability::Manual { reason, span }`. `reason` is plain language; `span` is `{start, end}` in Unicode scalar values of the face's imported oracle text, end exclusive. |
| Status and abilities | `Full` has no clause; `Partial` has at least one clause and one modelled ability; `Manual` and `Unplayable` have no abilities. Clauses do not overlap and lie inside the text. |
| `Caps` | The primitives the running engine implements, named by enum and variant: `Effect.DealDamage`, `Keyword.Flying`, `Trigger.ZoneChange`, `Cost.Tap`, `Mod.ModifyPT`, `ReplaceWhen.TokenCreation`, `ReplaceBy.Multiply`, `Ability.Loyalty`; an ability with a `limit`, a zone other than the battlefield, sorcery timing or a `condition` ("activate only if") also uses `Ability.Limit`, `Ability.From`, `Ability.Sorcery` or `Ability.Condition` (a mana ability from another zone also `Ability.ManaAbility.From`), a discard at random also uses `Effect.Discard.Random`, life of a counted amount paid in an effect `Effect.Pay.Counted`, a token that copies an object `Effect.CreateToken.Copy`, and a mana ability the engine activates (a cost of `{T}`, sacrificing the source, mana and fixed life, energy or counters of the source, tapping or sacrificing other permanents, exiling cards from the hand; an optional "Activate only if you control ..." condition; mana added some number of times, mixed or chosen from the commander's colours or from what lands could produce; then effects that ask nothing) that is not the plain one or one mana of a fixed type or any colour (Treasure) `Ability.ManaAbility.Complex`, and one of another shape (a mana cost with an X or without a `{T}`, an effect that asks) `Ability.ManaAbility.Other`. A plain `{T}: Add {X}` (cost exactly `{T}`, one fixed mana, added once) is the one primitive `Ability.ManaAbility`, since the engine runs that shape and not the general ability. The printed gaps have names too (`Card.XCost`, `Card.Faces`, `Card.VariableStats`). `mtg_engine::caps()` supplies it; each primitive task adds its entry there, and `lower` carries every ability the `Caps` cover into the definition. A choice of mana other than any one colour names its kind (`Effect.AddMana.OneOf`, `.OneOfLists`, `.ChosenColor`, `.CommanderIdentity`, `.ProducedBy`). Mana added with a restriction names `Effect.AddMana.Restricted`. Value nodes (`Ref`, `Selector`, `Amount`, `Condition`) are not gated, except those the value evaluator cannot read yet, which count as primitives of their own: `Amount.Devotion`, `Amount.Greatest`, `Amount.Total`, `Amount.CardTypes`, `Condition.Performed`, `Condition.Monarch` and `Condition.CitysBlessing` (an `X` is read as the spell or ability that announced it resolves; an activated ability with an `{X}` in its mana names `Cost.Mana.X`, a loyalty ability that costs `-X` names `Cost.Loyalty.X`, an `{X}` paid in an effect `Effect.Pay.X` (its `paid` effects read that X, whatever the mana cost of the card) and a part of such a payment that counts X `Effect.Pay.X.Parts`, and an `X` read anywhere else (except in the trigger "when you cycle this card" or "when this creature becomes monstrous", which reads the X of that cost when the face prints one), in a trigger, a delayed trigger, an ability a token or a modification carries or a target filter, `Amount.X.Elsewhere`, and a divided amount that is not the amount of `DealDamage` or `PutCounters` (or a sum with it), `Amount.Divided.Elsewhere` and `Amount.EvenShare.Elsewhere`). A player chosen as a permanent enters names `ReplaceBy.Choose.Player` or `ReplaceBy.Choose.Opponent` beside `ReplaceBy.Choose`. |
| Engine gap | `used_primitives(ability)` lists what an ability needs. A face with an uncovered primitive reports the clause "the engine does not run X yet" over the whole face text. A card moves from `Manual` to `Partial` to `Verified` as primitives land, with no file edit. |
| Declared ahead of the engine | A node the engine does not run is declared with a stub: an effect has a file in `resolve/prim/` that refuses by its `Caps` name, a value the evaluator cannot read returns `ResolveError::Unsupported`, a cost part has its name in `price.rs`, a trigger pattern matches no event in `triggers/detect.rs`, a modification or a rule refuses in its `lower` with `ResolveError::Unsupported`, a keyword has a rules value that changes nothing. The support walker names the node, so a card that uses it stays `Partial` until the task that runs it adds the entry to `caps.rs`. |
| No file | `has_rules_text(text)` is true when the text has anything outside parentheses (an unclosed parenthesis counts as text). A card with no file is `Verified` when no face has rules text and no printed gap applies (`Card.XCost`, `Card.Faces`, `Card.VariableStats`, unreadable printed values), `Manual` otherwise. |
| `oracle_fingerprint(text)` | Lines trimmed, whitespace collapsed, blank lines dropped, joined by `\n`; SHA-256; first 16 bytes as 32 hex characters. |
| `implementation_hash(card)` | The same digest of the compact JSON of every face's abilities. |
| `SupportRecord.fingerprint` | SHA-256 (first 16 bytes) over `oracle_id`, state, clauses, implementation hash, stored fingerprints and current fingerprints. It changes with the data, the imported text and the engine's coverage of the card. The deck's `coverage_hash` is built from `(oracle_id, fingerprint)` (`deck-check.md`). |
| `Library::support(&PrintedCard, &Caps)` | The pure per-card lookup the deck check calls. |

## Execution

State is the fold of events; the engine is a pure function from state to `Progress` (events or a decision).

| Topic | Rule |
|---|---|
| Ability objects | `GameObject` has a kind: `Card`, `Token`, `Ability`. An ability lives in `Zone::Stack` with an id from the same counter and holds its source, a `Snapshot` of it, the controller, an `Arc` to its rules (it resolves without its source, CR 113.7a), its `Choices` and, for a trigger, the cause. A spell is the card object. An ability of an object is named by `AbilityRef { object, ability, source }` and read with `abilities_of` and `ability` in `ability_reader` (`engine.md`, Spells and abilities). |
| Identity | Every object has a zone-change counter bumped by the fold (CR 400.7). A stored reference is `(ObjectId, counter)`. |
| Announcement | One procedure (`announce/`, `engine.md`, Announcement) in the order of CR 601.2b to 601.2h: modes, the alternative cost, optional costs, X, targets, division, the legality check, the total cost, the payment. Each is a decision asked while the cast or activation is pending outside state: nothing is logged until the cast is paid, cancel is free. A specification that allows no target is skipped, one that cannot be met rewinds the cast. A mode with an unmeetable specification is not offered, and a modal ability with too few legal modes cannot be cast (a trigger is removed). The final batch carries `Choices { modes, x, targets per spec, division, optional costs }` (a field left out of the wire form while empty). A trigger chooses its targets when it goes on the stack (CR 603.3d); those answers are logged. |
| Activation | An ability whose cost is nothing, or any of `{T}`, sacrificing (`SacrificeSelf`), exiling (`ExileSelf`) or discarding (`DiscardSelf`) the source and mana, is activated from `PriorityOptions.abilities`: `PermanentTapped` (for `{T}`), `ZoneChanged` with `ZoneCause::Sacrificed` or `Exiled` (from the zone the source is in) or `CardDiscarded`, `AbilityActivated`, then `AbilityPutOnStack`, with the source photographed before the costs; an ability works from the zone its `from` names, so the owner of a card in a hand, a graveyard, exile or the command zone is offered its activated abilities there, and one that returns its card (`Move` of `This`) does nothing when the card has changed zones by the time it resolves (CR 400.7); the mana is paid in the same step by the `PayMana` decision (`engine.md`, Spells and abilities), whose sources leave out the source of an ability that taps or sacrifices it. An ability with `timing: Sorcery` is offered only to the active player in a main step with an empty stack. A token sacrificed this way has ceased to exist and its ability still resolves: Clue is `{2}` and sacrifice, Food `{2}`, `{T}` and sacrifice. A mana ability of the shape `mana_shape` reads (`{T}` and/or the sacrifice, mana of one type added some number of times, effects that ask nothing: Treasure, Sol Ring, Gaea's Cradle, the painlands) is not put on the stack: `ActivateMana`, or the tap of a payment, does the cost, adds the mana and runs the effects that follow in one batch. Other costs wait for their primitives in `Caps`. |
| Target recheck | On resolution each target is tested again with the spec that chose it: same object, same zone, selector matches, not protected. If the object had targets and every one is illegal, it does nothing (`ResolutionEnded { Fizzled }`), including parts that do not use the target (CR 608.2b). Otherwise it resolves and ignores the illegal targets, except one that an earlier step of the same resolution moved: it stays named for the steps after it and is read as it was (CR 608.2h). One function, `legal_targets(state, spec, source, controller)`, serves choosing and rechecking. |
| Journal | `GameState.resolving: Option<Resolving { object, position, bindings, snapshots, journal }>`; `position` is a path of indices into the effect tree. `step(&GameState)` reads state and journal, never mutates, and returns `Done(events)` (ending with `StepDone { position, bound, snapshots }`) or `Ask(decision)`. An answer is validated, logged as `ChoiceMade { player, choice }` and appended to the journal by the fold; the same step runs again and consumes it. `StepDone` folds the next position, bindings and snapshots and clears the journal. A step with k decisions runs k+1 times. Random numbers come from the seeded substream, whose counter moves only when events fold. |
| Events | `AbilityTriggered`, `AbilityPutOnStack`, `AbilityActivated`, `ResolutionBegan`, `ChoiceMade`, `StepDone`, `ResolutionEnded { Resolved, Fizzled, Countered }`, and one per primitive without an event: `ZoneChanged { object, from, to, cause }`, `LifeGained`, `LifeLost`, `CountersChanged`, `TokenCreated`, `LibraryLooked`, `CardsFound`, `Scried`, `Surveilled`, `Revealed`, `LibraryBottomed`, `ChosenAtRandom { cards }` (the cards of a public zone that a pick at random took; public, and its fold spends a substream of the generator), `Entered { object, tapped, controller }`, and `Cycled { player, object }`, logged after the `CardDiscarded` of the cost of cycling and public like it. Each has a fold arm and a redaction arm in the protocol's `redact`: a library peek, a search, a scry and a surveil are private to the seat that did them (a scry also puts the cards back, and `LibraryBottomed` moves cards of a library to its bottom; other seats see counts), a reveal is public, `Entered` is public (it follows the `ZoneChanged` that put a card onto the battlefield tapped or under another player's control), and `ZoneChanged`, `ChoiceMade` and `StepDone` reach a seat whole only when every card they name is visible to it. The folds of `LibraryLooked`, `CardsFound`, `Scried`, `Surveilled` and `Revealed` also record the cards in the resolution (`Resolving.shown`: the seat that looked, found or scried, or every seat for a reveal), which the views read until the resolution ends or the card changes zones (`engine.md`, Library effects). |
| Spell resolution | A spell with a `Spell` ability or any ability runs `ResolutionBegan`, its steps and `ResolutionEnded`. For a card `SpellResolved` follows `Resolved` and moves it (a permanent to the battlefield, an instant or sorcery to the graveyard); a fizzled or countered spell goes to the graveyard with `ResolutionEnded`, and an ability ceases to exist with it. A permanent spell has no steps and is only `SpellResolved`. A resolution whose controller has left the game ends `Countered`. |
| Cast during resolution | The cast sub-procedure runs inside a step; its decisions are journal decisions; the spell goes on the stack above the resolving object. |
| Nothing to choose | An attack or block with no legal option is not asked and logs `NothingToDo`. A choice of cards with no more candidates than must be taken (`Discard`, `Sacrifice`) is not asked either: the engine takes them all, or nothing when there are none, and logs only the move. Any other decision with one legal answer and no "may" is answered by the engine unless the seat has Full Control (`arena-flow.md`); a payment that cannot be made is the exception, asked with No the only answer so the pace shows nothing. |

### Interpreter

| Topic | Rule |
|---|---|
| Miracle | `Keyword::Miracle(cost)` adds a trigger that works from a hand: `Nth{event: Drew{who: You, what: This}, nth: 1}` with `CastFromZone{what: This, way: Miracle}`, which asks the owner whether to cast the card for the miracle cost (`CastWay::Miracle`) as the trigger resolves and casts nothing when the card left the hand in the meantime. The trigger names the card to every seat while it waits on the stack; the owner is always taken to reveal it. A miracle cost with {X} (Entreat the Angels: {X}{W}{W}) announces X in place of the X of the printed cost (CR 107.3): the total cost is the miracle mana plus X generic for each {X} of the miracle cost, so the printed {X}{X} still pays twice for X; the question names the cost with its {X}. The compiler reads the keyword line. |
| Madness, suspend and foretell | `Keyword::Madness(cost)` adds a replacement `Discarded{This} MoveTo(Exile)` that works from a hand and a trigger that works from exile: `CastFromZone{what: This, way: Madness, otherwise: [Move This to Graveyard]}`, which casts the card for the madness cost (`CastWay::Madness`). `Keyword::Suspend{time, cost}` adds the upkeep trigger that removes a time counter, the trigger of the last counter (`CastFromZone{what: This, free, way: Suspend}`) and a static ability that gives a creature cast that way haste while its owner controls it; the suspending itself is a special action of the engine (`PriorityAction::Suspend`). `Foretell(cost)` adds no ability: the special action (`PriorityAction::Foretell`, {2} from the hand during the owner's turn, the card lies face down in exile) and the cast for `cost` from a later turn (`CastWay::Foretold`, read as `CastWith(Foretold)`, "if this spell was foretold") are the engine's. |
| Morph, megamorph and disguise | `Morph(cost)`, `Megamorph(cost)` and `Disguise(cost)` add no ability: the engine casts the card face down for {3} (`CastWay::FaceDown`, a 2/2 creature with no name, cost, colour or ability; ward {2} for a disguise), turns it face up as a special action for `cost` in mana (`PriorityAction::TurnFaceUp`; a megamorph also gets a +1/+1 counter) and runs `TurnedFaceUp{what}`, read from "When this creature is turned face up, ...". A cost with a part besides mana, or with an {X}, is not paid yet and the card stays Partial. A manifested or cloaked creature card is turned face up the same way for its mana cost. |
| Dredge | `Dredge(n)` adds one replacement of its owner's draw that works from the graveyard (`ReplaceWhen.Draw`, `ReplaceBy.Dredge(n)`). The replacement step offers it for a draw when the card lies in that graveyard as the batch has left it and the library holds at least `n` cards after the draws before it; several cards and other draw replacements are put in the order the player picks, then a yes or no is asked. A yes replaces the draw by the mill of the top `n` cards (`ZoneCause.Milled`) and the move of the card to the hand, so no card is drawn; a no is asked again for each draw the same event is doubled into. A card milled by a dredge is offered to the later draws of the batch. The compiler reads "Dredge N". |
| Cascade and discover | `effect::cascade()` and `effect::discover(count)` list the three effects of each: `ExileUntil` (cards leave the top of the library face up until a nonland card of lesser value than the source, or of value `count` or less, is found; it binds the card as `found` and every card exiled as `exiled`), `CastFromZone { what: found, free }` (the player may cast it as part of the resolution; `otherwise` is done when they do not or cannot, which for discover moves the card to the hand) and a `Move` of `exiled` to the bottom in a random order, which passes over the cards that were cast since they are other objects. The cascade keyword adds the trigger "when you cast this spell" that works from the stack (`keywords/cascade.rs`); the compiler reads "Discover N." and the three sentences of an exile until a card is found, its free cast and the move of the cards exiled. |
| Impulse draw | `effect::impulse(count, duration)` lists the two effects: `ExileTop` (the top cards of the library leave it face up; it binds each as the object it becomes in exile, as `impulse`) and `Rule { MayCast { from: Exile, only: Bound(impulse), play: true }, duration }` (the player may cast the cards, or play a land among them, until the duration ends). `only` names the objects when the rule begins, so a card that leaves exile is a new object the permission no longer covers, and a rule that names no card begins nothing. `play` adds the land play (CR 305.1): the engine offers a land of the permission with the timing and the land plays of any land. The compiler reads "Exile the top card of your library." (also "the top two cards") and "You may play that card this turn.", "Until end of turn, you may play that card." or "Until the end of your next turn, you may play those cards." (also "them"), the pronoun following the count. |
| Cast of a target | "You may cast target instant card from your graveyard [without paying its mana cost]." is `CastFromZone{what: Target(0), free?}` (`compile/read/c11b_cast_target.rs`); a following "If that spell would be put into your (a, their) graveyard, exile it instead." gives the cast `as: cast` (the spell bound as it is on the stack, nothing bound when it is not cast) and adds `Shield{ZoneChange{what: [In(Stack), Is(Bound(cast))], from: Stack, to: Graveyard}, MoveTo(Exile), Forever}`. "from that player's graveyard" in a trigger is the graveyard of the player of the event (`OwnedBy(Triggering)`). |
| Once each turn | "Once during each of your turns, you may cast a creature spell from your graveyard." (also "a Zombie creature spell", "an artifact spell", "an instant or sorcery spell") is a static `Rule{MayCast{what: [kinds], from: Graveyard, once: true}}` (`compile/read/c11b_once.rs`): one card through it in each of its controller's turns, none on another player's turn. |
| Primitives | `resolve/prim/<effect>.rs` holds one file per `Effect` variant. Draw, Discard, Mill, Scry, Surveil, Proliferate, Explore, Connive, Amass, Monstrosity, Adapt, Bolster, LookAtTop, Reveal, Search, Choose, ChooseAtRandom, Shuffle, Move, Destroy, Exile, ExileUntil, ExileTop, CastFromZone, Sacrifice, Counter, DealDamage, GainLife, LoseLife, AddMana, PutCounters, RemoveCounters, MoveCounters, Tap, Untap, WinGame, CreateToken, Continuous (with `Mod.ModifyPT` and `Mod.AddKeyword`), DelayedTrigger, If, May, Pay and Modal run and are in `Caps` (a `Move` onto the battlefield is the primitive `Effect.Move.Battlefield`, also in `Caps`; a `CreateToken` of a copy is the separate primitive `Effect.CreateToken.Copy`, a `Discard` with `random` is `Effect.Discard.Random` and a `Move` to the top of a library in random order `Effect.Move.Random.Top`, none of the three in `Caps`); every other file is a stub that returns `ResolveError::Unsupported("Effect.<Name>")` and is not in `Caps`, so no game offers a card that uses it. A task replaces its stub, adds its name to `RUNS` in `caps.rs` and to the expected list of `the_capabilities_name_exactly_the_effects_that_run` in `resolve/prim/mod.rs` (the fast check skips that test: run `cargo test -p mtg-engine --lib`). |
| Destroy, exile, counter | Each names its objects with a `Ref` (a target, `Each(selector)` for "all", a bound name) and acts on all of them together, in the order the reference lists them, in one step. A name of an object that has changed zones since (CR 400.7) or repeats is passed over. `Destroy` moves a permanent that is still on the battlefield and not indestructible (`keywords::is_indestructible`) to its owner's graveyard. `Exile` moves an object from whichever zone it is in to exile, whatever its keywords; abilities, the resolving spell and objects already in exile stay, and its `as` name holds the objects exiled as the objects they become in exile, for the `Move` that returns them (a `Move` onto the battlefield with `transformed` puts a transforming double-faced card there with its back face up and leaves a card of any other layout where it is). `Counter` ends a spell or ability on the stack, other than the resolving one, as `Countered`: the spell goes to its owner's graveyard, an ability ceases to exist, nothing of either resolves. The moves carry `ZoneCause::Destroyed` and `Exiled`; a counter is `ResolutionEnded { Countered }`, which triggers read as a move from the stack with `ZoneCause::Countered`. Each step carries a snapshot of every object it moved. A destruction that `regenerate` allows carries `ZoneCause::Destroyed`, which a regeneration shield replaces; `regenerate: false` ("can't be regenerated") carries `ZoneCause::Buried`. |
| Regenerate | `Effect::Regenerate` gives each permanent it names that is on the battlefield a regeneration shield (CR 701.19) until end of turn: the next time the permanent would be destroyed it is tapped, loses all its damage and the deathtouch mark and leaves combat instead (`Event::Regenerated`), and the shield is used up. A destroy effect, lethal damage and deathtouch destroy; zero toughness, a sacrifice, a burial, exile and a state-based move do not. The shield replaces the destruction before any effect that replaces the move applies, and two shields take two destructions. |
| Counters | `PutCounters`, `RemoveCounters`, `MoveCounters` and `MultiplyCounters` (doubling and tripling the counters a holder has, of one kind or each) act on the permanents on the battlefield (still the objects named, each once) and the players in the game that their references name, all together in one step. `PutCounters` gives each the count (nothing for a count of 0 or less); `RemoveCounters` takes up to the count of the kind, never more than a holder has; `MoveCounters` takes up to the count from the first permanent `from` names and puts as many on the first permanent `to` names, and moves nothing when either is missing, when they are one permanent or when the first holds none (CR 122.5). Counters belong to the object that holds them: a permanent that changes zones holds none when it comes back (CR 400.7). `Amount::Counters { on, counter }` adds up the counters of the kind on everything `on` names, read live while an object is the one named and from its snapshot after it has left, and `Pred::HasCounter(kind)` selects the objects that hold at least one (a condition over counters is `Compare` of that amount). A cost that removes counters from the source waits for the costs of (g). |
| Proliferate | Each player `who` names who is in the game chooses any number of the permanents on the battlefield that hold a counter (`ChooseCards`, asked whenever one holds a counter) and answers a `YesNo` for each player in the game who holds a counter; every player decides before any counter moves. It logs `Proliferated { player }` (public, also when nothing was chosen or nothing holds a counter) and one `CountersChanged` of +1 for each kind each chosen permanent or player holds, which a replacement effect sees as counters put. |
| Explore | Each creature `what` names that is on the battlefield explores in turn (one that has left, with no snapshot of it in the ability, does not): its controller reveals the top card of their library (`Revealed`, public) after an `Explored { object, player }`. A land goes to the hand (`ZoneChanged` from the library, no question). Any other card, or no card because the library is empty, gives the creature one +1/+1 counter as a proposed `CountersChanged` that a replacement effect sees, and a revealed card is offered to the graveyard with `ChooseCards` over that card alone (none or one, asked whenever a nonland card is revealed). A card that stays on top is the one the next explore of that library reveals; every answer is in before anything happens. |
| Amass | The controller puts N +1/+1 counters on an Army creature they control (CR 701.47): with several Armies they choose one with `ChooseCards`, and the Army becomes a creature of the subtype too (a `Forever` `AddSubtype` effect). With none the step creates the 0/0 black Army token of the subtype, binds it and ends with `Next::Again`, so the effect runs again once the token is on the battlefield and the counters go through the replacement effects (Hardened Scales, Doubling Season); a token that is not there on the rerun ends it. `Amassed { object, player }` follows the counters and is the trigger event of `Amassed{who}`. The compiler reads "amass Orcs 2" (the plural subtype, a number or X). |
| Monstrosity, Adapt, Bolster | `Monstrosity` acts on the source when it is a permanent on the battlefield that is not monstrous: it proposes N +1/+1 counters as a `CountersChanged` that a replacement effect sees and then logs `Monstrous { object, player }`, which sets the designation (`PermanentState::monstrous`, cleared with the permanent when it leaves; `Pred::Monstrous`, read by `Condition::Matches`, is "as long as ~ is monstrous"). `Adapt` does the same when the source holds no +1/+1 counter and logs `Adapted`. `Bolster` puts the counters on the creature of the least toughness (as the layers leave it) among the creatures the controller controls, who chooses among ties with `ChooseCards`, and logs `Bolstered { object, player }`; with no creature nothing happens. A count of 0 or less proposes no counters and the log entry still follows. `TriggerPattern::BecomesMonstrous`, `Adapted` and `Bolstered` read them. |
| Class levels (CR 716) | `PermanentState::level` is the level designation of a permanent (a Class that has gained no level reads as level 1; the new object after a zone change starts again). `Effect::BecomeLevel { level }` acts on the source when it is a permanent below that level and logs `ClassLevelGained { object, player, level }` (public), which sets the designation; `Condition::Level { of, op, level }` compares it and `TriggerPattern::BecomesLevel { what, level }` reads the event. "{cost}: Level N" is an `Activated` ability at sorcery timing, conditioned on `Level` equal to N-1, that becomes level N; the abilities of a level are conditioned on `Level` at least N, so the lower levels keep working. `ObjectView.permanent.level` shows the level of a Class. |
| Fabricate | `Keyword::Fabricate(n)` adds an enters trigger of the source whose effect is `Fabricate { count }`: the controller of a source that is on the battlefield is asked (`YesNo`) whether to put the counters on it, proposed as a `CountersChanged` that a replacement effect sees; otherwise, and always for a source that has left the battlefield (CR 702.123b), the effect creates that many 1/1 colorless Servo artifact creature tokens through `CreateToken`, so Doubling Season and Parallel Lives apply. |
| Repeat for each | `RepeatEach { over, as, effects }` does `effects` once for each player or object `over` names, in the order the reference lists them, with the name `as` bound to the one the pass is for ("for each player ... that player": `over` is `EachPlayer`, `Opponents`, `Each(selector)` or a bound list). The list is fixed when the repeat begins, so an object that has left by its pass is passed over by the effects that name it, and a name the body binds is bound anew in each pass. The passes are positions of the effect tree (`engine.md`, Spells and abilities). `Effect.RepeatEach` is in `Caps`. |
| Dice and coins | `RollDice { who, sides, plus, results }` rolls a die of `sides` faces and does the first row of `results` (`from`, `to` or none for "15+", `effects`) that holds the number rolled plus `plus` ("roll a d20 and add the number of cards in your hand"); no row that holds it is no effect. `FlipCoin { who, win, lose, until }` does `win` or `lose`, or nothing with both empty ("Flip a coin."); `until` flips again after each won flip, `win` done after each, and stops at the first loss ("flip a coin until you lose a flip"); the call carries no choice, so a flip is won with even odds. `TriggerPattern::CoinFlipWon { who }` is "whenever you win a coin flip" (`Trigger.CoinFlipWon`). Both take their number from the game's seeded random stream and log it (`DieRolled`, `CoinFlipped`, public events), so a replay reads the log and draws nothing; `who` names one player, and "for each opponent" is a `RepeatEach`. `Effect.RollDice` and `Effect.FlipCoin` are in `Caps`. `dice: 2, choose: true` ("roll two d4 and choose one result") rolls two dice, asks the roller between two different numbers (`ChooseOption`) and logs the chosen die last; it has no table. `Amount::DieResult` reads the dice the resolution logged (`Resolving.rolled`): `Only` ("the result") and `Chosen` ("that result") the last, `Other` ("the other result") the one before; the compiler reads them where a count stands, and the sentence of the other result may open with "Then". `TriggerPattern::DiceRolled { who, highest }` is "whenever you roll one or more dice", once for the dice a player rolls in one step, or with `highest` "a die's highest natural result", once for each die that rolls its faces (`Trigger.DiceRolled`, `Amount.DieResult`). |
| Vote | `Vote { options, secret, as }` has each player vote (CR 701.38), the controller first and the others in turn order. `Named(["time", "knowledge"])` binds each name to the players who voted for it, once for each vote, so `Players(Bound("time"))` is the number of votes ("if time gets more votes" is a `Compare` of two of them, "for each time vote" a `RepeatEach` over the name); `Of(ref)` votes for the players or objects the reference names and binds `as` to those with the most votes, every one of them when tied ("exile each permanent with the most votes or tied for most votes"). `secret` ("secretly votes for ... then those votes are revealed") applies to named choices only. `Effect.Vote` is in `Caps`. "For each sprout vote" and "the number of truth votes" are the amount `Players(Bound("sprout"))`, read only for a name of a vote read before (`vote::w2d_tally`). |
| VillainousChoice | `VillainousChoice { who, first, second, they }` is "S faces a villainous choice — A, or B" (CR 701.56): the one player `who` names chooses `first` or `second`. "That player" faces it as `Triggering`, "target opponent" as a target, "each opponent" in a `RepeatEach` over `Opponents` bound as `opponent`; "that player" and "they" in the options name `who`, and `they` keeps which word the card prints (`compile/read/w2d_villainous.rs`, tests in `tests/w2d_villainous.rs`). `Effect.VillainousChoice` is in `Caps`. |
| Planechase | `Planechase { act }` is "planeswalk" (`Planeswalk`) or "chaos ensues" (`ChaosEnsues`, CR 901). A game without a planar deck, which every game the engine plays is, does nothing for it, so the Will of the Planeswalkers vote of the Path cards is held and changes nothing. `Effect.Planechase` is in `Caps`. |
| Learn | `Learn { who }` logs `Learned { player }`, then the player may discard a card (`ChooseCards`, none or one, not asked with an empty hand) and draws a card if they do; a Lesson from outside the game is not an option in Commander. |
| Crewed | `Crewed { saddle }` ends the crew and saddle abilities (`keywords/{crew,saddle}.rs` append it after the type or designation change): when the source is on the battlefield it logs one public `Crewed { object, player, creatures, saddle }` whose `creatures` are the objects `Ref::Paid` names, the creatures tapped to pay. The fold counts it for the object (`Tally::Crewed` or `Saddled`) and records the creatures of `creatures` that are on the battlefield for it in `Tallies::crews` (cleared with the turn, hashed while not empty), which `Pred::Crewed(Ref)` and `Pred::Saddled(Ref)` read: "creature that crewed (saddled) it this turn" is the creatures tapped for the permanents the reference names, and a creature that changed zones since is another object. `BecomesCrewed` and `BecomesSaddled` trigger on it with the controller and the permanent as subjects, and `Nth` counts the first time each turn apart for the Vehicle and the Mount. |
| The city's blessing, Ascend | `GameState::has_citys_blessing` is a per-player designation that nothing removes (`CitysBlessingGained`, public, hashed). `Keyword::Ascend` has no hook: `citys_blessing::sync` gives it in the state-based actions to a player who controls a permanent with ascend and ten or more permanents, and `resolve::step` gives it to the controller of an instant or sorcery with ascend as the spell begins to resolve, before its effects. `Condition::CitysBlessing(who)` reads it; the compiler reads "you have the city's blessing" wherever it reads "you're the monarch". |
| Dungeons, venture | A dungeon is a file under `crates/mtg-cards/dungeons/` (`w11d_dungeon.rs`): its name, `undercity` for Undercity, and its rooms in order, each a `name`, the printed `text`, `leads_to` (the names of the rooms one arrow down; none for the last room) and an optional written `ability` (an `Ability::Spell`). A room without one is read by the card compiler as a spell's text; the check refuses a dungeon with no room, a doubled name, an arrow up or to no room, an unreached room or a last room with an arrow, and validates each room ability; a room the compiler does not read keeps its dungeon from being ready. `Effect::Venture{who,undercity}` is "venture into the dungeon", `TriggerPattern::Ventured{who}` "whenever you venture into the dungeon" (with `room`, the room ability of the dungeon that is the source), `TriggerPattern::CompletedDungeon{who}` "whenever you complete a dungeon" and `Condition::CompletedDungeon(who)` "if you've completed a dungeon". The unread rooms of Tomb of Annihilation and Undercity and the Runestone Caverns of Dungeon of the Mad Mage are written out (`RepeatEach` of a `Pay` for "each player loses 2 life unless they ...", a `Choose` and a `Move` with counters for Throne of the Dead Three); Mad Wizard's Lair is not, so Dungeon of the Mad Mage is not offered. |
| The initiative | `Effect::TakeInitiative{who}` is "you take the initiative" (CR 725), `TriggerPattern::TakesInitiative{who}` the event of taking it and `Condition::Initiative(who)` "if you have the initiative"; the engine ventures into Undercity as it is taken and in its holder's upkeep (`engine.md`). |
| Day and night | `GameState::day_night` is the designation (`DayNight`, in `mtg-cards`, shown by the turn view). `Effect::BecomeDayNight { to }` sets it; `Condition::IsDay`, `IsNight` and `NeitherDayNorNight` read it; `TriggerPattern::DayNightChanges { to }` triggers on a change between day and night, not on the first day; `ReplaceBy::DayIfNeither` on an `Enters` of the card starts the day as it enters. The compiler reads "It becomes night.", "If it's night, it becomes day. Otherwise, it becomes night." (an `If` with `otherwise`), "it's day", "it's night" and "it's neither day nor night" wherever it reads "you're the monarch", "Whenever day becomes night or night becomes day" (also "night becomes day", "day becomes night") and "If it's neither day nor night, it becomes day as ~ enters." (`compile/read/day_night.rs`). A cost reduction "if it's night" is the `condition` of a `CostModifier`. |
| Connive | Each creature `what` names that is on the battlefield (or that the ability remembers) connives in turn: `Connived { object, player }`, then its controller draws a card and discards a card, and a creature still on the battlefield gets one +1/+1 counter (a proposed `CountersChanged`) when the card discarded is not a land. The discard is asked with `ChooseCards` (one card) whenever the hand and the card drawn hold more than one, so the effect is one step: the card drawn is predicted as the top card of the library and can be discarded, unless a replacement effect changes the draw (a commander drawn), when the choice is among the cards already in the hand. A second creature of the same player draws the next card. |
| Loyalty abilities | `Ability::Loyalty` is activated from `PriorityOptions.abilities` like any ability, with a loyalty cost instead of a `Cost`: the active player in a main step with an empty stack, once for each planeswalker each turn, and for a minus cost only while the planeswalker holds that many loyalty counters (CR 606). Paying it is a `CountersChanged` of the loyalty kind before `AbilityActivated`, so the ability goes on the stack with its source as it stands once the cost is paid and resolves without it. A `-X` cost asks X up to the loyalty counters and removes that many. `Amount::Stat` and `Pred::Compare` read `Stat::Loyalty`: the loyalty counters of a planeswalker on the battlefield (as they were from a snapshot after it has left), the printed number in any other zone, and nothing for what is not a planeswalker. A planeswalker enters with its printed loyalty as a proposed `CountersChanged`, and takes damage as lost loyalty (`engine.md`, Spells and abilities). |
| Steps | One effect is one step. `If`, `May`, `Pay` and `Modal` are entered by position: `[2, 1, 0]` is the first effect of the second list of the third effect (`resolve/tree.rs`). The lists of a `Modal` are the modes chosen, in printed order, a mode chosen twice twice. |
| If | The condition is read in the state the earlier steps left: the resolution enters `then` when it holds and `otherwise` when it does not, and a list with nothing in it goes on to the next effect. A condition the evaluator cannot read is refused by name; its cards are outside `Caps` (see Values). |
| Tap, untap | Each names its permanents with a `Ref` and acts on all of them together, in the order the reference lists them. A permanent already in that state, an object that is not a permanent or has changed zones since (CR 400.7), a player and a repeated name are passed over. The events are `PermanentTapped` and `PermanentUntapped`. |
| Mill | Each player the effect names who is in the game puts the top cards of their library into their graveyard, the top card first, in one step: a `ZoneChanged` with `ZoneCause::Milled` for each. A short library gives what it has, an empty one nothing, and milling is not drawing. |
| Attach | Each permanent `what` names that is still the object named moves onto the first permanent `to` names when it may be attached there (an Aura what its `Enchant` keywords allow, an Equipment a creature, a Fortification a land; CR 701.3): an `Attached` for each, in one step, with a new timestamp (CR 613.7a). One that may not stays (CR 701.3b), one already there is not attached again (CR 701.3c), and a `to` that names nothing attaches nothing. An Aura spell needs no `Attach`: its target comes from `Enchant` and it enters attached to it (CR 303.4a, 303.4f). |
| Unattach | Each permanent `what` names that is still the object named and is attached to something becomes unattached (`Unattached`, CR 701.3d), all in one step; one attached to nothing stays as it is. |
| WinGame | Every opponent of a winner who is in the game loses at once (`PlayerLost`, in turn order from the active player), and the end-of-game check after the resolution names the one player left (CR 104.2a, 104.2b). The rest of the resolution is done first. |
| Values | `Scope` in `resolve/value.rs` reads `Ref`, `Selector`, `Amount` and `Condition` from the state, the bindings, the choices and the snapshots, and changes nothing. `DealDamage` and `PutCounters` read the amount once for each target they name (`Scope::amounts_for`, `resolve/share.rs`): `Divided` is that target's share of `Choices.division`, `EvenShare` the amount rounded down among the targets still legal, `Times{by,of}` the factor times `of`, `Half{of,up}` half of `of` rounded up or down (never below zero). `X` is the value announced for the spell or ability, and 0 where none was chosen (CR 107.3); the spell's enters trigger (its intervening "if" included) and its "enters with X counters" replacement read the X it was cast with (CR 107.3m), a trigger on a spell being cast ("when you cast this spell", "whenever you cast a spell") the X of that spell, a delayed trigger the X of what set it up, and a target filter or selector the X announced (`Pred::Compare` with `to: X`). It refuses by name what it cannot read: a divided amount with no target to give it to (`ResolveError::Unsupported`). The support walker names each of them as a primitive that `Caps` lacks (`Amount.X.Elsewhere` for an X read anywhere but those places, `Amount.Divided.Elsewhere`, `Amount.EvenShare.Elsewhere`), wherever the value stands, so no card that would meet a refusal in a game is `Verified`. |
| Replacement | Every batch of events passes through `replace::process(state, proposed, journal)` in `Engine::commit` before the fold; the events of a resolution step are in the batch with their `StepDone`. |
| Damage and drawing | `DealDamage` is `DamageDealt` for each recipient that is still a player in the game, or a creature, planeswalker or battle on the battlefield (CR 120.1); other recipients are passed over, and damage of 0 is no event. `Draw` uses the turn code's `draw`, so an empty library is logged as before. |

### Decisions the effects need

`Decision.player` names who answers, so a decision may go to any seat. Every effect decision carries `source` (the object that asks). Decisions for a seat that is not the active player have a response clock (`server.md`). `ChooseTargets` and `OrderTriggers` are asked and have their prompts in the web; `ChooseCards` is asked by `Discard`, `Sacrifice`, `Proliferate` (the permanents that hold counters), `Explore` (the revealed card), `Connive` (the card to discard), `Choose` over objects, `Scry`, `Search`, `Move` of an Aura onto the battlefield (what it enchants) and the legend rule of the state-based actions (the permanent to keep) (its question carries `text`, "Discard a card." or "Search your library for a basic land card."; a search offers the matching cards, between 0 and the most that may be found), `OrderCards` by `Scry` and `Move` (the controller puts cards in one library in an order, top first), `YesNo` by `Proliferate` (one question for each player who holds counters) and by `May` ("Draw a card?"; the players `who` names are asked in turn order until one says yes) and by `Pay` ("Pay {2}?"; the same order until one pays, those who cannot pay are asked too, with No the only answer), `PayMana` by `Pay` after a yes (the payment cannot be backed out of), `ChooseOption` by `Choose` over players ("Choose an opponent."; a player with one option is not asked) and `ChooseMode` at announcement by `Modal`; `YesNo`, `ChooseOption`, `ChooseMode`, `ChooseCards` and `OrderCards` have their prompts and response clocks; `ChooseNumber` (X), `ChooseCosts` (optional costs) and `DivideAmount` (a target specification with `divide`) are asked at announcement, answered and given a response clock like the others (their options carry `can_cancel`, and `text` in the words of a card for the number and the division; the answer can be `Cancel`), and `ChooseNumber` is the answer to an effect that asks for a number (`Choice::Number` in the journal); no card has an `{X}` for the announcement until (g2), and the optional costs are those of `Kicker` and `Multikicker`: the keyword makes `ChooseCosts` list a kicker the player can pay for and `ChooseNumber` ask the times a multikicker is paid (`engine.md`, Announcement); `Condition::Kicked` and `Amount::Kicks` read what was paid, in the spell, in the permanent it becomes and in its triggered abilities. The four kinds without a prompt (`ChooseNumber`, `DivideAmount`, `ChooseCosts`, `ChooseReplacement`) have a placeholder in the web, which M5 replaces. The questions of `YesNo`, `ChooseOption`, `ChooseCards` and `OrderCards` carry `text` in the words of a card, and `ChooseMode` says whether the cast can be backed out of (`can_cancel`).

| Decision | Who answers | When | Corpus cards that need it |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ChooseTargets` | controller | announcement; trigger put on stack | Lightning Bolt, Doom Blade, Swords, Giant Growth, Counterspell, Burst Lightning, Eternal Witness, Izzet Charm, Jace, Pacifism (Enchant), Bonesplitter (equip), Archon of Cruelty, Fireball, Fire // Ice |
| `ChooseMode` | controller | announcement | Izzet Charm |
| `ChooseNumber` | controller | announcement (X, multikicker); resolution ("any number") | Fireball, Wolfbriar Elemental |
| `DivideAmount` | controller | announcement | Fire // Ice |
| `ChooseCards` | any seat | resolution; state-based actions (the legend rule); cost payment | Rampant Growth, Cultivate, Flooded Strand, Brainstorm, Jace 0, Faithless Looting, Archon (sacrifice and discard, by the target player), Sneak Attack |
| `OrderCards` | any seat | resolution | Opt, Brainstorm, Jace 0, cascade (bottom) |
| `YesNo` | any seat | resolution ("may", pay or not) | Eternal Witness, Jace +2, Rhystic Study (both players), Delver |
| `ChooseOption` | any seat | resolution, replacement step | Hero of Bladehold in multiplayer (which defender), Treasure (colour), Mending Hands with the combat damage of two attackers (which damage the shield stops first) |
| `ChooseCosts` | controller | announcement (optional costs; alternative costs are separate `castable` entries) | Burst Lightning, Gatekeeper of Malakir, Mulldrifter, Faithless Looting |
| `OrderTriggers` | controller | two or more triggers of one controller that are not all of one source and ability | a card with two triggers on one event |
| `ChooseReplacement` | affected player | two or more applicable replacement effects | Anointed Procession with a second token effect |
| `PayMana` | any seat | announcement of a spell or an ability with a mana cost; resolution after a yes to "Pay {N}?" | all spells; Rhystic Study, Izzet Charm |

### Snapshots (last known information)

`Snapshot { id, counter, controller, owner, zone, characteristics, keywords, counters, attachments }` is built by `snapshot(state, id)` and kept in three places only. `counter` names the object it describes, `characteristics` has every continuous effect applied, `keywords` are those it had after layer 6 (none that an effect removed), and `counters` and `attachments` are the permanent's own (empty off the battlefield).

| Place | Content |
|---|---|
| Stack ability | Its source and, for a trigger, the objects of its cause, taken at detection (CR 603.10a: look-back uses the state before the event). |
| Resolution | Every object a step moves off the battlefield or stack, carried by `StepDone`, so "its power" and "its controller" in Swords to Plowshares read the creature as it was when exiled (CR 608.2h). `Target(i)` still names a target that an earlier step moved, so those later steps find it. |
| `Choices` | The value of X, which outlives the spell. |

`Amount::Stat`, `Amount::Counters`, `ControllerOf`, `Matches`, `HasKeyword` and `HasCounter` read the live object when its counter matches and the snapshot otherwise; the damage a source that has left deals reads the keywords and the controller of its snapshot.

## Triggers, replacement and layers

| Topic | Rule |
|---|---|
| Detection | After a batch is folded, `triggers::detect(pre, events, post)` returns `Triggered { source, ability, controller, cause, snapshot, delayed }`, logged as `AbilityTriggered`. A move out of the battlefield (dies, leaves, sacrificed) is judged against `pre`: the abilities that worked then and the objects as they were (CR 603.10a); every other event against `post`. An ability works from the zone its `from` names (the battlefield unless it says otherwise): a triggered ability of a card in a graveyard, a hand, exile, the command zone, a library or the stack is tried against the same events as a permanent's, in id order after the permanents, and a trigger that triggers an additional time (`TriggerAgain`) is a permanent's alone. |
| State triggers | `TriggerPattern::State { condition }` is the whole pattern of a triggered ability (CR 603.8), read by `Scope::holds` like any condition. It is looked for where a player would receive priority, after state-based actions and before triggers go on the stack, and is logged as `AbilityTriggered` with a cause that names nothing. It triggers when its source is in the zone its `from` names, the condition holds and no instance of that ability of that object waits in the queue or is on the stack; once that instance has resolved, been countered or been removed it triggers again if the state still holds, and a source that changed zones is a new object that triggers on its own (CR 400.7). `limit` and `intervening_if` apply as for any trigger. |
