| Pattern events | `ZoneChange`: `LandPlayed`, `SpellResolved`, `CreatureDied`, `CardDiscarded`, `CardDrawn`, `ZoneChanged` and the end of a spell that did not resolve. `Attacks`: each attacker of `AttackersDeclared`. `SpellCast`: `SpellCast`, with the caster and the spell as subjects. `Sacrificed`: `ZoneChanged` with cause sacrifice, with the permanent and who sacrificed it. `Milled`: `ZoneChanged` with cause `Milled` from a library to a graveyard (never a surveil or another move), with the card as the one subject, so a batch of mills over several libraries triggers once; `by` is the owner of the library. `StepBegins`: `StepBegan`, with the active player. `BecomesTarget`: each object among the targets of `SpellCast` and `AbilityPutOnStack`, with the object, what targets it and the controller of that. `DealsDamage`: each `DamageDealt`, `combat` true in the damage steps with nothing resolving, with the recipient as the one subject. The source of the damage is `Cause.source`, which `Ref::Dealer` names ("Whenever a Vampire you control deals combat damage to a player, put a +1/+1 counter on it"); a batch of damage and every other event have none. `LifeGained`: `LifeGained`. `LifeLost`: `LifeLost`, `LifePaid` and each `DamageDealt` to a player (CR 120.3a), with the player. `Drew`: `CardDrawn`, with the player. `Discarded`: `CardDiscarded` and a move from a hand with cause discard, with the player and the card. `Cycled`: `Cycled`, with the player and the card; the discard that pays for cycling is also a `Discarded`. `BecomesCrewed`, `BecomesSaddled`: `Crewed` with `saddle` false or true, with the controller and the permanent. `Tapped`, `Untapped`: `PermanentTapped`, `PermanentUntapped`. `TappedForMana`: the `ManaAdded` that follows the `PermanentTapped` of its source in a batch, once for the tap, with the player as the one subject. `CountersPut`, `CountersRemoved`: `CountersChanged` of a permanent with a positive or negative delta. `Chapter`: a `CountersChanged` of lore counters on the source that takes it from fewer than the number to the number or more (one trigger for each chapter it passes). `Blocks`: each pair of `BlockersDeclared`, with the blocker and the attacker; `BecomesBlocked`: each attacker blocked, once. `GroupAttacks`: `AttackersDeclared`, once, with the attackers that match. `Attached`: `Attached`, with the attachment and what it is attached to. `BecomesMonarch`: `MonarchChanged`, with the new monarch as the one subject (`Triggering` is that player). `Proliferated`: `Proliferated`, with the player as the one subject. `SpellCopied`: `SpellCopied`, with the controller of the copy and the copy as subjects; a copy is never a `SpellCast`. `Unblocked`: each attacker of the player who declared blockers that no creature blocks (CR 509.1h), once that player has declared. `Explored`, `Connived`: `Explored`, `Connived`, with the controller and the creature as subjects. `AttacksPlayer` also names the monarch (`at: Monarch`). |
| Attached subject | An Equipment or an Aura names the permanent it is attached to by `Is(Attached)` in the selector of a pattern ("whenever equipped creature dies, attacks, becomes tapped, deals combat damage to a player"). It is judged against the same state as the pattern, so a host that leaves the battlefield is still the attached creature as it was before the batch. |
| `Batch` | `Batch(pattern)` is the whole pattern of a triggered ability: the events of one `detect` call that the inner pattern matches (one damage step, one effect) trigger it once, once for each player the events are about ("one or more creatures you control deal combat damage to a player" hurts each player in a batch of its own), after the single events of the batch. The inner pattern is one event that can happen to several things at once (damage, a zone change, taps, counters); An `AnyOf` of zone changes that each name a zone left other than the battlefield is one batch too ("cards are put into exile from your library and/or your graveyard": `c6_gap_batch_of_moves`), since the engine judges those events together against the state after them. `Triggering` names the players and objects of all its events. `GroupAttacks` is the batch of attackers. |
| `Nth` | `Nth { event, nth }` triggers on the nth card drawn (`Drew`), spell cast (`SpellCast`), life gain (`LifeGained`), life loss (`LifeLost`: an effect, a payment and damage that causes it count once each), card cycled (`Cycled`, any card or `Other`), surveil (`Surveilled`) or token created under the player's control (`ZoneChange` of `[Token, ControlledBy(You)]` from no zone) of a player in a turn. The fold counts them in `GameState.tallies` by player and kind, clears the counts when a turn begins and hashes them with the state; `detect` finds the place of each event in its batch from the count after it. |
| `Nth` of an object | `Nth` also counts for the object: its `Attacks` ("attacks for the first time each turn"), its `BecomesCrewed` and `BecomesSaddled` ("becomes crewed for the first time each turn", counted apart for the Vehicle and for the Mount), its `BecomesTarget` with `by` empty (by anything) or `[ControlledBy(You)]` (by what its controller controls: Seedglaive Mentor's valiant), `DealsDamage { source: [], to: Some(object), combat: None }` (damage to the object from any source, "is dealt damage for the first time each turn") and `CountersPut { counter: None }` on the object ("one or more counters are put on it for the first time each turn": one count for each counter kind put, so two kinds put together are two times). The fold counts them in `GameState.object_tallies` by object (an `ObjectRef`, so a permanent that changed zones starts again), the player whose spell or ability targets it and kind, clears them when a turn begins and hashes them when not empty. An object named twice by one spell becomes a target once; damage of 0 is not counted. A target counted for what an opponent controls, for a kind of spell, damage to a player and damage from one source are refused by validation, and so is a count of cycling the card itself, of every token, of one kind of counter or of any other event. |
| `Surveilled`, `ZoneChangeExcept` | `Surveilled { who }` triggers on a surveil of the players it names (`Happened::Surveilled` from `Event::Surveilled`, even of no cards; it is never in a batch). `ZoneChangeExcept { what, except, to? }` is `ZoneChange` with the zone the object must not come from in place of the zone it comes from: "is put into a graveyard from anywhere other than the battlefield" is `except: Battlefield, to: Graveyard`. A token that is created comes from no zone and never matches it. |
| `Amount::Triggering` | The amount of the event that triggered the ability, in `Cause.amount`: the damage dealt, the life gained or lost, the counters put or removed, the attackers of a `GroupAttacks`, the events of a `Batch` and the damage a batch of damage dealt in all ("that damage": Broodcaller Scourge puts a permanent card with at most that mana value from the hand); zero for any other event. |
| Queue and order | `GameState.pending_triggers` fills at detection. Before a player would receive priority (CR 117.5, 603.3) the active player's triggers go on the stack, then each other player's in turn order (CR 101.4). A controller with two or more asks `OrderTriggers` once, unless they are all of one source and ability; otherwise they go on in the order they triggered. Each trigger chooses its targets as it goes on (CR 603.3d, logged) and is removed if it cannot. State-based actions run before each sweep; the sweep repeats until none is pending, and a trigger that triggers during a sweep waits for the next round. |
| Intervening if | `intervening_if` is checked when the event happens, against the same state as the pattern, and again as the ability begins to resolve; if false then, the ability does nothing and ends `Lapsed` (CR 603.4). |
| `AnyOf` | Joins patterns ("enters or attacks"); one event triggers the ability once. |
| `limit` | `OncePerTurn` is kept per object in state, reset at the turn, and holds for triggered and activated abilities. |
| Delayed triggers | `Effect::DelayedTrigger` logs `DelayedTriggerCreated` and adds a `Delayed` to `GameState.delayed`: its pattern, effects, controller, the names the resolution had bound (the ability starts with them) and a duration. It triggers on the next event its pattern describes and is then gone (`once`), else it lasts until the end of the turn it was set up in. A duration that ran out, or a controller who left the game, drops it. |
| Replacement step | Effect steps and rules code propose events; `replace::process` returns the events that happen or the question it needs answered. It sees the abilities of the permanents in play through `ability_reader` (printed, granted, added by a keyword; `from` names the zone an effect works from) and, for a card that enters, the `Enters` effects it prints itself. A replacement is a `Replacement` ability and applies at most once to an event and to the events that replace it (CR 614.5). |
| Events replaced | `TokenCreation`: the tokens one effect makes for a player are one event (a run of `TokenCreated`). `Damage`: a `DamageDealt`, with `combat` true in the damage steps with nothing resolving; the life a lifelinker gains with it follows its damage. `ZoneChange`: `ZoneChanged`, `CreatureDied`, `CardDiscarded`, `Discarded` is the `CardDiscarded` (or its `ZoneChanged` with `ZoneCause::Discarded`) that puts a card into a graveyard, a spell that resolves as an instant or sorcery and a spell that does not resolve; a card drawn, a land played, a spell cast and a card entering are not zone changes here. `Destroyed`: a destruction (CR 701.8): a `ZoneChanged` from the battlefield with `ZoneCause::Destroyed` or `Buried` (a destroy that can't be regenerated) and a `CreatureDied` from lethal damage or deathtouch, not zero toughness, a sacrifice or an exile; it is also a move for `ZoneChange` and `MoveTo`. `CountersPut`: a `CountersChanged` that puts counters, planeswalker loyalty as it enters included and a loyalty cost paid to activate not. `LifeGain`: `LifeGained`. `Draw`: `CardDrawn` and `DrewFromEmptyLibrary`. `Enters`: a land played, a permanent spell that resolves and a card put onto the battlefield; a token that is created does not enter here. |
| Applying | `Multiply` and `Add` change the number in the event (a draw twice is two draws, the cards the top of the library in order); `MoveTo` sends the object elsewhere (a death, a discard or a spell becomes a `ZoneChanged` to the new zone, so nothing reads it as a death or as a card put into a graveyard); `Tapped`, `WithCounters` and `Choose` add to the `Entered` event of the card and propose the counters as an event of their own, which other effects then change (CR 616.2); `PayLifeOrTapped` asks yes or no and logs `LifePaid`. `UmbraArmor` replaces a destruction with `DamageRemoved` (a frozen step that clears the damage and the deathtouch mark) and the destruction of the object with the ability, a new `ZoneChanged` with `ZoneCause::Destroyed` that other effects replace in turn (a regeneration shield on the Aura, Rest in Peace) unless the batch already moves the Aura or it is indestructible; with a regeneration shield on the creature the controller is asked which applies (`ChooseReplacement`, the armor first). `Prevent` removes the event. |
| `Instead` | `ReplaceBy::Instead(effects)` runs the effects in place of the event (`replace/w5d_instead.rs`), without the stack, as its controller, and their events take its place carrying the effects already applied, so it never applies to them again (CR 614.5). The effects read the event as a trigger reads its cause: `Triggering` is the player or object it is about (the player who would gain life, draw or get the tokens, the recipient of damage, the object that would move, enter or get counters), `Amount::Triggering` its amount (the life, the damage, the counters, the tokens, one card drawn) and `Dealer` the source of damage, which only a `Damage` replacement has (validation). A step that asks a question (`May`, `Modal`, `Choose`, `Pay`, `Search`, `Sacrifice`, `Discard`, `TapOrUntap`) is named `ReplaceBy.Instead.Asks`, which `Caps` lacks, and leaves the event as it is. The compiler reads "that player loses that much life instead" (Plague Drone), "all damage that would be dealt to you is dealt to enchanted creature instead" (Pariah), "if ... would die, instead exile it and ..." with one more step (Ravenous Slime) and "instead put that many +1/+1 counters on ~ and that player mills that many cards" (Szadek) (`compile/read/w5d_replaced.rs`). |
| Named preventions and kinds of token | `PreventAnd(effects)` prevents all of a damage event (CR 615) and runs the effects as `Instead` does, reading the damage ("prevent that damage and put that many +1/+1 counters on it": `PutCounters` on `Triggering` with `Amount::Triggering`); `PreventAllBut(n)` prevents all but `n` of it ("prevent all but 1 of that damage"). Both are preventions, so damage that can't be prevented passes them; a `Shield` of `Damage` takes `PreventAnd` and is never used up (Gatta and Luzzu). `TokenCreation{what}` covers only tokens of a kind named by types, subtypes, supertypes or colours ("one or more creature tokens", validation); the engine splits a run of tokens of several kinds by the kinds the effects name, so `Instead([CreateToken{count: Triggering}])` makes as many other tokens for those only (Divine Visitation). The compiler reads these shapes, the shield after "choose target creature you control." and the sacrifice of "another Vampire or Zombie" (`compile/read/w9c_replaced.rs`, `costs.rs`). |
| Replacement choice | When two or more effects apply to an event the affected player, or the controller of the affected object, picks the one that applies first (`ChooseReplacement`, CR 616.1); an effect about its own object applies before the others without a question (CR 614.15). The answers to the questions of one batch are its journal: the engine holds the batch, adds each answer and runs `process` again, which asks the same questions in the same order, so `process` is a pure function of state, batch and journal. |
| Reveal or tapped | `RevealOrTapped(selector)` ("As this land enters, you may reveal an Elemental card from your hand. If you don't, this land enters tapped.") asks the controller yes or no (No the only answer when no card matches, so the pace does not show the hand), then which card when two or more match, and logs a `Revealed` that every seat sees before the land enters; without a reveal it enters tapped. It is `ReplaceBy.RevealOrTapped` in `Caps`. |
| As it enters | `Choose` asks the controller with `ChooseOption` (the five colours, the four that `ColorOtherThan` leaves, or the creature types of the creature cards in the game) and logs the choice in `Entered.chosen`; the permanent keeps it (`PermanentState::chosen`) and `Pred::ChosenColor`, `Pred::ChosenSubtype`, `Produce::ChosenColor` and `Produce::OrChosenColor` (the type it prints or the chosen colour, as a Thriving land or a Gate adds it) read it for the source of the ability; a permanent that has not chosen offers only the printed type. `Tapped`, `Choose` and `WithCounters` of one entering apply in no asked order (`ChooseReplacement` is not asked for them). |
| Rules of the game | `Effect::Rule` (with a duration) and `Ability::Rule` (while its source has it) change a rule through a `GameRule`. `MustAttack` and `MustBlock` name creatures that attack or block each combat if able (CR 508.1d, 509.1c: a requirement on the declaration, so only a creature that can attack or block is required). `MustAttackPlayers{who, players}` pairs the creatures with the players in order and each must attack its player this turn if able, which is the only player it may attack (a player who cannot be attacked asks nothing). `AttackTax{who, attacked, attackers, mana, per}` makes the creatures `attackers` selects (every creature when empty) attack `who` (`attacked`: `Player`, `Planeswalkers` they control or `Both`) only if their controller pays `mana` repeated `per` times (an amount counted when the attack is declared) for each of them; the taxes of several rules add up (CR 508.1h), and `mana` has no `{X}`. `AttackLimit` and `BlockLimit` let no more than that many creatures attack or block together each combat (CR 508.1c, 509.1b: a restriction on the whole declaration, at least one); `AttackLimitAt{most, at}` limits the attacks at each player or planeswalker `at` names (`You` or `This`). `CantBeCountered` names spells, `NoLifeLossFromDamage` ("damage doesn't cause you to lose life": the damage is dealt in the form `NoLifeLoss`, so the life stays and all else the damage does happens, as with infect), `CantGainLife`, `CantDraw`, `CantSearch` (libraries), `RevealTop` (play with the top card of the library revealed to every seat) and `SkipDrawStep` (the draw step does not begin on their turns, CR 614.10) players. `TriggerAgain` makes a triggered ability of a permanent the rule's controller controls trigger an additional time when the event `cause` describes (a pattern as a triggered ability writes it: "an artifact or creature entering", "a creature dying", "a Wizard you control entering") makes it trigger; one limited to once each turn is not repeated. "This spell can't be countered" is `Ability::Rule` with `from: Stack`; any other rule works from the battlefield. An effect fixes the players and objects it names when it resolves; a static ability finds them whenever the state changes. A rule is not granted by `GrantAbility`. Each rule is in `Caps` as `Rule.<Name>`, with `Effect.Rule` and `Ability.Rule`; `DamageCantBePrevented` names the sources whose damage it covers (a selector judged when the damage would be dealt, any source when empty) and whether it is combat damage (`combat`); the rule is `Rule.DamageCantBePrevented` in `Caps`. |
| Prevention | `ReplaceBy::Prevent` prevents all of the damage of an event, `ReplaceBy::PreventUpTo(n)` up to `n` of it, `ReplaceBy::PreventRemovingCounter(kind)` all of it and one counter of the kind from the permanent with the ability when it has one (Phantom Nantuko: a static `Replacement` of `Damage` to `This`; the removal is a `CountersChanged` that takes the place of the damage event, once for each event). `Effect::Shield` makes a prevention shield (CR 615) for a duration: `when` is a `ReplaceWhen::Damage` whose recipient `to` is fixed when the effect resolves (the players and objects it names then; a shield that names none begins nothing) and whose `source` and `combat` are judged when the damage would be dealt. `Prevent` makes it prevent everything it covers ("prevent all combat damage that would be dealt this turn"); `PreventUpTo(n)` prevents `n` in all ("the next 3 damage"), takes the damage of the events of a batch in the order the affected player picks (`ChooseOption`, asked only when the events differ and it cannot cover all of them), and remembers what is left (`Event::ShieldUsed`) or ends when it has none. A shield applies to an event once and takes part in the order the affected player picks like any replacement effect (`ChooseReplacement`, whose candidate names the shield); shields that prevent everything are never asked about. A shield of another `when` or `by` refuses by the name `Effect.Shield.<ReplaceWhen>`. `GameRule::DamageCantBePrevented` wins over any prevention effect and any shield: they do not apply to the damage it covers and are not used up. When the damage of a lifelinker is prevented in part the life it gains is what is left. |
| Derived statics | `layers.rs` works through the layers once for a state: the effects of resolved spells and abilities and the `Static` abilities the permanents have at that point of the pass, found through `ability_reader`, apply layer by layer to every permanent at once, so a static's `affected`, `condition` and amounts read the other objects as the earlier layers and earlier effects left them (Favorable Winds finds the flying Levitation gives). A static ability works while its source is in the zone its `from` names and its `condition` holds; one that works from a graveyard, a hand, exile, the command zone, a library or the stack (Anger: "as long as this card is in your graveyard and you control a Mountain, creatures you control have haste") affects the objects a selector names, never itself, and has the timestamp 0 since only permanents are stamped; one whose first modification comes after layer 6 (7b and later, restrictions) is dropped when its source has lost it by then, and one that has begun to apply goes on applying to the permanents it began with, so Humility stops a lord's pump but not "creatures you control are artifacts and get +1/+1" (CR 613.6). `affected` is `This`, `Attached` (the host) or `Each`, tested against the characteristics built so far; the amounts and the player of `SetController` are worked out when the modification applies. |
| Layers | `SetController` 2; `AddType`, `RemoveType`, `SetTypes` (the creature types go when it is no longer a creature), `AddSubtype`, `SetCreatureTypes`, `AddAllCreatureTypes` 4 (`SetCreatureTypes` replaces the creature types and leaves the other subtypes, CR 205.1b: Turn // Burn); `SetColors` 5; `AddKeyword`, `AddProtectionFromChosenColor`, `RemoveKeyword`, `GrantAbility`, `LoseAbilities` 6; `SetPT` 7b, or 7a in a characteristic-defining `Static`; `ModifyPT` 7c; `SwitchPT` 7d; `CantAttack`, `CantBlock`, `CantBeBlocked`, `CantBeBlockedBy(Selector)`, `CanBlockOnly(Selector)`, `MinBlockers(n)`, `MaxBlockers(n)` and `Goaded` are restrictions without a layer (`Goaded` keeps the controller of the effect or static as the goader; a goaded creature attacks each combat if able and attacks a player other than its goaders if able, CR 701.15, read by `characteristics::goaded_by` in the attack declaration; `Effect::Goad` is a `Goaded` effect until the goader's next turn). `Modification::layer()` returns it. Every modification runs from `Effect::Continuous` and from a `Static`, each with its name in `Caps` (`Mod.SetPT`, ...). `GrantAbility` gives an activated, a triggered or a static ability, validated as the ability it is in a scope of its own (a given static ability gives no abilities in turn and does not define characteristics); a keyword is `AddKeyword`. A given static ability applies in the layers after layer 6. `LoseAbilities` takes away what the object has so far, the plain mana abilities and keywords included, and what is granted after it stays. |
| Locked or live | A resolving spell or ability that modifies characteristics or control fixes its object set when it begins (CR 611.2c): `Effect::Continuous` evaluates `affected` once, to the permanents it names then, each as the incarnation named (a permanent that leaves and returns is a new object, CR 400.7), and works out its amounts then. A `Static` evaluates its selector at every derivation. |
| Timestamps | A static ability takes its permanent's entry time (an Aura's or Equipment's effects, the time it became attached), or the time of the effect that gave it when that is later (CR 613.7a); a resolved effect takes its creation time (CR 613.7), one for each modification in the order of the card's text. Both are counters assigned by the fold. |
| Durations | `UntilEndOfTurn` ends in the cleanup step with the damage (CR 514.2); `UntilEndOfCombat` after the end of combat step; `UntilYourNextTurn` when the controller's next turn begins; `WhileSourceOnBattlefield` follows the source named on the effect; `WhileYouControlSource` follows it while the controller of the effect controls it as the layers derive it (`Duration.WhileYouControlSource` in `Caps`, `engine.md`); `Forever` ends only when every object it names is gone. All six run. |
| Dependencies | Within a layer an effect follows those it depends on; otherwise effects apply by timestamp, after the characteristic-defining abilities of the layer (CR 613.3). A depends on B when applying B to what it applies to changes whether A exists (its source has the ability, its condition holds), which objects A applies to or what A does to them (CR 613.8a); the engine finds it by trying B on copies of those objects and asking A again, for the pairs where one reads (`Pred::Type`, `Subtype`, `Color`, `HasKeyword`, `Compare`, `ControlledBy` and the amounts and conditions around them) what the other writes (the characteristics its modification changes). The order is chosen again after each effect applies; a loop of dependencies goes by timestamp (CR 613.8b). The modifications of one `Static` never depend on each other. |
| Characteristic-defining | A `Static` with `defining: true` (CR 604.3) is a characteristic-defining ability: `affected` is `This`, no `condition`, no `from`, and its modifications are `SetPT` (layer 7a), `SetColors`, `AddType` or `AddSubtype`, so Nightmare's power and toughness are each the number of Swamps its controller controls. It works in every zone, so a card in a hand, a graveyard, exile, a library or on the stack has the characteristics it defines, with its controller the owner, and a printed `*` is a number once an ability defines it (`Card.VariableStats`). On the battlefield it applies first in its layer and neither it nor another effect depends on the other kind. It is `Ability.Static.Defining` in `Caps`. |

### Copies

| Part | Rule |
|---|---|
| Token copy | `CreateToken{ token: CopyOf{ of: Ref, except: [Modification], no_mana_cost } }` ("create a token that's a copy of target creature you control", "... of it", "... of enchanted creature"): one token for each object `of` names, with the copiable values of that object (CR 707.2) and the exceptions applied to them, and no mana cost when `no_mana_cost` is set (embalm, eternalize), tapped and attacking when `tapped` and `attacking` are set (Flamerush Rider); an object that has left is read by last known information, and a transformed double-faced permanent is copied as the face it shows, with no back face. It is `Effect.CreateToken.Copy` in `Caps`. |
| Enters as a copy | `Replacement{ when: Enters{what: This}, by: AsCopy{ of: Selector, except: [Modification], tapped?, until_end_of_turn? } }`: a selector with `In(Graveyard)` offers the cards of every graveyard ("You may have this creature enter as a copy of any creature card in a graveyard", Body Double), `tapped` has the permanent enter tapped when it copies ("enter tapped as a copy of any land on the battlefield", Vesuva) and `until_end_of_turn` makes a copy that ends in the cleanup step ("As this artifact enters, you may have it become a copy of any creature on the battlefield until end of turn, except it has haste", Cursed Mirror). "You may have this enter as a copy of any creature on the battlefield" is `of: [Type(Creature)]`, "a creature you control" adds `ControlledBy(You)` and "a creature an opponent controls" `ControlledBy(Opponents)` (the choice is offered only the creatures the words name), and Phantasmal Image's exceptions are `AddSubtype(Illusion)` and a `GrantAbility` of its trigger. It is `ReplaceBy.AsCopy` in `Caps`. |
| Exceptions | `AddType`, `AddSubtype`, `AddKeyword`, `GrantAbility`, `SetColors`, `SetPT`, `SetTypes` and `RemoveSupertype` with fixed amounts are part of the copiable values. Any other modification in an `except` list is named `Copy.Except.<Mod>`, so the card is not Verified. The compiler reads the parts joined by "and" ("it isn't legendary and it's an artifact in addition to its other types and it has myriad"), the same parts as a list that names its subject once ("it isn't legendary, is an artifact in addition to its other types, and has myriad"), a type alone ("it's an artifact" and "it's an artifact and it loses all other card types" are `SetTypes`), subtypes and keywords with it ("it's a Vehicle artifact with crew 3 and it loses all other card types": `SetTypes`, `AddSubtype`, `AddKeyword`) and an activated or triggered ability in quotes (a mana ability too); the writers produce each of these wordings. |
| Copy of each | `CreateToken{ token: CopyOf{ of: Each(selector) } }` ("For each token you control, create a token that's a copy of that permanent", "For each creature token you control, create a token that's a copy of that creature"): one token for each permanent the group holds when the effect resolves, each a copy of that permanent (a token copied by a token is a copy of the first token's copiable values). The noun after "that" is the group's one card type, else "permanent". The group may be a player's: "For each creature target player controls, create a token that's a copy of that creature" is a player target and `ControlledBy(Target(0))`. "Create a token that's a copy of target creature. If this spell was kicked, create five of those tokens instead." (Rite of Replication) is an `If` of `Kicked` over two `CreateToken` of the same `CopyOf`, one token and the count. |
| Populate | "Populate" is a `Choose{ from: Each([Type(Creature), Token, ControlledBy(You)]), as }` followed by a `CreateToken` of `CopyOf{ of: Bound(as) }`. "Populate. The token enters tapped and attacking." (Ghired, Conclave Exile) sets `tapped` and `attacking` on that `CreateToken`; "Populate. The token created this way gains haste. Sacrifice it at the beginning of the next end step." (Determined Iteration) binds it as `tokens` and adds a `Continuous` with the keywords and a delayed `Sacrifice`. |
| Copy of a spell | `CopySpell{ who = You, what: Ref, count = 1, new_targets, except: [Modification] }` ("Copy target instant or sorcery spell. You may choose new targets for the copy"; Fork's "except that the copy is red" is `except: [SetColors([Red])]`): each spell `what` names that is still on the stack is copied `count` times (CR 707.10). A copy is put on the stack above the spell and is not cast: no cast trigger, no payment, no storm count. `who` names the player who controls the copies and chooses their new targets (the controller of the effect by default; Demonstrate names the opponent it chose).
 It has the modes, X, optional costs and targets of the original, the values of the face the original is in use as, and the exceptions; `new_targets` lets its controller choose new targets with the ordinary target decision. A copy that resolves or is countered ceases to exist, and a copy of a permanent spell becomes a token. It is `Effect.CopySpell` in `Caps`. |
| Next spell cast | "When you next cast an instant or sorcery spell this turn, copy that spell. You may choose new targets for the copy." (Doublecast) is a `DelayedTrigger{ when: SpellCast{what, by: You}, once, this_turn, effects: [CopySpell{what: Triggering}] }` with the count, exceptions and new targets of a copy of the spell cast (`compile/read/w2g_next_cast.rs`); `this_turn` drops the trigger at the end of the turn it was set up in, also when it has not triggered. On an instant or sorcery the line is the spell's effect, not a triggered ability. Doublecast, Galvanic Iteration, Teach by Example, Storm King's Thunder, Dual Strike and Thunderclap Drake are reproduced. |
| Copy of an ability | `CopyAbility{ what: Ref, count = 1, new_targets }` (Strionic Resonator, Lithoform Engine) copies an activated or triggered ability on the stack; `Pred::Ability(Triggered)` and `Pred::Ability(Activated)` select them as targets ("target triggered ability you control"). The copy has the source, rules, cause and choices of the original, is not activated or triggered, and may take new targets. It is `Effect.CopyAbility` in `Caps`. |
| Becoming a copy | `BecomeCopy{ what, of, except, duration }` ("Target creature becomes a copy of that creature until end of turn", Cytoshape: a `Choose` followed by this with `of: Bound(chosen)`): the permanents `what` names take the copiable values of `of` as the first layer. `UntilEndOfTurn` ends in the cleanup step and the permanent is what it was before; `UntilYourNextTurn` ends the same way as its controller's next turn begins ("Target Shapeshifter becomes a copy of target creature until your next turn", Shapesharer: `what: Target(0)`, `of: Target(1)`; `Copy.Duration.UntilYourNextTurn` in `Caps`); `Forever` stays while the permanent does. Any other duration is named `Copy.Duration.<name>` and the card is not Verified. `what` may be a group: "Each other creature becomes a copy of target nonlegendary creature until end of turn" (Mirrorweave) is `what: Each([Type(Creature), Not(Is(Target(0)))])` and "Choose target creature you control. Each other creature becomes a copy of that creature until end of turn, except it isn't legendary." (Nanogene Conversion) is the same effect with the exception; a land that "becomes a copy of target nontoken artifact you control with mana value X" (The Mycosynth Gardens) has `duration: Forever`. It is `Effect.BecomeCopy` in `Caps`. |
| Storm | `Keyword(Storm)` adds the trigger "when you cast this spell, copy it for each spell cast before it this turn": `CopySpell{ what: This, count: Triggering, new_targets }` working from the stack. It is `Keyword.Storm` in `Caps`. |
| Demonstrate | `Keyword(Demonstrate)` adds a cast trigger from the stack: `May{ CopySpell{ who: You }, Choose{ from: Opponents, as: "demonstrated" }, CopySpell{ who: Bound("demonstrated") } }`. The caster's copy goes on the stack first and the opponent's above it, so the opponent's resolves first; each controller chooses new targets for their own copy, and an opponent who keeps a target that is illegal for them has a copy that fizzles. Nobody is asked which opponent in a game of two. A copy is not cast, so it demonstrates nothing. It is `Keyword.Demonstrate` in `Caps`, and the compiler reads the keyword line (`Hideaway N` is read too; the engine does not run it yet, so those cards stay Partial). Replication Technique, Excavation Technique and Incarnation Technique are Verified. |
| For Mirrodin!, Job select, Equip to | `For Mirrodin!` and `Job select` add an enters trigger that creates a 2/2 red Rebel or a 1/1 Hero token and attaches the Equipment to it (`living_weapon::enters_attached_to`, shared with living weapon); `EquipTo{to, cost}` is an equip ability that targets only the creatures `to` selects ("Equip legendary creature {2}", "Equip commander", "Equip creature token"). The cards are Verified corpus cards (Mirran Bardiche, Goldwarden's Helm, Samurai's Katana, Thinking Cap and others). |
| Still waiting for (o2c, o2fa to o2fd) | A storm trigger that resolves after its spell has left the stack copies it from last known information (o2c); the compiler reads no "when you next cast" with an effect other than a copy of the spell (o2fa), no exception that sets a name (o2fb), no token copy that attacks a player (o2fc) and no "Populate X times" (o2fd). |

## Keywords

| Item | Rule |
|---|---|
| Data | `{"Keyword": {"Ward": "{2}"}}`. `Keyword` is an enum in `keyword.rs` with `name()` in Scryfall's spelling (compared with the record's `keywords`). It declares every keyword the roadmap lists. |
| Engine | `keywords/mod.rs` maps each variant to a `KeywordRules` value defined in `keywords/<name>.rs`; a keyword without its task is a stub that changes nothing. A keyword task replaces its stub and adds its `Caps` entry in `caps.rs`. The lists of keywords (`Keyword::NAMES`, `RUNS` in `caps.rs`) are slices with one entry per line and no length, and tests hold them to the enum and to the rules that run. `keywords_of(state, id)` is the one reader of the keywords an object has: the keyword abilities `ability_reader` lists, with layer 6 joining there: the keywords continuous effects grant (`AddKeyword`) follow in timestamp order, never twice; `RemoveKeyword` removes one and `LoseAbilities` every keyword so far, in the same order, so a keyword granted after them stays. The keywords of combat, hexproof and flash are built, and ward, prowess and battle cry as triggered abilities (`engine.md`, Combat and keywords). `player_keywords(state, player)` is the same reader for a player (hexproof, CR 702.11d). |
| Convoke, improvise, delve | No hook: the payment reads the keywords (`help::offered`, `engine.md`, Payment); `Keyword.Convoke`, `Keyword.Improvise` and `Keyword.Delve` are in `Caps`, and the compiler reads the keyword lines and their reminder text. |
| Keywords given to the cards of a hand, a graveyard or exile | "Each creature card in your hand has cycling {1}{U}." is a `Static` whose `affected` is `Each` of a selector with `In(Hand)` (`[{"Type": "Creature"}, {"In": "Hand"}, {"OwnedBy": "You"}]`) and whose mods are `AddKeyword` with the keyword and its cost. The layers hold the cards of a zone a static names like permanents (`docs/engine.md`, Continuous effects), so the cards cycle, flash back, are discarded to madness or drawn for miracle as if they printed the keyword. The compiler reads "Each <card phrase in your hand or graveyard> has <keyword> [cost]" and "<cards> in your hand have <keyword> [cost]" for cycling, miracle, flashback, madness, unearth, encore, scavenge (a mana cost) and retrace (`compile/read/card_grants.rs`); a cost "equal to its mana cost" and a union of groups stay with the table. |
| Keywords given to spells | "Nonartifact spells you cast have improvise." is a `Static` whose `affected` is `Each` of a selector that holds `Spell` (`["Spell", {"Not": {"Type": "Artifact"}}, {"ControlledBy": "You"}]`, the selector of a cost modifier) and whose mods are `AddKeyword`. The layers hold permanents only, so `spell_keywords::given_to_spell` reads these statics from the permanents in play and judges the spell as the announcement does (on the stack under its caster, `Scope::casting`); `help::offered` adds what it returns to the spell's own keywords. The validator allows `Convoke`, `Improvise` and `Delve` only (`Ability.Static.SpellKeywords` in `Caps`); a static over spells with another keyword stays with the table. The compiler reads "<spells> [you cast / your opponents cast] have <keyword> [and <keyword>]" (`compile/read/spell_keywords.rs`, a spell kind is a cost-modifier subject: "Nonartifact spells you cast", "Artifact spells you cast", "Legendary creature spells you cast", "Noncreature spells"), and writes it back. |
| Split second, undaunted | Split second answers the hook `silences_players`: while a spell with it is on the stack `priority_options` leaves `castable` and `abilities` empty and `ways::grantable` refuses every cast a resolution grants; mana abilities, special actions and triggers go on. Undaunted adds the cost modifier `Reduce {1}` of the spell itself `per` `Amount::Players(Opponents)` (the opponents still in the game), read like affinity. `Keyword.SplitSecond` and `Keyword.Undaunted` are in `Caps`, and the compiler reads the keyword lines. |
| Affinity, typecycling | `Keyword::Affinity(Selector)` adds a `CostModifier` of the spell itself ({1} less, `per` the count of the selector's permanents the caster controls); `Keyword::Typecycling { find, cost }` adds the cycling ability with a `Search` of the library for `find`, a reveal, a move to the hand and a shuffle, and logs `Cycled` as cycling does. The compiler reads `Affinity for <plural noun>` (artifacts, artifact creatures, historic permanents, a subtype such as Equipment or Islands, tokens), `Landcycling`, `Basic landcycling` and `<land type>cycling`; other types stay with the table. |
| Upkeep and death keywords | `Echo(cost)`, `CumulativeUpkeep(cost)`, `Undying` and `Persist` return abilities from `adds_abilities`. Echo: `StepBegins` of your upkeep, the intervening "if" `NewlyControlled(This)` (the permanent was new to its controller when their turn began), `Pay` the cost, unpaid `Sacrifice` `This`. Cumulative upkeep: the same step, `PutCounters` an age counter (`CounterKind::Other("age")`) on `This`, then `Pay` the cost `times` the age counters on `This` (a cost paid 0 times is paid and asks nothing, as for a permanent that has left), unpaid `Sacrifice` `This`; the instances of one permanent count the same counters. Undying and persist: `ZoneChange` of `This` from the battlefield to a graveyard with the intervening "if" that `This` had 0 counters of their kind (last known information), then `Move` the card now in a graveyard (`Each` of `In(Graveyard)` and `This`, found by identity) to the battlefield with `counters` of that kind; a card that left the graveyard in response, and a token, are not found. Each is in `Caps` (with `Condition.NewlyControlled`), and the compiler reads the keyword line and its reminder text. The cost of echo and cumulative upkeep is paid by a `Pay`: mana, life, a discard, a sacrifice (`Effect.Pay.SacrificeSelf` stays outside `Caps`). |
| Attack, cast and entering keywords | `Exalted`, `Melee`, `Mentor`, `Dethrone`, `Annihilator(n)`, `Extort` and `Evolve` return one triggered ability each from `adds_abilities`. Exalted: `AttacksAlone` (the only declared attacker, a creature you control; `Triggering` is the attacker) pumps it +1/+1 until end of turn, once for each instance. Melee: `Attacks` of `This` pumps it by `Players(Attacked)`; `Attacked` is the opponents the controller's creatures attacked this combat, directly or through a planeswalker, in turn order, a creature that left combat still counting. Mentor: `Attacks` of `This` targets an attacking creature with less power than `This` (`Pred::Compare` against the `Stat` of `This`) and puts a +1/+1 counter on it; with no such creature the trigger is removed. Dethrone: `AttacksPlayer` of `This` at `MostLife` (the player with the most life, or each one tied for it, judged as the trigger goes on the stack; attacking a planeswalker does not count) puts a +1/+1 counter on `This`. Annihilator: `Attacks` of `This`, `Sacrifice` of `n` permanents by `Defending` (the player the attacking creature attacks or whose planeswalker it attacks, read from the combat, or from where it was attacking once it has left), asked only when the player has more. Extort: `SpellCast` by you, `Pay` `{W/B}`, paid: each opponent loses 1 life and you gain as much life as there are opponents. Evolve: `ZoneChange` to the battlefield of a creature you control with the intervening "if" that its power or its toughness is greater than that of `This` (the entering creature read as it was, also after it left), `PutCounters` +1/+1 on `This`. Each is in `Caps`, with `Trigger.AttacksAlone`, and the compiler reads the keyword line. |
| Counter keywords | `Vanishing(n)` and `Fading(n)` return an `Enters` replacement `WithCounters` of n time (the suspend counter) or fade counters from `adds_abilities`, and the upkeep triggers. Vanishing: `StepBegins` of your upkeep with the intervening "if" of a time counter on `This`, `RemoveCounters`; and `CountersRemoved` of time counters on `This` with the intervening "if" of none left, `Sacrifice` `This`, so any removal of the last counter sacrifices it. Fading: one upkeep trigger whose effect is `If` a fade counter is on `This` then `RemoveCounters` else `Sacrifice` `This`, so a counter put on in response saves it. `Backup { count, abilities }` holds the abilities that follow the keyword on the card, which the compiler copies in (a backup followed by a clause it does not read, or by an ability that cannot be granted, is left to the table; other backups are not listed). Its enters trigger targets a creature, puts `count` +1/+1 counters on it, and when the target is not `This` (`Matches` `Other`) grants each listed ability until end of turn: a keyword as `AddKeyword`, any other ability as `GrantAbility`. The walker checks the listed abilities as granted ones. Each is in `Caps`, and the compiler reads `Vanishing N`, `Fading N` and `Backup N` with their reminder text. |
| Copy keywords | `Myriad`: an `Attacks` trigger on `This`; when the controller has two opponents or more it asks once (`May`), then `MyriadCopies{ of: This, as }` makes, for each attacking creature `of` names, a token copy for every opponent other than the player it attacks (or whose planeswalker it attacks), tapped and attacking that opponent and never declared as an attacker, and a delayed trigger exiles the bound tokens at the beginning of the end of combat step. `Squad(cost)` is an additional cost paid any number of times, asked and counted as a multikicker is (`Amount.Kicks`, `Condition.Kicked`), with an enters trigger `CreateToken` of `CopyOf` `This` and `count: Kicks`; the copies were not cast, so they pay nothing and make none. `Ravenous` is an `Enters` replacement `WithCounters` of `X` +1/+1 counters and an enters trigger that draws a card when `X` is 5 or more. Each is in `Caps` (with `Effect.MyriadCopies`), and the compiler reads the keyword lines and their reminder text. |
| Riot | `Riot` returns an `Enters` replacement `ReplaceBy.Riot`: as the permanent enters its controller picks a +1/+1 counter on it or haste for as long as it stays. A static ability that gives it (`AddKeyword(Riot)` to a group, "Nontoken creatures you control have riot.") works for a creature that is about to enter, and each instance asks on its own. It is in `Caps` with `ReplaceBy.Riot`; the compiler reads the keyword line, `have riot` in a group static, and "Activate only if this creature has a +1/+1 counter on it." beside an activated ability. |
| Decayed | `Decayed` is the block restriction of the holder and an attack trigger whose delayed trigger of `StepBegins{EndCombat}` has the controller sacrifice the creature (`Sacrifice` of `Is(This)`). It is in `Caps`; the compiler reads the keyword line, "with decayed" on a token and "gains decayed" in an effect. |
| Fight | `Fight{what, with}`: each creature `what` names deals damage equal to its power to each creature `with` names, and the reverse, in one batch; the compiler reads "enchanted creature fights up to one target creature you don't control", "~ fights target creature", "it fights" / "that creature fights" (the card, or the creature the sentence before or the event named) and the fights of two targets: "Target creature you control fights up to one target creature you don't control", "Target creature fights another target creature", also after a sentence about the first target ("Put a +1/+1 counter on target creature you control. Then that creature fights ...", "Target creature you control gets +X/+X until end of turn. It fights ...") and with other sentences around it. The reader puts the second "target" phrase in the clause as `Target(1)` (`selectors::second_target`; every other template still reads one target per clause), and "another" / "other" in it is `Not(Is(Target(0)))`, not `Other` (the card). The writer is `render/each_target.rs` (`fight_among`: the sentences before that name no target, the first target's sentences, the fight as "it fights" in any of its wordings, the sentences after). Enrage ("Whenever ~ is dealt damage, it fights ...") and "Whenever ~ or another creature you control enters, that creature fights ..." (`Triggering`) read; Apex Altisaur, Gruul Ragebeast, Primal Might, Clash of Titans, Hunt the Weak, Prey Upon, Pit Fight, Epic Confrontation, Prizefight and Smell Fear are Verified. |
| Devour, casualty, offspring | `Devour{counters, of}` returns an `Enters` replacement `ReplaceBy.Devour`: as the permanent enters its controller picks any number (`ChooseCards`, min 0, asked only when something qualifies) of the other permanents they control that `of` matches (creatures, artifacts, lands or Foods), they are sacrificed in the same batch before it enters (their replacements and dies triggers apply), and it enters with `counters` +1/+1 counters for each. `Casualty(n)` is an optional additional cost, a sacrifice of a creature of power n or more, read as `Condition.AdditionalCostPaid`, with a trigger from the stack like storm's that copies the spell once (`CopySpell`, new targets) when the cost was paid; the copy is not cast, so it has no casualty trigger. `Offspring(cost)` is an optional additional cost with an enters trigger that makes one token copy of `This` whose power and toughness are set to 1 (`CopyOf` with a `SetPT` exception) when the cost was paid; the token paid nothing and makes none. Each is in `Caps` (with `ReplaceBy.Devour`), and the compiler reads `Devour N`, `Devour artifact N`, `Devour land N`, `Devour Food N`, `Casualty N` and `Offspring {cost}` with their reminder text. |
| Commander keywords | `Partner`, `Partner with <name>`, `Partner—<text>` (`PartnerGroup(text)`: Friends forever, Character select, Survivors, Father & son), `Doctor's companion` and `Choose a Background` are deck-building rules (`CardDefinition::partners_with`, `can_be_commander_beside`), and `Partner with` also adds an enters trigger; "~ can be your commander" is `Rule.CanBeCommander` (written without `from`, never as an effect). Each is in `Caps`, the compiler reads the keyword line and its reminder text, and Scryfall lists `Partner` for `Partner with` and `Partner—<text>` cards (`engine.md`, Commander). The name of `Partner with` is the name of a card (CR 702.124): "Partner with itself" is the card's own name (a deck holds two of it), and the compiler leaves a line to the table when the library has no card of that name (`Partner with Knight`; `compile_among`, which `generate` calls with the names the rules may use). |
| Companion | `Companion(DeckCondition)` with `Each {of, has}`, `Distinct {of}` and `ExtraCards(n)` over the starting deck (`mtg_cards::companion`, `engine.md`, Companion). The compiler reads "Each permanent card in your starting deck has mana value N or less", "Your starting deck contains only cards with mana value N or greater and land cards", "Each nonland card in your starting deck has a different name", "Each creature card in your starting deck is a … card" and "at least twenty cards more than the minimum deck size"; the other conditions (even or odd mana values, an activated ability, a shared card type, one mana symbol, expansion symbols, nostalgic) leave the line to the table. |
| Required additional costs | `Ability::AdditionalCost{ required: true }` is "As an additional cost to cast this spell, discard a card" (also a sacrifice, an exile from a hand or a graveyard, life; a fixed count of one or more, or X). The compiler reads the cost forms an ability's cost reads, with or without "you may", and "reveal a Giant card from your hand" (`RevealFromHand`, the card stays in the hand); an X in an optional cost or in `{X}` mana is left to the table. A choice between costs ("discard a card or pay 3 life", "sacrifice a creature, discard a card, or pay 4 life", "reveal a Dinosaur card from your hand or pay {1}": Bitter Triumph, Dusk Mangler, Thunderherd Migration) is the one item `OneOf([Cost])` of a required cost, each cost of one part or of mana; the walker names it `Cost.OneOf` there and `Cost.OneOf.Elsewhere` anywhere else, so no other cost runs it. The reader of a subtype before "card" ("an Elf card", "a Giant card from your hand") is shared by every phrase that names cards. A discard names a kind of card ("discard a land card": Magmatic Insight). An X the cost counts ("pay X life": Toxic Deluge, "exile X cards from your graveyard": Skeletal Scrying) is the X the caster announces; a face with such a line reads the X in its effects as one with `{X}` in its mana cost does, including "-X/-X" (`Times{ by: -1, of: X }`). The engine adds the parts to every way the card is cast (`engine.md`, Announcement). |
| What a cost took | `Ref::Paid` is the objects the cost of an activated ability or the required additional cost of a spell took, and `Amount::Stat { of: Paid, stat }` reads their power, toughness or mana value as they were (`engine.md`, Announcement). The compiler reads "the sacrificed creature's power", "the discarded card's mana value" and the like after "equal to" and in a where clause (`where_x::paid_stat`) only where the cost takes objects (`Reader.paid`: the ability's own cost, or `Own.pays` for a spell with such a required additional cost), and the walker refuses `Paid` where it takes none. |
| Optional costs | `additional_cost` gives the costs of kicker, multikicker, buyback and entwine, and `Ability::AdditionalCost` one of its own; each is asked with the cast (`engine.md`, Announcement). "Kicker {W} and/or {2}{B}" is two `Kicker` keywords, each its own optional cost, and `Condition::KickedWith(cost)` ("if it was kicked with its {W} kicker") asks for the one that was paid (`announce::kicked_with`, the card must print a Kicker with that cost). A kicker, buyback, entwine, flashback, evoke or madness cost may hold life and parts that pick objects (`Kicker—Sacrifice a creature`, `Evoke—Exile a red card from your hand`, `Flashback—{1}{U}, Pay 3 life`); the compiler reads them in words, and the walker names `Keyword.Cost.<part>` only for the keywords that pay none and for counters removed or an X amount. |
| Exile until the source leaves | `Exile{what, duration: WhileSourceOnBattlefield}` ("exile target nonland permanent an opponent controls until ~ leaves the battlefield", CR 610.3): the card waits in exile linked to the source and returns as a new object under its owner's control once the source has left the battlefield; a source that left before the effect resolved exiles nothing (CR 610.3c); `UntilOpponentBecomesMonarch` ("until an opponent becomes the monarch", Palace Jailer) returns the card once any opponent of the exiler has become the monarch, whoever holds the permanent or whether it is still there; other durations are not run (`Exile.Duration.*` gaps). The compiler reads and writes the sentence for any source noun (`compile/read/effects.rs`) |
| Mana spent | `Condition::ManaSpent(mana)` holds when at least one mana of that type was spent to cast the spell the source is or came from (CR 601.2h); the compiler reads "sacrifice it unless {M} was spent to cast it" and the intervening "if {M} was spent to cast it" (`compile/read/spent.rs`) |
| Hideaway | `Hideaway(n)` adds an enters trigger of `effect::hideaway(n)`: `LookAtTop`, a `Choose` of one (the rest bound), `ExileHidden` of the chosen card and a `Move` of the rest to the bottom in a random order; each instance is its own trigger. `ExileHidden` exiles face down with `ZoneCause::Hideaway{by}` (`by` is the source permanent), which lays the card down as `FaceDown::Hideaway{by}`. `Pred::ExiledWith(Ref)` selects the cards in exile whose hider is a permanent the reference names (`[In(Exile), ExiledWith(This)]`); a permanent that left is a new object, so its new self finds nothing and the card can never be played. `CastFromZone{play: true, free: true}` plays the card: a land is put onto the battlefield as a land play only on the controller's turn in a main phase with nothing on the stack but the resolving ability and a land play left (CR 305.1), any other card is cast free; the step binds whether it did, so `If{Performed}` right after it reads "if you do". The owner of the hidden card and the current controller of its permanent look at it (`GameState::may_look_as`); players who controlled the permanent before are not tracked. `Keyword.Hideaway` and `Effect.ExileHidden` are in `Caps`; the compiler reads the keyword line and its reminder text, and the sentences of the cards: "You may play (or cast) the exiled card without paying its mana cost" (`cast::exiled_card`; `CastFromZone{play}` of `Each[In(Exile), ExiledWith(This)]`) with its condition before ("Then if C, you may ...") or after ("... if C"), "if there are cards exiled with it, you may play one of them without paying its mana cost" (a `Choose` bound as `played` and the cast) and "If you do, return ~ to its owner's hand" (an `If{Performed}`; "return ~" reads in abilities only). The conditions C are those of `as_long_as` plus `facts.rs`: "you attacked with three or more creatures this turn" and "an opponent was dealt 7 or more damage this turn" (`Amount::Tallied{who, what}` against a number; `TurnTally::Attackers` counts the creatures declared as attackers by their controller, `DamageTaken` the damage dealt to a player after prevention, both from zero at each turn, `CountersPut` the times counters were put on the object `who` names (read through an object, one for each counter kind put, `Tally::Countered`; "a counter was put on ~ this turn" is `Tallied { who: This, what: CountersPut }` at least 1, as an intervening "if" or a condition)), "creatures you control have total power 10 or greater", "each player has no cards in hand" and "there are cards exiled with it". A state of the game opens an `If` only after "Then" or before the exiled-card cast; at the start of a trigger it is the intervening "if", which the table keeps. |
| Cipher | `Keyword(Cipher)` ends the `Spell` ability of the card with `Choose{from: Each[Type(Creature), ControlledBy(You)], count: 0..1, as}` and `Encode{what: This, on: Bound}` (`keywords::cipher::encoded`, not for a copy of the spell), and adds a trigger that works from exile: `DealsDamage{source: [EncodedWith(This)], to: EachPlayer, combat}` with `CopyCard{what: This, as}` and `CastFromZone{what: Bound, free}`. `Encode` moves the resolving spell card from the stack to exile with `ZoneCause::Encoded{by}` (the creature as it is now), which the fold keeps on the card as `GameObject::encoded_on` until a zone change; nothing happens for a copy of a spell, a creature that is gone or one its controller does not control. `Pred::EncodedWith(Ref)` is a creature that a card the reference names is encoded on (`GameState::encoded_creature`: the card lies in exile and the creature is still the permanent it was, so a creature that left and came back carries nothing). The trigger is controlled by the controller of the creature. `CopyCard{who, what, as}` copies each face-up card of exile into a token card there (`Event::CardCopied`, owner `who`) and binds it; `CastFromZone` casts it free, and a copy anywhere but on the stack ceases to exist at the next state-based check (`Event::CopyCeased`, CR 704.5e). A cast copy is a token spell that is neither encoded nor copied again. `Keyword.Cipher`, `Effect.Encode` and `Effect.CopyCard` are in `Caps`; the compiler reads the keyword line and its reminder text. |
| Manifest, cloak | `Manifest{who, count, library, as}` and `Cloak{..}` put the top `count` cards of the library of `who` (or of the player `library` names) onto the battlefield face down under the control of `who` (`FaceDown::Manifest` and `Cloak`, `engine.md`). `who` defaults to you and `count` to one; `as` binds the permanents. The compiler reads "Manifest the top card of your library.", "Cloak the top two cards of your library.", "Its controller manifests the top card of their library." and "you manifest the top card of that player's library." (`compile/read/manifest.rs`, in the sentence and "you may" shapes); "it becomes an Aura with enchant creature. Manifest the top card of your library and attach ~ to it." in a trigger of the card is a `Continuous` of the subtype and the keyword for good, a `Manifest` bound as `manifested` and an `Attach` to it (`compile/read/c6_blk_aura.rs`, Cloudform and Lightform); a chosen card, "manifest dread" and another follow-up that uses the permanent stay with the table. Reality Shift, Ransom Note, Mastery of the Unseen and Thieving Amalgam are Verified. |
| Gift | `Gift(kind)` (`GiftKind`: a card, a Treasure, a Food, a tapped Fish, an Octopus, an extra turn) is an optional additional cost with no mana that names an opponent: `ChooseCosts` offers it once for each opponent in the game and at most one is chosen, and the spell offers it only when the targets it needs with the promise exist. `Condition.AdditionalCostPaid` reads "if the gift was promised", `Ref.Promised` is the opponent (nobody once they have left the game) and `Amount.Promised` is 1 or 0, which a `TargetSpec` reads in `bounds.max` for the target that only a promise adds ("If the gift was promised, instead destroy two target ...": the first target always, the second with `bounds {min 1, max Promised}`; a specification whose most is zero asks for nothing). The keyword adds an enters trigger that gives the gift to `Ref.Promised` and an instant or sorcery runs the same effects before its own. `Keyword.Gift` and `Amount.Promised` are in `Caps`; the compiler reads the keyword line and "the gift was promised". |
| Mutate | `Mutate(cost)` is a way to cast a creature card from a hand (or a commander from the command zone) for a mana cost, targeting a non-Human creature with the same owner (`CastWay.Mutate`); the keyword adds no ability, the card's own "Whenever this creature mutates" is `Trigger.Mutates` (`TriggerPattern::Mutates`, "it" is the creature) and "the number of times this creature has mutated" is `Amount.Mutations`, read after "where X is" and "equal to". The compiler reads the keyword line and its reminder text; the engine side is in [engine.md](engine.md), Mutate. |
| Bestow | `Bestow(cost)` is a way to cast a creature card from a hand as an Aura spell with enchant creature (`CastWay.Bestow`, read as `CastWith(Bestow)`, the cost mana plus any part a spell cost pays); the keyword adds no ability, the card's own static ability on `Attached` is the bonus. The compiler reads the keyword line and its reminder text. |
| Graveyard casts | `Unearth(cost)` adds a sorcery-speed ability that works from the graveyard (the card returns with haste, a granted replacement exiles it when it would leave, a delayed trigger exiles it at the next end step); `Escape(cost)` is a way to cast from the graveyard (`CastWay.Escape`), its cost mana plus `ExileFromGraveyard` with `Other`, and "~ escapes with a +1/+1 counter on it" is an `Enters` replacement conditioned on `CastWith(Escape)`; `Rebound` has no data, the replacement step does the rest; `Aftermath` has no data and stands on the second half of a split card, which the engine casts for its mana cost (X included) from the graveyard only and exiles when it leaves the stack. The compiler reads all four keyword lines and the "escapes with" sentence. `JumpStart` and `Retrace` have no data: each is a way to cast from the graveyard (`CastWay.JumpStart`, `CastWay.Retrace`) for the printed mana cost and the discard of a card (a land card for retrace), picked with the cast; a jump-started spell is exiled when it leaves the stack, a retraced one is not. `Embalm(cost)` and `Eternalize(cost)` add a sorcery-speed ability from the graveyard whose cost is `cost` and the exile of the card (`ExileSelf`) and whose effect is `CreateToken{ CopyOf{ of: This, except: [SetColors, AddSubtype(Zombie)], no_mana_cost } }` (eternalize: black and `SetPT` 4/4). The compiler reads the four keyword lines. `Encore(cost)` adds the same kind of ability whose effects are `CreateToken{ CopyOf{ of: This }, count: Players(Opponents), as: "encored" }`, haste for the named tokens, `Rule{ MustAttackPlayers{ who: Bound("encored"), players: Opponents } }` and a delayed trigger at the next end step that sacrifices them; the compiler reads the keyword line. |
| Scavenge, transmute | `Scavenge(cost)` adds a sorcery-speed ability from the graveyard whose cost is `cost` and the exile of the card and whose effect puts `Stat{This, Power}` +1/+1 counters on a target creature (the power the card had, by last known information). `Transmute(cost)` adds a sorcery-speed ability from a hand whose cost is `cost` and the discard of the card and whose effects search the library for a card whose mana value equals the discarded card's (`Pred.Compare{ManaValue, Eq, Stat{This, ManaValue}}`), reveal it, put it into the hand and shuffle. The compiler reads both keyword lines. |
| Awaken | `Awaken{count, cost}` is a way to cast a spell from a hand for a mana cost (`CastWay.Awaken`, read as `CastWith(Awaken)`). The spell ability of the card gains, behind its own targets and effects, a target "land you control" whose most is `Amount.CastWith(Awaken)` (one when the spell was cast that way, so nothing is asked otherwise) and, when it was, `count` +1/+1 counters on it and a forever `Continuous` that makes it a 0/0 Elemental creature with haste in addition to its types; `resolve::spell_ability` adds both for announcement and resolution. The compiler reads the keyword line. |
| Gravestorm | `Gravestorm` adds the cast trigger of storm with `count: Amount.PermanentsGraved`, the permanents put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn (a per-turn `Tally.Graved` of the owner, tokens included), read as the trigger resolves. The compiler reads the keyword line. |
| Sunburst | `Sunburst` adds two `Enters` replacements, +1/+1 counters for a creature and charge counters for any other permanent, each `Amount.ColorsSpent` of them: the colors among the mana spent to cast the spell (none for a permanent that was not cast). The compiler reads the keyword line. |
| Umbra armor | `UmbraArmor` adds one replacement that works from the battlefield: `ReplaceWhen::Destroyed` of what the Aura is attached to (`Is(Attached)`) by `ReplaceBy::UmbraArmor` (`keywords/umbra_armor.rs`; the step is in `replace.rs`, see Replacement step). The pump and the granted ability of the Aura are a `Static` on `Attached` and end with the Aura. The compiler reads the keyword line and `has base power and toughness 8/8` in a static (`SetPT`). |
| Dash, ninjutsu | `Ninjutsu(cost)` adds an activated ability from the hand whose cost is the mana plus the return of an unblocked attacker you control (`Pred.Unblocked`) and whose effects put the card onto the battlefield tapped and call `JoinAttack`; the compiler reads its keyword line. `Dash(cost)` is a way to cast a creature card from a hand (`CastWay.Dash`, the cost mana plus any part a spell cost pays); the keyword adds haste conditioned on `CastWith(Dash)` and an enters trigger that sets a delayed trigger returning the permanent to its owner's hand at the next end step. The compiler reads the keyword line and its reminder text. `Blitz(cost)` is the same kind of way (`CastWay.Blitz`): the keyword adds haste and a trigger that draws a card when the permanent dies, both conditioned on `CastWith(Blitz)`, and an enters trigger that sets a delayed trigger sacrificing the permanent at the next end step; the compiler reads the keyword line and its reminder text. `Prowl(cost)` is a way to cast a card from a hand (`CastWay.Prowl`, mana only) offered on a turn in which a creature of its caster dealt combat damage to a player and shared a creature type with the card (a changeling shares with any creature that has a type); it adds no ability, and "if its prowl cost was paid" is `CastWith(Prowl)`. The compiler reads the keyword line, "When ~ enters, if its prowl cost was paid, ..." and "If this spell's prowl cost was paid, ..." after the spell's effects. `Mayhem(cost)` is a way to cast a card from its owner's graveyard in the turn they discarded it (`CastWay.Mayhem`, mana only; the card keeps its timing); it adds no ability, and "if its mayhem cost was paid" is `CastWith(Mayhem)`, read like the prowl sentences. The compiler reads the keyword line, a hybrid cost included; Sadistic Slash, Electro's Bolt, Swarm, Being of Bees, Spider-Islanders, The Squadron Sinister, Green Goblin, Back for More, Raging Goblinoids and Abomination, World Ravager are Verified. |
| Afterlife, horsemanship, spectacle | `Afterlife(n)` adds a dies trigger (`ZoneChange` of the source from the battlefield to a graveyard) that creates `n` 1/1 white and black Spirit creature tokens with flying for its controller. `Horsemanship` is the hook `restricts_blocking`: a blocker is refused unless it has horsemanship, so no other keyword answers it. `Spectacle(cost)` is a way to cast a card from a hand (`CastWay.Spectacle`, mana only) offered in a turn in which an opponent of the caster lost life (the per-turn `Tally.Lost`: a loss, a payment or damage that takes life; infect damage does not); it adds no ability, and "if its spectacle cost was paid" is `CastWith(Spectacle)`, read like the prowl sentences. The compiler reads the three keyword lines, `Other Knights you control have horsemanship` as a granted keyword and, in a keyword line, `protection from white and from blue` as one `Protection` for each quality (a proposal pairs the run of keywords with that one clause), `protection from multicolored` and `protection from mana value N or less / greater` (a `Compare` of the mana value), also after an ability word (`Void Shields — Protection from ...`). Light Up the Stage, Skewer the Critics, Rafter Demon, Knight of the Last Breath, Lu Xun, Sphinx of the Steel Wind, Mistmeadow Skulk, Reaver Titan and Stonecoil Serpent are among the Verified. |
| Graft, afflict | `Graft(n)` returns an `Enters` replacement `WithCounters` of n +1/+1 counters and a trigger on `ZoneChange` of another creature to the battlefield (any seat's) with the intervening "if" of a +1/+1 counter on `This`, whose effect is a `May` `MoveCounters` of one counter from `This` onto `Triggering`; each instance is its own pair. `Afflict(n)` returns a `BecomesBlocked` trigger of `This` that makes `Ref.Defending` lose n life. `TurnTally::LifeLost` is the life the players `who` names lost this turn, added up (`Tally.Lost`; "for each 1 life your opponents have lost this turn" is `AddMana` with `times: Tallied { who: Opponents, what: LifeLost }`). Both keywords are in `Caps`, and the compiler reads the keyword lines. Aquastrand Spider, Llanowar Reborn, Plaxcaster Frogling (compiled) and Neheb, the Eternal (hand-written: the compiler reads neither "each of your postcombat main phases" nor the life-lost count) are Verified. |
| Replicate | `Replicate(cost)` is an additional cost paid any number of times (`OptionalKind::Kicker`, so each payment is a kick: `Amount::Kicks`, `Condition::Kicked`) and a cast trigger from the stack, with the intervening "if" `Kicked`, whose effect is `CopySpell` with `count: Kicks` and new targets; the copies are not cast and do not replicate. A cost with a part (`Replicate—Tap an untapped Dalek you control.`) asks its pick again for each payment and bounds the payments by the objects there are. The compiler reads the keyword line, the cost in words included; Exterminate! is Verified. |
| Read ahead | `ReadAhead` adds an `Enters` replacement `Choose(EntersChoice::Chapter)`: as the Saga enters, its controller picks a chapter from one to its last (`ChooseOption`, "Chapter N"), the lore counter it enters with becomes that many and the permanent keeps `Chosen::Chapter(n)`, so the trigger detection skips the chapters below it then and later (CR 702.155). The compiler reads the keyword line with its reminder text, "You and target opponent each draw two cards." (your `Draw`, then the other player's of the same count; also "target player" and "each opponent") and "Put a +1/+1 counter on each of up to two target creatures." (one `PutCounters` on the several targets). Love Song of Night and Day and Rishkar, Peema Renegade are Verified. |
| Exert | `Exert { once_per_turn }` (CR 701.43): "You may exert ~ as it attacks", and with `once_per_turn` "If ~ hasn't been exerted this turn, you may exert it as it attacks"; "When you do" after it is `Trigger.Exerted` of `This`, and "Whenever you exert a creature" `Exerted` of the creatures its controller controls. The engine runs both (engine.md, Exert); "it can't be blocked this turn and you scry 1" and "untap all other creatures you control and after this phase, there is an additional combat phase" read as two sentences joined with "and" (`compile/read/w11a_joins.rs`). |
| Renown, exploit | `Renown(n)` adds a combat damage trigger that, unless the creature is renowned (`Pred.Renowned`), runs `Effect::Renown{count}`: the counters and the renowned designation; "when this creature becomes renowned" is `Trigger.BecomesRenowned`. `Exploit` adds an enters trigger `May` of `Effect::Exploit`, the sacrifice of a creature the controller controls; "when this creature exploits a creature" / "whenever a creature you control exploits a creature" is `Trigger.Exploits`, judged as the battlefield was before the sacrifice. The compiler reads the keyword lines, both triggers and "have exploit" in a grant (`engine.md`, Renown and Exploit). |
| Hooks | `restricts_blocking`, `restricts_blocked`, `answers_evasion`, `min_blockers`, `forbids_attacking`, `ignores_summoning_sickness`, `attacks_untapped`, `is_indestructible`, `modifies_damage`, `restricts_targeting`, `restricts_player_targeting`, `protects_from`, `grants_timing`, `adds_abilities`. Combat, damage, state-based actions, targeting (`protects` and `protects_player`, part of `legal_targets`) and casting at instant speed (`grants_instant_timing`) call theirs; a stub answers "no". A keyword expressible in the DSL (ward, prowess, battle cry, equip, reconfigure, living weapon) returns abilities from `adds_abilities`, activated, triggered and static ones, which `ability_reader` lists after the keyword under `AbilitySource::PrintedKeyword` or `GrantedKeyword`, and is tested like any card; one that bends rules machinery (flying, first strike, trample) is a hook in combat or damage code. |

## Validation

Errors name the file and the path inside it; all are collected.

| Level | Checks | Runs in |
|---|---|---|
| 1 Parse | Syntax, types, unknown fields, unknown variants (a keyword typo reads `cards/serra_angel.json: faces[0].abilities[1].Keyword: unknown variant ...`) | `Library::from_sources`, `load_dir` |
| 2 Structure | File name is the slug of the name; oracle id is a UUID; unique ids and names; token id is its file name; face names unique and joined by ` // ` to the name; `impl_status` fits the abilities; a fingerprint on every face of a `Full` or `Partial` card; clause spans non-empty and disjoint; `Target(i)` in range and used; `Bound` bound earlier on the path (not visible after its branch, no duplicates); `Triggering` only in triggers; `Divided` and `EvenShare` only with a divided or multi-target spec; `TargetCount` in range; `Kicked` and `Kicks` only with a kicker keyword; `Performed` only in the condition of an `If` that follows another effect of its list; devotion to at least one colour; a choice of mana lists two types or more and a mana count is not zero; a `GroupAttacks` counts one creature or more; `RemoveCounters` is paid from the battlefield; `Modal` the only effect of its ability; `CreateToken::Named` exists; reference kinds fit (`Draw.who` is a player); bounds minimum not above maximum; a rule names players (`CantGainLife`, `CantDraw`, `CantSearch`, `RevealTop`, `LookAtTopAnyTime`, `NoMaxHandSize`, `SkipDrawStep`, `AdditionalLandPlays`, the last for at least one land) or objects (`MustAttack`, `MustBlock`, `CantBeCountered`), the `cause` of `TriggerAgain` has no target, `Triggering` or bound name, `from: Stack` is only for `CantBeCountered` of `This`, and `CanBeCommander` is written without `from`; `Players` and `LowestLife` name players; `Crew` has a power of at least one; an activated ability works from the battlefield, a hand, a graveyard, exile or the command zone, and a cost that needs its source in a place is paid from there (`Tap`, `Untap` and `SacrificeSelf` on the battlefield, `DiscardSelf` in a hand); a static ability from another zone has `affected` of the form `Each`; a state is the whole `when` of a triggered ability (not part of an `AnyOf`, a delayed trigger or the cause of a rule) and neither it nor what the ability does names `Triggering` | `Library::from_sources`, `load_dir` |
| 3 Printed | Oracle id, name, face count and names; fingerprints (drift); Scryfall keywords of ours present on some face, as an ability (a `Partner with` card has `Partner`), as a grant of some ability (an `AddKeyword`, the keyword of a token or of a granted ability: Scryfall lists `First strike` for "Enchanted creature has flying, first strike, and vigilance"), or ending a word in the text a `Manual` clause covers (`Plainscycling` names `Cycling`, `Mountainwalk` names `Landwalk`, `Flashback` does not name `Flash`); a listed keyword that no face, grant or clause has stays an error; `X` against the printed cost (an ability whose own cost has X is exempt); `Spell` only on instants and sorceries and required on a `Full` one; `Loyalty` only on planeswalkers; `Enchant` only on Auras; spans inside the text; card names in `Pred::Named` and `PartnerWith` exist | `Library::check_printed(&[PrintedCard])` |

## Compiler and Manual fallback

- The oracle-text compiler writes `Card`s with `mtg_cards::load::canonical`, validated by the same functions, with the fingerprint of the text it read.
- `compile::compile(&PrintedCard) -> Compiled { card, clauses }` is pure: reminder text and the card's own name (`~`) are stripped with a map back to the original text, the text splits into clauses (one per line, but "This land enters tapped. As it enters, choose a color." is two; a keyword list one per keyword; modes, level blocks and ability words stay whole), each clause has a `ClauseKind` (keyword, spell, triggered, activated, loyalty, modal, static), and `clauses` lists every clause, read or not, for the coverage report. The spell lines of an instant or sorcery are one `Spell` ability that reads whole or not at all; a line that reads on its own beside one that does not has `held_by_others`, and the report leaves it out of the blockers. A loyalty line (`+N`, `−N`, `0`, `−X`, a colon and the sentences of a spell) is a `Loyalty` ability on a planeswalker face only; the X of `−X` is the number of counters removed.
- "That much" and "that many" name the amount of an event (`Amount::Triggering`), so only a triggered ability reads them: in a spell or an activated ability ("Target opponent loses 3 life. You gain that much life.") they stay with the table, as no amount stands for what an earlier effect did. The X of a stat in the filter of a cost ("sacrifice an artifact or creature with mana value X") is the value of the permanent the cost takes, not an X the caster announces, so such a cost, and the X in the effects that follow it, stay with the table.
- Keyword lines are read into `Ability::Keyword` when the DSL declares the keyword and its parameter reads (`Enchant` on an Aura only; the filter after "Enchant" is an object phrase of the selector reader: "creature you control", "creature an opponent controls", "permanent", "nonland permanent", "artifact or land", "creature, land, or planeswalker", "Forest", and "player" and "opponent" are `EnchantPlayer`; a zone, "noncommander" and "with another Aura attached" stay with the table); the card is `Full` when every clause is read, `Partial` when some are, `Manual` with no abilities when none is, and `card` is `None` when no face has rules text or the data cannot hold the card (over four faces, two faces with one name).
- Hand-written files win over generated ones for the same oracle id. Generated rules are `card_rules` rows (oracle id, JSON, status, fingerprint, compiler version); hand-written ones are repository files. `Library::from_sources` loads the files; `Library::with_generated(&[GeneratedRules])` adds the rows: a file wins, and a row that does not parse or fails the structure checks is left out and reported, so rules of another build never stop the rest.
- `generated::generate(printed, hand_written, names)` compiles a card among the `names` (`compile::compile_among`) and checks the result with the checks of a file (`Library::check_generated`: levels 1 and 2 as a file of its own next to the predefined tokens, then level 3 against the imported card): `Rules` (`Full` or `Partial`), `Nothing` (no rules text, nothing read, or a card the data cannot hold) or `Invalid` with the problems. `generated::input_fingerprint(printed)` digests what the compiler reads (names, costs, type lines, oracle text, characteristics, colors, keywords) and `generated::COMPILER_VERSION` digests the compiler's sources and the rules data types at build time; a row with another fingerprint or version is read again.
