- `mtg-cli compile [--all | --card <name>]` writes the rows (`data-sources.md`). A modal ability whose mode has no label offers the renderer's wording of the mode (`render_mode`, with `~` as the card's name) in `ChooseMode`.
- `compile/read/` holds the templates for mana, spell effects (draw, discard, mill, life, damage, destroy, exile, counter, tap, untap, regenerate, scry, until-end-of-turn pumps; one target per clause) and the library and counter effects below. A template reads a whole clause or nothing, `render_variants` writes the result back as English, and the clause counts as read only when one of those wordings is the clause's own, compared after whitespace, the closing period, `~` for "this creature", ", then" for a new sentence, "enters" for the older "enters the battlefield", "precombat" and "postcombat" main phase for "first" and "second", "it" and "them" for "that card" and "those cards" after "put" and "reveal", and without "of their choice". The effects of one player in a row are written both as separate sentences and as one compound sentence, so either wording of a card matches.
- An instant or sorcery whose lines are only keywords stays `Manual`, except one with cascade: it is `Full` with no `Spell` ability, cascades as it is cast and resolves with nothing to do (Into the Time Vortex, Throes of Chaos; the validator accepts it, `validate::w17a_casting_does_something`).
- The spell lines of an instant or sorcery are one `Spell` ability, read when every line reads; "it", "its controller" and "it can't be regenerated" refer to the clause's target. A line without a closing period that no template reads (`Mayhem {1}{B}`) is a keyword-like clause kept `Manual`; the effect lines around it still read as the `Spell`. "Choose one —" and its bullets are one `Effect::Modal` (also after the comma of a trigger): each mode is read by the effect templates with targets of its own and no label, and the `Modal` is the only effect of its ability; "choose two", "one or both", "up to" and Spree stay with the table.
- A triggered clause is an event, a comma, an optional intervening "if" on a life total, and sentences read by the effect templates; an ability word before the dash is flavor. The events are `enters`, `dies`, `leaves the battlefield` and `attacks` of the card or of an object phrase ("another creature you control"), `casts` and `sacrifices` by you, an opponent or a player, the beginning of a step, and several joined by "or" (`AnyOf`). The card and a second subject doing the same ("Whenever ~ or another Elf you control enters", "Whenever this creature or another creature dies or deals combat damage to a player", "Whenever ~ or equipped creature attacks") are an `AnyOf` of the card's event and the second subject's for each verb in turn, written with "Whenever" only; a second subject without "another" or "equipped", a count of one event ("for the first time each turn") and "enters from your graveyard" stay with the table. "You may" and one step is `Effect::May`. Beyond the spell templates it reads "return target card from your graveyard to your hand", "return a land you control to its owner's hand" (a `Choose` bound as `returned` and a `Move`), tokens (Treasure, Clue and Food, described creature tokens that may enter tapped, attacking or tapped and attacking, counted by a number, by the X of the cost, by "that many" or by "where X is the number of", created by you or by another player ("target opponent creates", "its controller creates"); "with flying", "with flying and vigilance" and "with flying, vigilance, and haste" are the token's keyword abilities, any of the plain keywords the readers know; keywords with a parameter and a predefined token with a keyword stay with the table) and "you win the game". Delayed triggers, damage, life and counter events and other conditions stay with the table.
- Tokens with an amount (`compile/read/create.rs`, tests in `tests/token_sizes.rs`): "an X/X black Horror creature token, where X is ..." is `TokenRef::Sized` (a characteristic of an object, a count, a life total, a spell's mana value, the creatures that died this turn); "a number of ... tokens equal to its power", "X tokens, where X is the number of +1/+1 counters on it" and "a token for each creature put into your graveyard from the battlefield this turn" are counts of the tokens; a static ability in quotes ("This token can't block.") is a token ability beside the activated and triggered ones; "They have ..." and "It has ..." as a sentence of their own give the same abilities as "with ...".
- A sentence that acts on the tokens just created (`compile/read/token_binds.rs`, written by `render/bound.rs`, tests in `tests/token_binds.rs`): the `CreateToken` binds them as `tokens` and the effect after it reads `Bound("tokens")`: "Put X +1/+1 counters on it, where X is ...", "Put a +1/+1 counter on each of them", "then attach this Equipment to it" (`Effect::Attach`), "That token gains haste until end of turn" and "Those tokens gain haste ..." (`Effect::Continuous`) with "and attacks this combat if able" (`GameRule::MustAttack`). Two kinds of tokens in a row bind nothing. An intervening "if you control ..." (`Condition::Compare` over a count, as a static reads it: "if you control your commander") guards a trigger.
- One subject with several verb phrases ("target opponent sacrifices a creature, discards a card, and loses 3 life") is the effects of its sentence in order, "you create a 2/2 green Wolf creature token and that player mills ten cards" adds the player of the event as a second subject after "and", and a later sentence has its own subject; "unless ... pays {M}" after a sentence is an `Effect::Pay` whose unpaid effects are that sentence, for the controller of a countered spell ("its controller") and for the caster after a spell-cast event ("that player"). The effects "exile ~", "return target creature to its owner's hand", "all creatures get -N/-N until end of turn" and "put two cards from your hand on top of your library in any order" (a `Choose` bound as `back` and a `Move`) are read.
- An activated clause is a cost, a colon, sentences read by the effect templates and the restrictions on when it may be activated (`compile/read/costs.rs`, `abilities.rs`). The cost parts are read and written in the printed order: `{T}`, `{Q}`, a run of mana symbols, "Pay N life", "Sacrifice ~", "Sacrifice a creature" / "another creature" / "two artifacts" / "a Cleric creature" (a kind only, never who controls it; a subtype before a type word in the singular is a qualifier, as in the plural), a run "Sacrifice a Swamp and a Forest" (one `Sacrifice` part for each permanent, written back as one clause) and "Discard a card" / "Discard a land card" / "Discard two creature cards" (a `Discard` with a filter, the noun must be "card"); an `{X}` in the cost lets the effects use X. Mana ("Add {G}{G}", "Add one mana of any color") is an effect like any other, so it also reads in a spell and a trigger. "Activate only as a sorcery", "Activate only once each turn" and both joined by "and only" set `timing` and `limit`. "Activate only if", costs that remove counters, tap other permanents or pay energy, and abilities from another zone stay with the table. A mana ability with other effects ("{T}: Add {W} or {U}. ~ deals 1 damage to you.") is read whole, as the rules play it without the stack.

- "Spend this mana only to cast creature spells." after the sentence that adds mana is its restriction (`only`): one use per sentence, "cast" a spell or spells of a card type, two joined by "or", "noncreature", "multicolored" or "legendary" (or any spell), "activate abilities", or "on costs that contain {X}". The renderer writes the singular and the plural of "spell"; "of the chosen type" is the creature type the source chose as it entered (`SpendFilter.chosen_type`; the engine puts the type in `subtypes` when the mana is added, and the mana is none while no type is chosen), alone for a spell or with "or activate an ability of a creature source of the chosen type"; a subtype or a list of them ("Vampire, Cleric, and/or Demon spells", `SpendFilter.subtypes`) and a card type or a list of them ("instant and/or sorcery spells") are read for a spell, and the abilities of the same kind follow in any of the wordings the cards use ("or activate abilities of Dragons", "of Elemental sources", "or activate an ability of a Dinosaur source", "abilities of artifacts"); the facts about a spell are read after its noun in any order: "with mana value 5 or greater" (`min_value`), "with {X} in their mana costs", "you don't own" and "from your graveyard" (`facts`: `XInCost`, `NotOwned`, `FromGraveyard`), and a second kind of spell follows "or" ("or a legendary creature spell", "or spells with {X} in their mana costs"). Any other two uses stay with the table. "Add X mana in any combination of colors" (or of two or three listed types) counts X as "Remove X storage counters from this land" (`CostItem::RemoveCounters` of `Amount::X`) or ", where X is ..." names it (`Produce::AnyCombination.count` is an `Amount`; `mana_shape` requires the X of the mana and of the cost to go together). An ability word before an activated ability is left out when it is Ferocious (`ACTIVATED_WORDS`); a quoted mana ability is given by a static ability like any quoted activated ability ("Enchanted land has "{T}: Add one mana of any color."").

- A keyword counter ("with a lifelink counter on it", "Put a reach counter on each of them", also first strike and double strike) is a `CounterKind::Other` of the keyword's name that the layers turn into the keyword (`engine.md`, Continuous effects); the counters the rules give another effect (shield, stun, finality, exalted, decayed, lore, defense) stay with the table. "the amount of life you gained this turn" is the tally `Tallied{You, LifeGained}`.
- A search for "up to X basic land cards, where X is the greatest power among creatures you control" names its count after the cards: the search reads the where clause and its amount is the `max` of the count. The put follows in the same sentence or as the sentence "Put those cards onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle." (a variant of the writer).
- A search is "Search your library for" a filter, an optional ", reveal it", the move of what is found and ", then shuffle" (`Search`, `Move`, `Shuffle`; one sentence on the card), also after "you may" (an `Effect::May` of the three) and as "then shuffle and put that card on top". Another player searches their own library after a removal: "Its controller may search their library for a basic land card, put it onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle" is a `May` of the controller of the target holding their `Search`, the move and their `Shuffle`. A search for two cards is two searches in a row, the second leaving out the first card (`Not(Is(Bound))`), a move for each and one shuffle: "a Forest card and a Plains card", and "up to two basic land cards that share a land type" (each search finds up to one; the second adds `SharesSubtype` of the first, bound as `land2`). The filter is adjectives, one or more subtypes joined by "or", card types joined by "or" (or "permanent"), "card" and "with mana value N or less" (also "X or less" when the mana cost has an `{X}`, a `Compare` against `X` that the search reads with the X the spell or ability announced); the count is "a", "up to N" or "any number of", found as `land` for a land filter and `card` otherwise. "Look at the top N cards of your library" is `LookAtTop` bound as `top`; with "Put one of them into your hand and the rest on the bottom of your library in any order" (or "in a random order", or "into your graveyard") it adds `Choose` (`kept`, `rest`) and two `Move`s. "Shuffle your library", "target player shuffles their library" and "Put target card from your graveyard on the bottom of your library" (also "on top of", and a permanent "of its owner's library") are read. Names, other zones and other orders stay with the table.
- Counters are put (`+1/+1`, `-1/-1` and a named kind such as charge, poison or time), removed and moved by number words on the card, on one target or, for putting, on "each creature you control": "Remove a +1/+1 counter from ~", "Move a +1/+1 counter from target creature onto ~" (one of the two is the card). All, X, a second target and counters as a cost stay with the table, as do the counters whose effect the rules give and not the card (keyword counters, stun, shield, finality, lore, defense) and any clause whose reminder text speaks of a counter: the data holds a counter as a name, and the reminder text that defines it is dropped.
- A counter of a named kind removed from a permanent the payer controls is a cost part (`compile/read/costs.rs`, tests in `tests/c11c_counter_costs.rs`): "Remove a +1/+1 counter from a creature you control" (also "another creature", "a permanent") is `RemoveCounters` with `from`, the kinds of permanent; the engine asks `ChooseCards` among those that hold such a counter. A count above one and "from among" stay with the table. "if it had counters on it" (also "one or more", and of one kind) after a leave or dies event is a `Compare` of `AnyCounters` (or `Counters`) of `Triggering`, read from its last known information (`compile/read/c11c_had_counters.rs`).
- Exert (`compile/read/w9d_exert.rs`, tests in `tests/w9d_exert.rs`): a line "You may exert ~ as it attacks. When you do, ..." is two clauses (`clauses.rs`): the first reads into `Keyword::Exert` (also "as he attacks" and the once-per-turn wording of Combat Celebrant), the second is a triggered clause whose event "When you do" is `Exerted` of `This` and whose "it" is the card. "Whenever you exert a creature" is `Exerted` of the creatures you control. Exert as a cost part stays with the table.
- Copies and unblocked attackers as events (`compile/read/triggers.rs`, writer `w2e_copied.rs`, tests in `tests/w2e_trigger_events.rs`): "Whenever you copy a spell" (also "an instant spell", "an opponent copies") is `SpellCopied`, "Whenever you cast or copy an instant or sorcery spell" (magecraft) an `AnyOf` of `SpellCast` and `SpellCopied` of the same spell and player, and "Whenever ~ attacks and isn't blocked" (any subject) is `Unblocked`, whose "it" is the attacker. "attacks a player and isn't blocked" and "isn't blocked this combat" stay with the table.
- A counter of any kind (`compile/read/w2f_any_counters.rs`, tests in `tests/w2f_any_counters.rs`) is `RemoveCounters` without `counter`: as a cost from a permanent the payer controls ("Remove a counter from a creature you control"), the engine asks `ChooseCards`, then `ChooseOption` for the kind when the permanent holds several (`announce/w2f_counter_kinds.rs`, kept in `CostTaken.kinds`); as an effect ("Remove three counters from target permanent"), the controller picks each counter with `ChooseOption` (`resolve/prim/w2f_any_counters.rs`). "Remove up to N" sets `up_to`, which adds "Stop" to each question, for a named kind too. A counter of any kind from the source as a cost and X counters stay with the table.
- "It" in a trigger about a creature (`compile/read/pronouns.rs`, tests in `tests/pump_wordings.rs`): when the event is the card attacking, blocking, becoming blocked or entering, "it" in the effects is the card, and for another creature that attacks, becomes blocked or enters it is the creature of the event (`Ref::Triggering`); another blocker, damage and every other event have no "it". "Whenever ~ attacks, it gets +1/+1 until end of turn", "Whenever a creature you control attacks, it gets +2/+2 until end of turn" and "put a +1/+1 counter on it" are read, and the writer says "~" and "it" for the card; a sentence after "untap ~" may open with "It gains ...". "gains protection from the color of your choice until end of turn" (also after "gets +N/+N and") is `AddProtectionFromChosenColor` in a spell, an activation or a trigger, never in a static. "Whenever one or more +1/+1 counters are put on <subject>" (or "one or more counters") is a `Batch` of `CountersPut`; a noun "with a +1/+1 counter on it" is `HasCounter`, and "Each creature you control with a +1/+1 counter on it has trample." a static of that group, written as "Each ..." or as the plural; a pump of a group counted by the sentence before may say "Those creatures". "creatures with +1/+1 counters on them", "you gain protection" and "put a +1/+1 counter on it" after damage (the cause holds the damaged player) stay with the table.
- "Surveil N", "Proliferate" and "you become the monarch" are effects; "if you're the monarch", "if an opponent is the monarch" and "if there is no monarch" (`Not` of `Monarch(EachPlayer)`) are intervening conditions (`Condition::Monarch`), and the same three after or before a static ("Creatures you control get +1/+1 as long as you're the monarch.", "As long as you're the monarch, permanents you control have hexproof.") are its `condition`; "Whenever you become the monarch" (also "an opponent", "a player") is `BecomesMonarch` with "that player" the new monarch; the monarch is `Ref::Monarch`: "the monarch's end step", "attacks the monarch" (`AttacksPlayer`, where "that player controls" is the monarch's) and "creatures the monarch controls" (`ControlledBy(Monarch)`); "Add {W} or {U}", "Add {B}, {R}, or {G}", "Add {W}{W}, {W}{U}, or {U}{U}" and "Add one mana of the chosen color" and "Add {R} or one mana of the chosen color" are mana (`OneOf`, `OneOfLists`, `ChosenColor`, `OrChosenColor`); "Pay {E}" is a cost (`PayEnergy`) and "you get {E}{E}" an effect (`PutCounters` of the energy counter on you; its reminder text does not keep the clause from being read); an X in a cost counts the value announced ("Pay X life", "Remove X ki counters from ~", "Sacrifice X creatures", "Discard X cards", "Exile X cards from your graveyard", "Tap X untapped artifacts you control", "Pay X {E}") and the walker no longer names `Cost.<part>.Counted` for it, only for an amount that is neither fixed nor X; and "Whenever you gain life", "lose life", "draw a card", "discard a card" and "cycle a card" (by you, an opponent or a player) are events (`LifeGained`, `LifeLost`, `Drew`, `Discarded`, `Cycled`). "Your commander" before a verb is a commander its owner designated (`[Commander, OwnedBy(You)]`, written "When" or "Whenever"); "that player" after an attack at "one of your opponents" is the player attacked (`Ref::Defending`); "while you're the monarch" after an event other than damage is its intervening condition, and "if you were the monarch as the turn began" is `MonarchAtTurnStart`. A card that uses one is `Partial` or `Manual` until the engine runs it; the report names it (`Effect.Surveil`, `Effect.AddMana.ProducedBy`) instead of the unread wording.
- "You get an emblem with" and "target opponent gets an emblem with" a quoted ability (`compile/read/w6b_emblem.rs`, tests in `tests/w6b_emblem.rs`) read as `Effect::CreateEmblem`: one quoted static, triggered or activated ability, or two joined by "and", read as the ability of a permanent, with "this emblem" as `~`; the renderer writes each ability in its quotes.
- Counting wordings of the mana permanents: an intervening "if ~ has no charge counters on it" is a `Compare` of the counters on the card with zero, "it" after it is the card ("return it to its owner's hand" is a `Move` of `This` to the hand), "for each of that spell's colors" counts `Stat { of: Triggering, stat: Colors }`, and "Exile a card from your hand" is a cost part (`ExileFromHand`); Mana Bloom, Ramos and Cadaverous Bloom are read whole.
- Single-blocker wordings (`compile/read/tests/tail_pairs.rs`): "that player loses half their life, rounded up" (or "down"; "your" for you) is `LoseLife` of `Half` of the `Life` of that player; "return it to its owner's hand at the beginning of the next end step" chooses the card (the card that died for a dies trigger), bound as `later`, and sets up a delayed trigger that returns it; "each player discards their hand, then draws cards equal to the greatest number of cards a player discarded this way" binds the discarded cards as `discarded` and draws the `Largest` count of them among the players; "Target player exiles a card from their graveyard" is that player's `Choose` from their graveyard and an `Exile` of the choice.
- Single-blocker wordings, third round (`compile/read/tests/tail_third.rs`): "you may have that player lose N life" for the controller of a creature that entered is a `May` of `LoseLife` over `ControllerOf(Triggering)`; "You may cast a spell with mana value N or less from your hand without paying its mana cost." is a `Choose` of at most one nonland card of the hand and a free `CastFromZone` of it; "That creature gains shroud until end of turn." follows "attach it to target creature you control"; "return this card from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped" works from the graveyard at a step; "return it to its owner's hand [at the beginning of the next end step]" after another creature that died chooses that card from the graveyard (`Pred::SameCard(Triggering)`); "Target creature you control deals damage equal to its power to target creature an opponent controls." is a `DealDamage` from the first target (a first target that is no longer legal deals none); "creature dealt damage by this creature this turn" is `Pred::DamagedBy(This)`; "Whenever a Vampire you control deals combat damage to a player, put a +1/+1 counter on it" counts on `Ref::Dealer`; "As this land enters, you may reveal an Elemental card from your hand. If you don't, this land enters tapped." is `RevealOrTapped`. Baral's and Rishkar's Expertise, Rienne, Predator Ooze, Vampiric Dragon, Blood Seeker, Suture Priest, Silver Shroud Costume, Silversmote Ghoul, Super Combo, It's Clobberin' Time!, Rakish Heir, Stensia Masquerade, Shaman of the Great Hunt and eight lands that reveal a card are Verified.
- The card of another creature that died (`compile/read/c6_au_dead.rs`, tests in `tests/c6_au_dead.rs`): a new object in the graveyard (CR 400.7), named `Each(In(Graveyard), SameCard(Triggering))`. "Exile it" exiles it, also joined to the next sentence by "and put" or "and you create" (Patron of the Vein, Curse of Clinging Webs), and "return it (that card) to the battlefield under your control" moves it there under `You` (Changing Loyalty, Fool's Demise).
- A counter of an ability on the stack (`compile/read/c8_kwq_abilities.rs`, tests in `tests/c8_kwq_counters.rs`, CR 701.6b): "counter target spell, activated ability, or triggered ability", "target activated or triggered ability", "target activated ability" and "target triggered ability" are the one target of a spell, a triggered or an activated ability (`AnyOf` of `Spell` and `Ability` kinds), written back by the target phrase (Overcharged Amalgam).
- The templates of the events (`compile/read/triggers.rs`): "whenever ~ deals [combat] damage [to a player, an opponent]", "whenever you tap a land for mana", "whenever ~ becomes tapped, untapped, blocked, attached to a creature", "whenever a creature blocks", "whenever a +1/+1 counter is put on ~ / removed from ~", "whenever you cycle another card" and "when you cycle this card" (an ability that works from the graveyard, `from: Graveyard`). In the effects, "that player" is the player of the event (`Triggering`) and "that permanent's controller" the controller of the permanent of a tap or an untap. Also read: "equipped creature" or "enchanted creature" as the subject (`Is(Attached)`, written back in both ways), "one or more creatures you control deal combat damage to a player", "one or more cards are put into your graveyard from your library" (`Batch`), "one or more creatures you control attack" (`GroupAttacks`), "you draw your second card each turn" and "you cast your first spell each turn" (`Nth`, first to fifth), "~ becomes the target of a spell or ability [you control / an opponent controls]", "~ is dealt damage" (`DealsDamage` into the object) and, after "attacks", "becomes the target of a spell or ability you control" or "is dealt damage", "for the first time each turn" (`Nth` of the object, first to fifth; a count of targets by an opponent's spells stays unread). "It" is the card itself in the effects of a trigger on the card becoming a target ("put a +1/+1 counter on it"). And "that much" and "that many" for life, damage, cards and counters (`Amount::Triggering`). "For the first time each turn" (first to fifth) counts the events of a player after "you gain life", "you lose life" or "an opponent loses life", "you cycle another card" (not the card itself), "you surveil" and "you create one or more tokens" (first only), and of an object after "one or more counters are put on ~" (any kind), written back the same way. "Is put into a graveyard from anywhere other than the battlefield" and, with "your graveyard", the same for the cards its owner owns, is a `ZoneChangeExcept`; a subject with the noun "card" (Disa the Restless, Syr Konrad) stays unread. The condition "a counter was put on ~ this turn" (`facts.rs`) is read after "if".
- A noun is followed by who controls it or a number it has ("with power 2 or less", "with mana value X" when the mana cost has an X): `Pred::Compare` with a fixed number or `X`. "It deals 1 damage to any target" opens an activated ability for the source, and only as the first thing it does.
- "Whenever a creature attacks enchanted player" is `AttacksPlayer` of `AttachedPlayer`: "it" is the attacker (`Triggering`) and "its controller" the attacking player. A group "creatures enchanted player controls" is `Each` with `ControlledBy(AttachedPlayer)` in a static ability or a rule.
- "Whenever a creature attacks you or a planeswalker you control" is `AttacksPlayerOrPlaneswalker` of `You`: the attacker is "it" and "that creature", and its controller "that creature's controller".
- Block restrictions and granted mana (`compile/read/evasion.rs`, `selectors.rs`, tests in `tests/evasion.rs`, `tests/granted_mana.rs`): "with power less than this creature's power" and "with greater power" are a `Pred::Compare` of the stat against `Amount::Stat{of: This}` (the source of the static ability); "Creatures with power less than this creature's power can't block it" (also "... can't block creatures you control") and "~ can't be blocked by creatures with greater power" are `CantBeBlockedBy` of that selector, written in each wording. "Can't be blocked except by creatures with flying" (also "artifact creatures", "Slivers") is `CantBeBlockedBy` of the negated quality (`Not`, or `AnyOf` of `Not` for several; every blocker is a creature, so the type is dropped). A mana ability in quotes after "has" or "have" is a `GrantAbility` of the activated mana ability, for a group, a subtype or what an Aura is attached to.
- Limits on blockers and "your commander" (`compile/read/evasion.rs`, `statics.rs`, tests in `tests/block_limits.rs`, `tests/commander_statics.rs`): "~ can block only creatures with flying." (also "Enchanted creature", a group) is `CanBlockOnly` of the attackers it names; "~ can't be blocked by more than one creature." is `MaxBlockers(1)` and "can't be blocked except by two or more creatures" `MinBlockers(2)`, for the card, what an Aura or Equipment is attached to or a group ("Each creature you control with power 4 or greater ..."), and `MaxBlockers(1)` also ends a list of what a creature gets and has ("Equipped creature gets +1/+2, has reach, and can't be blocked by more than one creature."). "melee" and "myriad" are keywords a static ability can give ("Other creatures you control have melee."). "you control your commander" is a count of the commander cards the player owns and controls (`Pred::Commander` with `OwnedBy(You)`), so "As long as you control your commander, ..." is the `condition` of a static, and a Lieutenant ability word is flavor.
- Player keywords, quoted triggers and paired statics (`compile/read/player_keywords.rs`, `static_pairs.rs`, `statics.rs`, tests in `tests/player_keywords.rs`, `tests/granted_triggers.rs`, `tests/static_pairs.rs`): "You have hexproof." and "You have shroud." (also both, and under a leading "As long as ...") are a `Static` of `You` with `AddKeyword`; a triggered ability in quotes after "has" or "have" is a `GrantAbility` like the activated ones (the writer gives the inner ability in each wording, so "it deals" and "~ deals" both read); "creature defending player controls" is `ControlledBy(Defending)`; "As long as you control your commander, ~ gets +2/+2 and creatures you control have vigilance." is two `Static`s with the same condition, one clause that reads into two abilities as a level block does (`compile::read::static_pair`).
- A static clause is one sentence read by `compile/read/statics.rs`. How the card enters is a `Replacement` of `Enters`: "~ enters tapped", with "unless you control" a count (`Not` of a `Compare` on a `Count`: "two or more basic lands", "two or fewer other lands", "a planeswalker", "a Forest or a Plains", "a Mount or Vehicle"; "your opponents control eight or more lands"), "~ enters with three +1/+1 counters on it" (X when the mana cost has one; "~ enters tapped with two charge counters on it" is the same replacement with `tapped`), "As ~ enters" (or "As it enters"), "choose a color", "a color other than red" or "a creature type" and "As ~ enters, you may pay N life. If you don't, it enters tapped". Rules are "~ can't be countered" (from the stack), "~ attacks each combat if able", "All creatures attack each combat if able" and "Play with the top card of your library revealed". A `Static` is "~ can't block" (or attack, or both), and "gets +N/+N" and "has" or "have" keywords (`and`-joined) for "Equipped creature" or "Enchanted creature" (both `Attached`, so the renderer writes both wordings), "Creatures you control", "Other creatures you control", "All creatures" and "... of the chosen type" or "color"; "other" stands after the other adjectives, where the hand-written files put it. A `CostModifier` is "Noncreature spells cost {1} more to cast.", "Artifact spells you cast cost {1} less to cast.", "Spells your opponents cast cost {1} more to cast." and "This spell costs {1} more to cast for each target beyond the first." (read only beside a spell ability that has targets, else the table keeps it); "for each" a counted group changes the cost by its mana once for each thing counted ("This spell costs {1} less to cast for each creature card in your graveyard.", "{2} less ... for each land you control", "Creature spells you cast cost {1} less to cast for each +1/+1 counter on ~."); "This spell costs {X} less to cast, where X is the total power of creatures you control." (also "the greatest toughness among", "the total mana value of Dragons you control") is one mana changed as many times as the number says; and "Spells you cast from anywhere other than your hand cost {2} less to cast." selects `Not(CastFrom(Hand))` (`Pred::CastFrom`, a hand or a graveyard). A count of a party, of a quantity of this turn, two groups joined by "and", a "the number of" after an X and a condition stay with the table. A group of permanents that "enter tapped" ("Creatures your opponents control enter tapped.", "Artifacts and creatures your opponents control enter tapped.") is a `Replacement` of `Enters` with the group as `what`. A condition after "as long as" (a `Static` with `condition`: "an opponent has 10 or less life", "a player has 13 or less life", "you control a Plains", "it's your turn") lets the card itself get a bonus or a keyword ("This creature has haste as long as an opponent has 10 or less life"); without it a card is given nothing; a restriction ("can't be blocked", "can't block") may stand under one, and "isn't a creature" is read only under one (`RemoveType`). A keyword list also holds protections: "has protection from white and from black" is one `Protection` for each quality (a colour, "colorless", a plural card type or "everything"), also after "gains" in a pump. A line of two sentences or needing two replacements ("enters tapped with two charge counters on it"), a condition other than those ("for each", "if you attacked"), an X without an X in the mana cost, counters the rules define, the number of opponents and a player's life stay with the table.
- Conditions after "as long as" (`compile/read/as_long_as.rs`, tests in `tests/as_long_as.rs`) are one `Condition` of a `Static`, before the subject or after the predicate, and "it" names the card or its host: the state of the card or its host ("~ is untapped", "it's attacking", "equipped creature is legendary", a color), counters on the card ("has four or more +1/+1 counters on it", "has five or more quest counters on it", "has a divinity counter on it"; charge and level counters belong to `stations.rs` and `levels.rs`; with them "it's an artifact creature" is the predicate of a Vehicle), cards in the controller's graveyard ("there are seven or more cards in your graveyard", "fewer than eight", "four or more card types among cards in your graveyard" as `CardTypes`) or hand ("you have no cards in hand", "seven or more", "one or fewer"), devotion ("your devotion to green and white is less than seven" as `Devotion` `Lt`), and "you control no other creatures", besides the life, turn, monarch and "you control" counts read before. A subtype group after a leading condition ("As long as ..., Soldiers you control ..."), a count in an opponent's zone, a typed card count ("creature cards in your graveyard") and "also" before "get" stay with the table.
- Counted mana (`compile/read/effects.rs`, tests in `tests/counted_mana.rs`): "Add X mana of any one color, where X is ..." is `AnyColor(X)` with the where clause bound like any other, "Add an amount of {G} equal to ..." is `Fixed` added `times` the amount, and "Add {G}. If you control four or more creatures, add {G}{G} instead." is `Instead { base, when, instead }`, whose condition is read as in "as long as" and chosen when the ability is offered or resolves.
- Triggered mana abilities (CR 605.1b; `compile/read/mana_trigger.rs`, `mana_trigger.rs` in `mtg-cards`, tests in `tests/triggered_mana.rs`): a `Triggered` ability on `TappedForMana` with no target, no limit and one `AddMana` for the player who tapped is a mana ability. "Whenever you tap a land for mana, add one mana of any type that land produced." is `Produce::OfTapped` for `You` (a tap by any player: "Whenever a player taps a land for mana, that player adds ...", `who: Triggering`); "Whenever you tap a Swamp for mana, add an additional {B}." is a fixed mana; "Whenever enchanted land is tapped for mana, its controller adds an additional {G}" (also `{G}{G}`, "one mana of any color", "one mana of the chosen color"; "enchanted Forest") is `TappedForMana` of `[Is(Attached), Type(Land)]`, `who: Triggering`.
- Tallies and named lands in readers (`compile/read/facts.rs`, `defining.rs`, `statics.rs`, tests in `tests/mana_leftovers.rs`): "for each card you've drawn this turn" and "the number of cards you've drawn this turn" are `Amount::Tallied{You, Drawn}`, "if you played a land this turn" is that tally `LandsPlayed` at least one, and "you control an Urza's Mine and an Urza's Power-Plant" ("and" joins kinds the same player controls, the verb said once) is `Condition::All` of a count each; the two names of an Urza land read as one subtype (`subtypes::is_two_word_land`).
- A choice of both modes for a commander (`compile/read/modal.rs`, `choices.rs`, tests in `tests/modes_both.rs`): "Choose one. If you control a commander as you cast this spell, you may choose both instead." before the bullets of a spell (a `Modal` clause by `classify`) and the same sentence without "as you cast this spell" in a trigger are the `all_if` condition of a pair of modes; a header with three modes, "choose two" or an unread mode stays unread. Jeska's Will and Akroma's Will are corpus cards.
- "Choose two —" (two of three or more modes) and "Choose one or both —" (one or two of a pair) are the `choose` bounds of a `Modal` in `compile/read/modal.rs` (tests in `tests/c9_fight_modes.rs`); a mode keeps its own targets, so "Target creature you control fights target creature you don't control" reads inside one. Two mode sentences read with them: "Prevent all damage target instant or sorcery spell would deal this turn." is a `Shield` whose `source` is `Is(Target(0))` (`prevention.rs`), and "Double target creature's power and toughness until end of turn." (or "power" alone) is a `Continuous` `ModifyPT` by the target's own `Stat`s (`c9_fight_double.rs`, CR 701.10d). Dromoka's Command, Brigid's Command, Epic Fight and Savage Stomp are Verified corpus cards (`mtg-testkit` `tests/it/c9_fight_modes.rs`).
- "Choose three. You may choose the same mode more than once." (two to five; a `Modal` clause by `classify`) sets `repeat` with the fixed bounds, "Choose one or more —" is one or any more, and "choose one that hasn't been chosen —" sets `once_ever` (`compile/read/modal.rs`, written back by `choices.rs`, tests in `tests/c11d_modes.rs`). A mode name may hold small words or capitals ("Hire a Mercenary", "AV Bead"; a run of capitals is a name even where it opens a sentence), and a mode may name its X at the end ("where X is the number of lands you control"). Wretched, Verdant and Cabaretti Confluence, Sublime Epiphany, Clan Defiance, Kimoyo Beads, Black Market Connections and Will of the Sultai are Verified corpus cards (`mtg-testkit` `tests/it/c11d_modes.rs`).
- Mana costs and counts in readers (`costs.rs`, `effects.rs`, `selectors.rs`, `flicker.rs`, tests in `tests/mana_leftovers.rs`): "Mill a card" is a cost (`CostItem::Mill`; the tokenizer takes "Mill" after a comma as a cost verb), "Target player adds one mana of any color." is `AddMana` for `Target(0)`, "for each card in target opponent's hand" counts `[In(Hand), OwnedBy(Target(0))]` (the writer says "player's" for a target player), "Exile ~ with three time counters on it." is an `Exile` that binds the card and `PutCounters` on the bound object, "equal to its power" after "this creature or equipped creature attacks" is the power of `Triggering`, and an ability word before an activated ability is flavor except `MEANINGFUL_WORDS` (Channel, Bloodrush, Boast, Exhaust, Forecast), which name a zone or a limit.
- "Whenever enchanted player is attacked" is `PlayerAttacked` and "whenever a player attacks enchanted player with one or more creatures" is `PlayerAttacks` (`by`, `at`). The first triggers once for a declaration with a creature attacking that player (an attack at only their planeswalkers is not one) and has the attacked player as `Triggering`; the second triggers once for each attacking player, who is `Triggering` ("that attacking player"). "Each opponent attacking that player does the same" repeats the effects with `ControllerOf` of `Each` over `AttackingPlayer(AttachedPlayer)` and `ControlledBy(Opponents)` as `who`; the reader repeats a draw, a gain or loss of life, a token or "untap all nonland permanents you control" (Curse of Bounty spells out the untapping). `AttackingPlayer` is judged when the effect runs, so a creature put onto the battlefield attacking counts. Gold is a predefined token.
- Counted amounts (`compile/read/amounts.rs`): "for each ..." counts a selector (`Count`: "creature you control", "Goblin on the battlefield", "card in your hand", "creature card in your graveyard"; a group no one holds stands on the battlefield), the opponents (`Players(Opponents)`) or the counters on the card (`Counters`); "N ... for each" is `Times{by: N, of}`, and a plain "a ... for each" is the count itself. It follows the amount of a draw, a mill, a gain or loss of life, the counters put, the tokens created ("a Goblin token for each Goblin you control", also written "X tokens, where X is the number of ..."), the mana added (`AddMana.times`) and the change of power and toughness of a pump or a static ("gets +1/+1 for each artifact you control", a change that is zero stays zero and both stats take the same factor). The card itself may get a counted bonus. A count held by a target ("creature target opponent controls"), another player's zone ("in their graveyard"), a quantity ("different power") and two verbs with one count stay unread.
- The number of (`compile/read/defining.rs`, `where_x.rs`, tests in `tests/number_of.rs`): "the number of creatures you control" (`Count`, also "on the battlefield", "in your graveyard", "in all graveyards", "the total number of cards in all players' hands"), "the number of opponents you have" (`Players`), "the number of +1/+1 counters on ~" (`Counters`), "the number of times this creature has mutated" (`Mutations` of `This`), "twice the number of ..." (`Sum` of two), "the number of card types among ...", "the greatest power among ..." and "the total power of ..." are one amount reader, shared with the characteristic-defining abilities. It is the amount of a draw, a mill, a gain or loss of life, damage, counters put and tokens created in two wordings: X named at the end of the sentence (", where X is the number of artifacts you control", also in a pump of power and toughness, a scry, the counters a permanent enters with and a static pump such as "Equipped creature gets +X/+X, where X is ...") and "equal to" after the verb ("draw cards equal to the number of opponents you have", "you gain life equal to ...", "deals damage equal to ... to any target" or "deals damage to target creature equal to ...", "put a number of +1/+1 counters on ~ equal to ...", "create a number of ... tokens equal to ..."). The writer words the amount as X, names the count after the sentence, and derives the "equal to" forms from the same text; a token count by counters or opponents also says "for each". A count held by a target or another player, a negative pump (-X/-X), mana of X, an X/X token, damage dealt by "it" for another creature, "difference", "greatest number ... a player" and counts of what happened this turn stay unread.
- Damage (`compile/read/damage.rs`): "~ deals 3 damage to any target" with one recipient (a target, "each opponent", "each player", "you", "each creature [without flying]", "each other creature"); one group of objects and one of players are two `DealDamage` ("each creature and each player"); "and you gain 3 life" is a `GainLife` after the damage; "it deals" stands for the card in a trigger on its own entering, attacking or dying and in an activation. "You draw a card and you lose 1 life" names the subject again. A run of mana symbols has no order: `{G}{W}` and `{W}{G}` are one cost (`same_text`).
- Cast and limit wordings (`compile/read/selectors.rs`, `abilities.rs`, `amounts.rs`, tests in `tests/cast_spell_kinds.rs`, `tests/trigger_limits.rs`): a spell of a trigger on a cast, or any phrase with the noun "spell", may be a subtype ("a Merfolk spell", "an Aura, Equipment, or Vehicle spell" as an `AnyOf` of subtypes, "a Vampire creature spell"), "permanent" (an `AnyOf` of the six permanent types), "multicolored" (`Compare` of `Colors` at least 2) or "historic" (an artifact, a legendary or a Saga); "This ability triggers only once each turn." after the effects of a triggered ability is `limit: OncePerTurn`, which the engine already keeps; "for each time you've cast your commander (or a commander) from the command zone this game" is `Amount::CommanderCasts(You)`, the casts of all the player's commanders together (a draw, life, a token count, a pump of a group and the copies of a spell cast). "When you cast this spell", the exceptions "isn't legendary" and "loses all other card types" and "when you next cast" stay with the table.
- Characteristic amounts (`compile/read/where_x.rs`, tests in `tests/where_x.rs`): a sentence that ends with ", where X is its power" (also "this creature's" or "~'s", "that creature's"; power, toughness or mana value) reads its X as that characteristic (`Amount::Stat`) in a draw, a life change, damage, a pump, counters put and tokens; "its" is the sentence's "it", its one target or the object of the trigger's event (the card for its own event and in an activation), "that creature's" the creature of another's event. "Create a number of 1/1 white Rabbit creature tokens equal to ~'s power" is the same count. The writer words one characteristic of an ability as X and names it at the end of the sentence that says X; "it deals" stands for a card that dies or leaves the battlefield. Mana of X and an X/X token stay with the table; a count after "where X is" is the number-of amount below.
- Attack tax (`compile/read/attack_tax.rs`): "Creatures can't attack you unless their controller pays {2} for each creature they control that's attacking you." (also "attacking a planeswalker you control", "for each of those creatures"), "... attack you or planeswalkers you control unless ..." and "... attack planeswalkers you control unless ..." are an `Ability::Rule` of `AttackTax` for `Player`, `Both` or `Planeswalkers`; a group before "can't attack" ("Nonblack creatures") is `attackers`, "{X} ..., where X is the number of enchantments you control" is `{1}` with `per` the count, and the same sentence after "Until your next turn," is the `Effect::Rule` of it. "pays 2 life for each of those creatures" is `life` with no mana (Sivitri). A tap or no cost stays with the table. Propaganda, Ghostly Prison, Windborn Muse, Sphere of Safety, Baird, Norn's Annex, Archon of Absolution and Forbidding Spirit are reproduced.
- Limits on a declaration (`compile/read/w8a_declaration_limits.rs`): "No more than one creature can attack each combat." and "No more than two creatures can block each combat." are an `Ability::Rule` of `AttackLimit` or `BlockLimit` with the count word, and "No more than two creatures can attack you each combat." and "... can attack ~ each combat." are `AttackLimitAt{most, at}` with `at` `You` or `This`; another defender, "each turn" and a noun that does not agree with the count stay with the table (tests in `tests/w10a_attack_limit_at.rs`). Silent Arbiter, Dueling Grounds, Caverns of Despair, Crawlspace and Judoon Enforcers are reproduced.
- Limits under a condition or for one combat (`compile/read/w14a_limits.rs`): "As long as C, no more than one creature can attack you each combat." is the `Ability::Rule` of the limit with the condition of `as_long_as`; "each opponent can't block with more than one creature this combat" and "defending player can't block with more than two creatures this combat" are an `Effect::Rule` of `BlockLimitEach{most, who}` until end of combat. "For each player, choose a creature that player controls. Each player sacrifices all creatures they control not chosen this way." (`w14a_wanderer.rs`) is a `RepeatEach` over the players binding `player`: a `Choose` by you of one creature that player controls with the rest bound, and the sacrifice of the rest by that player. The writer of an exile returned later says "at the beginning of that player's next end step" for a delayed trigger whose `whose` is `OwnerOf` the exiled card (tests in `tests/w14a_leftovers.rs`). Mirri, Weatherlight Duelist is reproduced; The Eternal Wanderer is a corpus card the checker accepts `Verified`.
- Attacks on you (`compile/read/w6a_attacks_you.rs`): "can't attack you", "can't attack you or planeswalkers you control" and "can't attack planeswalkers you control" are `CantAttackYou` with `Player`, `Both` or `Planeswalkers`, a restriction on the creatures a static ability names ("Creatures with flying ...", "Creatures can't attack you." without "All") or the last part of an Aura's or Equipment's list ("gets +2/+2, has first strike, and can't attack you or planeswalkers you control"). "You" is the controller of the source. The Vow cycle, Blazing Archon, Reverence and Sandwurm Convergence are reproduced.
- Prevention (`compile/read/prevention.rs`): "Damage can't be prevented." and the forms "Combat damage can't be prevented.", "Damage that would be dealt by this creature can't be prevented." and "Combat damage that would be dealt by creatures you control can't be prevented." are an `Ability::Rule` of `DamageCantBePrevented` (a source selector, `combat` true for combat and false for noncombat damage); "Damage can't be prevented this turn." is the `Effect::Rule` of it until end of turn and "Prevent all [combat] damage that would be dealt this turn." an `Effect::Shield` of `Prevent` on a `Damage` event. A recipient, a target source and "the next N damage" stay with the table. "planeswalker that player controls" in a trigger about a damaged player is `ControlledBy(Triggering)`, and a trigger about damage the card deals may say "it deals" for the card. Questing Beast and Fog are reproduced.
- Group statics (`compile/read/c11h_attached.rs`, `c11h_rules.rs`, `c11h_prevent.rs`, tests in `tests/c11h_statics.rs`): "Prevent all [combat|noncombat] damage that would be dealt to <group>." is a `Replacement` of `Damage` to `Each` of the group by `Prevent`, and "During your turn, prevent all damage that would be dealt to you." (or to ~) the same under `YourTurn`; "<group> spells [you control] can't be countered." is the rule `CantBeCountered` of `Each` spell of the group; "As long as enchanted permanent is a creature, it ...", "... equipped creature is a Human [or an Angel] ..." and "... is red or green ..." are a `Matches` of `Attached`. The reminder text of riot does not hold a group grant of it back. A union with "you", a source and a duration stay with the table.

- "If you do, ..." after a sentence that was a "you may" ("You may draw a card. If you do, discard a card.") is an `If` of `Performed` that follows the `May` (`payments::after_may`); after any other sentence it stays with the table.
- Payments in an effect (`compile/read/payments.rs`): "You may pay {1}. If you do, draw a card." is a `Pay` of the controller whose `paid` effects are the sentence after "if you do"; "... unless that player pays 3 life" and "... unless you discard a card" are a `Pay` whose `unpaid` effects are the sentence before "unless". The cost is one part: mana, life, energy or a discard, and after "you may" a sacrifice. Two parts, an X, a number the game counts, "if you don't" and a sacrifice after "unless" stay with the table, as does "you" as the payer of a counter or of a choice.
- "You may pay X life, where X is ... If you do, ..." (`c10_pa_pay_x.rs`) is a `Pay` of `PayLife` of the count, with the count in the place of X in the paid effects; an X in a trigger on becoming monstrous is `Amount::Triggering`, the X of the monstrosity (`c10_pa_monstrous.rs`).
- X that a trigger reads without an `{X}` in the mana cost: "you may pay {X}{R}. If you do, ..." (mana with an `{X}` on a card whose own cost has none) is a `Pay` whose `paid` sentence reads X, and the writer prints the cost as it is; "When you cycle ~, ..." on a face with an `{X}` in its cycling cost (`Own::w6c_cost_x`) reads X as the X of that cost; "up to X target ..." is a target bound of at most X (`selectors.rs`). Tests in `compile/read/tests/w6c_x_triggers.rs`.

- Transform and conditions (`compile/read/transform.rs`, `conditional.rs`, `reveal.rs`): "Transform ~", "transform it" (after a sentence that acted on the card) and "transform target creature you control" (one target on the battlefield) are `Effect::Transform`, also after "you may" and in an activation with its "Activate only" restrictions. "If there are five or more hatchling counters on ~, remove all of them and transform ~." is an `Effect::If` on a `Compare` of the counters on the card ("or more" or "or fewer" with two or more, a counter the rules do not define); "Then if" after another sentence and "it" for the card are wordings of the same effect. The effects after the comma are steps joined by "and" or in a list ("remove all of them" is the count the condition made) or one sentence of the other templates; "otherwise" and other conditions stay with the table. "Look at the top card of your library. You may reveal that card. If an instant or sorcery card is revealed this way, transform ~." is a `LookAtTop` and a `May` of the `Reveal` and an `If` on a `Matches` of the bound card, for any card filter a search reads. Delver of Secrets and Ludevic's Test Subject are reproduced.

- A capitalized word inside a sentence is a subtype when it is one of the game's types (`compile/read/subtypes.rs`, from the Oracle type lines) or one on the type line of the card being read (`clause_with`); any other is a name and is not read. A group is the plural ("Heroes you control", "Elves", "Wolves", "Spies": `plural_subtype`, which the reader accepts only for the plurals it writes), read for a trigger, a spell or a static; at the start of a sentence the capital is grammar and the word is not read as a subtype. A trigger of another permanent's event may return the card itself from the graveyard ("Whenever a land you control enters, you may return this card from your graveyard to the battlefield", `from: Graveyard`).
- Tokens (`compile/read/create.rs`, writer `render/created.rs`, tests in `tests/token_groups.rs` and `tests/it/token_shapes.rs` of `mtg-testkit`): a sentence creates one or more kinds ("create a 1/1 green Snake creature token, a 2/2 green Wolf creature token, and a 3/3 green Elephant creature token", "create two Fish tokens and create two tapped Treasure tokens"), each a `CreateToken`; a draw, mill, scry or surveil joins a token by "and" or ", then" ("Draw two cards and create a Treasure token.", "Create three Elf Warrior tokens, then mill three cards."); a described token may be named ("named Carnivore", "Tiny, a legendary 2/2 green Dog Detective creature token", the name in `TokenSpec.name` and `supertypes`) and may have an activated or triggered ability in quotes beside its keywords ("with flying and \"Whenever this token attacks, ...\"", `this token` is `~`); Vibranium, Map and Mutagen are predefined tokens. The writer closes the joints the mask marks into these sentences, so separate sentences stay a wording of their own. The reminder text of infect does not hold a clause back.
- Copies (`compile/read/copies.rs`): "Create a token that's a copy of target creature you control" is a `CreateToken` of `CopyOf`; the copied object is a target permanent ("another target creature" adds `Other`), "~", "enchanted creature" or "equipped creature" (`Attached`), or "it" and "that creature" once the sentence before named the target. "Create two tokens that are copies of ..." (X when the mana cost has an `{X}`) sets the count. The exceptions follow a comma and are joined by "and": "it's a Zombie in addition to its other types" (`AddSubtype`, `AddType`), "it's blue and black" or "colorless" (`SetColors`), "it's 7/7" (`SetPT`), "it's a 3/3 Golem artifact creature in addition to its other types" (a `SetPT` and an `AddSubtype` or `AddType` for each word, in that order), "it has flying and haste" (`AddKeyword`) and `it has "When ~ becomes the target of a spell or ability, sacrifice it."` (`GrantAbility` of a triggered ability read in the quotes); several tokens say "they have haste". "..., except it has haste. Exile it at the beginning of the next end step." (also "Sacrifice") binds the token as `copy` and adds a `DelayedTrigger` on the end step. "Populate", also after "..., then", is the `Choose` and `CreateToken` of the Copies table, and "You may have ~ enter as a copy of any creature on the battlefield" or "of a creature you control", with the same exceptions, is an `AsCopy` replacement. "Create a tapped and attacking token that's a copy of ..." and "... a copy of X and that's tapped and attacking" (several: "X tokens that are copies of it and that are tapped and attacking") set `tapped` and `attacking`: the token enters attacking a player or planeswalker its controller picks (a game of two seats asks nothing), and "it" in a trigger of the card's own attack is the card. A card type before another in the singular ("target artifact creature") is both types. A copy of a spell and any other exception stay with the table. "Sacrifice ~" (and "sacrifice it" in an ability about the card) is a `Sacrifice` of the card, and "When ~ becomes the target of a spell or ability" is written with "When" and "Whenever". "..., where X is ..." after the copy names the number of tokens (Nacatl War-Pride, Rionya, Fire Dancer: "one plus the number of instant and sorcery spells you've cast this turn"), read inside the template because the sentences that act on the copies ("They gain haste. Exile them ...") follow it, and "for each card you've discarded this turn," (or any tally of `defining::died_each`) before "create a token that's a copy of ~" is the number; ". The token enters tapped and attacking." (also "The tokens enter") sets `tapped` and `attacking` after the sentence that creates the copy (Living Laser; the writer offers it for each copy that enters attacking). Clone, Mirror Image, Phantasmal Image, Cackling Counterpart, Rootborn Defenses, Growing Ranks, Followed Footsteps, Sundering Growth, Flamerush Rider, Loki, the Deceiver and March of Progress are reproduced.
- Copy groups and entering copies (`compile/read/group_copies.rs`, `kicked_copies.rs`, `copies.rs`, tests in `tests/group_copies.rs` and `tests/it/copy_cards.rs` of `mtg-testkit`): "Each other creature becomes a copy of target nonlegendary creature until end of turn." with or without "Choose target creature you control." as a sentence of its own (the writer offers both wordings; "that creature" names the chosen target) and the exceptions after "until end of turn"; "~ becomes a copy of target ... with mana value X" with no end is a `BecomeCopy` for ever and may carry exceptions; "For each creature target player controls, create a token that's a copy of that creature."; "Create a token that's a copy of target creature. If this spell was kicked, create five of those tokens instead."; "You may have ~ enter as a copy of any creature card in a graveyard", "enter tapped as a copy of any land on the battlefield" and "As ~ enters, you may have it become a copy of any creature on the battlefield until end of turn, except it has haste." Mirrorweave, Nanogene Conversion, The Mycosynth Gardens, Clone Legion, Rite of Replication, Body Double, Vesuva and Cursed Mirror are reproduced.
- Copy leftovers (`compile/read/copies.rs`, tests in `tests/copy_leftovers.rs` and `tests/died_under.rs`, `tests/it/copy_leftovers.rs` of `mtg-testkit`): a token copy of the targets a sentence of its own chose ("Choose up to two target creatures you don't control. For each of those creatures [of them], create a token that's a copy of that creature." and "Choose target creature you control. For each creature chosen this way, create a token that's a copy of it.") is a `CreateToken` of `CopyOf` over `Target(0)`, one token for each object chosen. The sentences after a copy are read in any mix of "it", "that token" and "the token" (several: "they", "those tokens", "the tokens"): "It gains haste [until end of turn]." (a `Continuous` of the bound copy, `Forever` when no end is named) and "Exile it at the beginning of the next end step.", "Sacrifice it ...", "... at end of combat." (a `DelayedTrigger` on the end step or the end of combat step). "Nontoken creatures you control enter with an additional +1/+1 counter on them for each creature that died under your control this turn." (also "Each other creature you control enters with an additional +1/+1 counter on it.") is an `Enters` replacement of the group with `WithCounters`, and "a creature died under your control this turn", "two or more creatures died under your control this turn", "for each creature that died under your control this turn" and "the number of creatures that died under your control this turn" read `TurnTally::CreaturesDiedUnder` (or `NontokenCreaturesDiedUnder`) of `You`. Hate Mirage, Twinflame, Gorma, the Gullet, Body Count, Priest of the Crossing, Liliana's Standard Bearer, Grumgully, the Generous, Dragonstorm Globe, Oath of Gideon, Oona's Blackguard, Tempestra, Dame of Games and Helm of the Host are reproduced.
- Copies of spells and abilities (`compile/read/copy_spells.rs`): "Copy target instant or sorcery spell [you control]", "target permanent spell you control" (a spell that is neither an instant nor a sorcery) and "target triggered ability", "activated ability" or "activated or triggered ability" ("you control" optional) are a `CopySpell` or `CopyAbility` of the clause's one target. ". You may choose new targets for the copy" sets `new_targets` and ", except that the copy is red" (colors or "colorless", spells only) sets `except`. "Choose a nonlegendary creature on the battlefield. Target creature becomes a copy of that creature until end of turn." is a `Choose` bound as `copied` and a `BecomeCopy`, and "~ becomes a copy of target creature until end of turn" a `BecomeCopy` of the card. After a spell is cast (the trigger is a `SpellCast`, also the nth in a turn), "copy it" and "copy that spell" are a `CopySpell` of `Triggering`, also after "you may" and after "you may pay {3}. If you do,"; a count follows ("twice", "three times", "X times" when the cost has an `{X}`), the sentence about new targets says "copies" for any count but one, and the exceptions follow a comma ("except the copy is an artifact in addition to its other types", "except that the copy is red", "except the copy is a 1/1 Spirit in addition to its other types", "except the copy has flying"; the same words as a token copy, with "the copy" for "it"). A card writes "it" or "that spell" and "except that the copy" or "except the copy" alike, and the renderer offers each. "~ becomes a copy of another target creature until end of turn" is a `BecomeCopy` of the card, also as "it" in an attack trigger of the card. "Choose" opens a `Modal` clause only when its line has an em dash. A player's sentence may continue with "and you" ("Target player loses 2 life and you gain 2 life"), and an Aura or Equipment may give one activated ability that is not a mana ability in quotes ("Enchanted creature has \"{R}, {T}: Copy target instant or sorcery spell you control. ...\"", also "Enchanted land", "artifact" or "permanent"). "When you cast this spell" (the card itself, a `SpellCast` of `This` working from the stack, as storm and demonstrate do) reads "copy it" as the card (`what: This`) and the count "for each time you've cast your commander from the command zone this game" as `Amount::CommanderCasts`. "except it isn't legendary" (also "the token isn't", "the copy isn't") is `RemoveSupertype(Legendary)` and "except it's an artifact and it loses all other card types" is `SetTypes`, both applied to the copiable values of the copy (`CardDefinition::except`; `RemoveSupertype` is valid only there, `SetTypes` also as a layer 4 modification). A copy of an ability that a player activates, a spell copied for each of a count and other exceptions stay with the table. Echo Storm, Empyrial Storm, Fury Storm, Hatut Zeraze Strike Force, Quantum Misalignment, Twincast, Reverberate, Reiterate, Fork, Dualcaster Mage, Dual Casting, Grapeshot, Brain Freeze, Tendrils of Agony, Strionic Resonator, Lithoform Engine, Cytoshape, Squirrel Nest, Mirari, Swarm Intelligence and Tilonalli's Skinshifter are reproduced.
- Groups and lords (`compile/read/selectors.rs`, tests in `tests/lords.rs`): in a plural phrase a subtype or a card type in the singular qualifies the plural noun after it. "Sliver creatures" is `[Type(Creature), Subtype(Sliver)]`, "artifact creatures" `[Type(Artifact), Type(Creature)]`, "creature tokens" `[Type(Creature), Token]`, "Dragon spells" `[Spell, Subtype(Dragon)]`; the card types come first, then the subtype, "other" and who controls them. The word that opens a sentence is a subtype too ("Sliver creatures you control get +2/+0", "Zombies you control get +1/+1"). The writer says "All Sliver creatures get +1/+1" and "Sliver creatures get +1/+1" for a group no one controls, and "Dragon spells you cast cost {1} less to cast" is a `CostModifier` over such a selector. A group of a subtype and an unknown noun, two subtypes and a singular token stay with the table.
- Land plays (`compile/read/lands.rs`, tests in `tests/lands.rs`): "You may play an additional land on each of your turns" (also "two additional lands", and "during" for "on"; "Each player may ..." for every player) is `Rule.AdditionalLandPlays`, "You may play an additional land this turn" and "You may play up to three additional lands this turn" the effect of it until end of turn, "You may play lands from your graveyard" and "... from the top of your library" a `MayCast` permission over `Type(Land)` with `play`, and "You may look at the top card of your library any time" is `LookAtTopAnyTime`. The engine plays all four (`engine.md`, Rules of the game).
- Level blocks (`compile/read/levels.rs`, tests in `tests/levels.rs`): "LEVEL 2-3" (or "LEVEL 4+"), the power and toughness line and the lines under it read into one ability each, every one conditioned on the level counters of the creature (`Compare` of `Counters{This, level}`, both bounds in an `All`): the size is a `Static` `SetPT`, a line of keywords a `Static` `AddKeyword`, any other line the ability it reads as, with the range joined to its own condition. A block with a line that does not read (a mana ability given to a group, protection from instants) stays with the table. "Each creature you control with a +1/+1 counter on it has flying" is a `Static` over `Each` with `HasCounter`, "Copy target instant or sorcery spell twice. You may choose new targets for the copies." a `CopySpell` of count 2, and "Whenever ~ attacks while saddled" an `Attacks` trigger with the intervening `Matches` of `HasKeyword(Saddled)`.
- Class levels (`compile/read/class.rs`, tests in `tests/class.rs`): "{3}{U}: Level 2" and the lines under it (up to the next level line) read into the level up ability (`BecomeLevel`, sorcery timing, `Level` equal to N-1) and one ability for each line, every one conditioned on `Level` at least N; "When this Class becomes level 2" is `BecomesLevel`; a block with a line that does not read stays with the table. The lines before the first level line are the abilities of level 1.
- Saga chapters (`compile/read/chapters.rs`, tests in `tests/chapters.rs`): a line "I — text" or "II, III — text" (and the bullets under a "Choose one —") reads like a spell ability and becomes one `Triggered` with `Chapter { number }` for each number it names, written back as "I — text"; a chapter whose text does not read stays with the table. "this Saga" is the card itself (`~`).
- Exile and return (`compile/read/flicker.rs`, tests in `tests/flicker.rs`): "Exile ~, then return it to the battlefield [transformed] under your control." (also "under its owner's control") is an `Exile` of `This` that binds `exiled` and a `Move` of that name onto the battlefield with `under` (none for the owner) and `transformed`; the writer joins the two into the one sentence. It is the last chapter of a transforming Saga. Another card exiled and returned, a return to the hand, a tapped return and "(front face up)" stay with the table.
- Station blocks (`compile/read/stations.rs`, tests in `tests/stations.rs`): "9+ | Flying, first strike" (the threshold, a bar, the first line) and the lines under it read into one ability each, every one conditioned on `Compare` of `Counters{This, charge}` at least the threshold (brackets add up: the lower ones keep working); a line of keywords is a `Static` `AddKeyword`, any other line the ability it reads as. The block of the highest threshold of a Spacecraft with a printed power also starts with a `Static` `AddType(Creature)` (`Own.creature_at`), so the Spacecraft keeps its printed power and toughness. A block with a line that does not read stays with the table. "this Spacecraft" is the card itself.
- Characteristic-defining abilities (`compile/read/defining.rs`, tests in `tests/defining.rs`): "~'s power and toughness are each equal to ...", "~'s power is equal to ... [and its toughness is equal to ... / that number plus N]" and "~'s toughness is equal to ..." are a `Static` of `This` with `defining` and one `SetPT`; a stat the sentence leaves out is the number printed on the card (`read::Own`, taken from the face), and the sentence stays with the table when that stat is `*`. The value is a count of "the number of" a group ("creatures you control", "Forests on the battlefield", "cards in your hand", "creature cards in your graveyard / in all graveyards / in your opponents' graveyards", "instant and sorcery cards in your graveyard"), "the number of card types among" such cards, "your life total", "your devotion to black and red", "the greatest mana value among" a group or "the total power of" it, "twice" a count, or counts and numbers joined by "plus". The engine evaluates every one but devotion (it needs the copiable mana cost, so it is a restructure of `Characteristics`). Group words that are not read: a subtype with "cards" ("Elf cards in your graveyard"), two zones in one count, "the chosen player's graveyard", counters, party size and numbers noted by the card.
- Cost changes under a condition and for two kinds of spells (`compile/read/cost_conditions.rs`, tests in `tests/cost_conditions.rs`): " if ..." after "to cast", or "If ..., " before the spell, is the `condition` of the `CostModifier` (it holds for the caster, read at the cost; " if it targets a Dinosaur you control" after the cost of the spell itself is `Pred::Targeting` beside `This` instead, `cost_conditions::c9_fight_targets`). The conditions are the ones of "as long as" (what the caster controls: "you control a Dragon", "a creature with flying"), "there are ten or more nonland permanents on the battlefield", "there are ten or more creature cards total in all graveyards", "an opponent controls no basic lands" (no opponent controls one), and "you gained life this turn" (`Amount::Tallied` of `TurnTally::LifeGained`, once for each gain). "Merfolk spells and Wizard spells you cast" and "Blue spells and red spells you cast" are one `Spell` with an `AnyOf` of the kinds; the writer says them as separate phrases. A condition on one opponent's graveyard, hand or lands ("an opponent has seven or more cards in their graveyard"), on the targets, or on the noun shared by two subtypes ("Aura and Equipment spells") stays with the table.
- The party (CR 700.8; `Amount::Party(who)`, `engine.md`, Party) and the chosen player (`Ref::Chosen`, `ReplaceBy.Choose.Player` and `.Opponent`): "the number of creatures in your party", "for each creature in your party" (also a cost change: "costs {1} less to cast for each creature in your party") and "if you have a full party" (`Compare` of `Party(You)` at least 4, in `compile/read/facts.rs`, tests in `compile/read/tests/party.rs`); "protection from the chosen player" in a static ability is `Protection` over `ControlledBy(Chosen)`. A chosen-player card is written by hand (True-Name Nemesis, Sewer Nemesis, Stuffy Doll: a count `OwnedBy(Chosen)`, a trigger `SpellCast{by: Chosen}`, a `DealDamage` to `Chosen`), as are the party cards Spoils of Adventure and Malakir Blood-Priest.
- Cost changes that read targets and tallies (hand-written rules, tests in `tests/it/cost_reductions_more.rs`): `Pred::Targeting(selector)` selects a spell or ability on the stack with a target the selector matches ("This spell costs {3} less to cast if it targets a creature that was dealt damage this turn" is `applies_to: [This, Targeting([Creature, DamagedThisTurn])]`; "Spells you cast that target a creature" and "Spells your opponents cast that target this creature" name it beside `ControlledBy`); `Amount::Largest{who, of}` counts `of` for each player `who` names taken as "you" and keeps the largest ("an opponent controls seven or more lands"), `Amount::StartingLife` is the life the game began with ("half their starting life total" is twice the life total at most that); `TurnTally::LifeGainedTotal`, `CreaturesAttacked` and `CreaturesDied` are summed over the players named, `SpellsCast` counts the spells cast before the one being cast ("the second spell you cast each turn" is one). The reader takes "Aura and Equipment spells you cast" and "Creature spells you cast with power 4 or greater", and the writer words both ways.
- Cost changes of the turn and of an ability (`compile/read/c11a_turn_costs.rs`, `c11a_ability_less.rs`, tests in `tests/c11a_turn_costs.rs`, `tests/c11a_ability_less.rs`): "for each 1 life your opponents have lost this turn", "for each creature that attacked this turn", "for each creature that died this turn", "for each 1 life you gained this turn" and "{X} ..., where X is the amount of life you gained this turn" are tallies of the turn (`LifeLost`, `CreaturesAttacked`, `CreaturesDied`, `LifeGainedTotal`); "The first creature spell you cast each turn costs {2} less to cast." (also "artifact", and "The second spell") is a `CostModifier` of the caster's spells of the kind under a `Compare` of `TurnTally::CreatureSpellsCast`, `ArtifactSpellsCast` or `SpellsCast` equal to the spells cast before. "This ability costs {1} less to activate for each instant and sorcery card in your graveyard." (also "more", "if" a condition, before "Activate only ...") after the effects of an activated ability is `CostItem::Changed { change, mana, per, condition }` at the end of its cost: no payment, the change the ability makes to its own mana as it is activated. "for each opponent you attacked this turn", a reduction by the target's power, Equip lines and abilities that reduce other abilities stay with the table.
- Life gain (`compile/read/life_gain.rs`, tests in `tests/life_gain.rs`): "If you would gain life, you gain that much life plus N instead." is a `Replacement` of `LifeGain` by `Add(N)`, "... twice that much life instead." `Multiply(2)`, and "Players can't gain life.", "Your opponents can't gain life." and "You can't gain life." are the rule `CantGainLife`. "As long as you have 25 or more life" (or "or less") after or before a static is its `condition`. Another player's loss, "draw that many cards instead", a life total in the condition of the replacement and "for the rest of the game" stay with the table.
- Phasing and protected players (`Effect::PhaseOut`, `GameRule::LifeTotalCantChange`, `GameRule::PlayersHave`, engine in `phasing.rs` and `rules.rs`): `PhaseOut{what}` phases out the permanents `what` names with what is attached to them (CR 702.26), "until your next turn, your life total can't change and you gain protection from everything" is two `Effect::Rule`s of `UntilYourNextTurn`, `LifeTotalCantChange(You)` and `PlayersHave{who: You, keyword: Protection([])}`, and "any number of target ... phase out" is a target with `max: null`. Teferi's Protection, Clever Concealment, Guardian of Faith and Robe of Stars are Verified corpus cards (`tests/it/phasing.rs` plays them at two to four seats). The compiler leaves these sentences to the table.
- Extra turns and added phases (`Effect::ExtraTurn`, `Effect::AddPhase`, engine in `turn.rs`, `steps.rs` and `resolve/prim/extra_turn.rs`, `add_phase.rs`, `engine.md`; reader and writer in `compile/read/turns.rs`, tests in `tests/turns.rs`): "Take an extra turn after this one.", "Target player takes two extra turns after this one." (`predicate` reads "take" and "takes"), "Take an extra turn after this one. Skip the untap step of that turn.", "After this main phase, there is an additional combat phase followed by an additional main phase.", "After this phase, there is an additional combat phase." and "After this main phase, there are two additional combat phases."; the writer words a list of a combat and a main phase and two combat phases with "main phase" and a single combat phase with "phase". Time Walk, Temporal Manipulation, Capture of Jingzhou, Time Warp, Time Stretch, Savor the Moment, Aggravated Assault, Seize the Day, Time Sieve and Magistrate's Scepter are in the corpus.
- Rules about targets, losing, casting, damage and counters (`compile/read/w17b_statics.rs`, tests in `tests/w17b_statics.rs`): "Cards in graveyards can't be the targets of spells or abilities." is `GameRule::GraveyardCardsUntargetable`, "You can't lose the game and your opponents can't win the game." `CantLoseGame { who: You, opponents_cant_win }` (without the second half `opponents_cant_win` is false), "Your opponents can't cast spells during your turn." `CantCastSpells { who: Opponents, your_turn }`, "Each creature [you control] assigns combat damage equal to its toughness rather than its power." `ToughnessAssignsDamage(You | EachPlayer)` and "Counters remain on ~ as it moves to any zone other than a player's hand or library." `CountersRemain`, written without `from` and never by an effect. Ground Seal, Skullbriar, Herald of Eternal Dawn and Dragonlord Dromoka are in the corpus.
- Phase conditions, creatures that attacked and rules for searches and extra turns (`compile/read/c4_phase.rs`, `c4_rules.rs`, `selectors::suffix`, writers in `render/oracle_only/c4_phases.rs`, tests in `tests/c4_conditions.rs` and `tests/c4_phases.rs`): "if it's the first combat phase of the turn" is `Condition::FirstCombat` (in a combat phase and `TurnState::combats` is 1; "... of your turn" adds `YourTurn`), "if it's your main phase" is `All[YourTurn, Any[Step(Main1), Step(Main2)]]` and "if it's your combat phase" is `All[YourTurn, InCombat]`, read for the intervening "if" of a trigger and after "if" in a sentence; "creatures that attacked this turn" is `Pred::AttackedThisTurn` (the attack tally of the object); "Your opponents can't search libraries." and "Players can't search libraries." are `GameRule::CantSearch`, and "If an opponent would begin an extra turn, that player skips that turn instead." (or "a player") is `GameRule::SkipExtraTurns`. The checker's writers word an untap and an added combat phase in one sentence ("untap all attacking creatures and after this phase, there is an additional combat phase"), energy got and then paid ("you get {E}{E}, then you may pay eight {E}. If you pay, ..."), "untap it and all Samurai you control", "untap each attacking creature" and an attack at the player with the most life. Stranglehold, Relentless Assault and Mindlock Orb are read by the compiler; Hellkite Charger, Lightning Runner, Godo, Scourge of the Throne, Hexplate Wallbreaker and Tifa are in the corpus.
- Goad (`compile/read/actions.rs`, `statics.rs`, tests in `tests/goad.rs`): "Goad target creature [an opponent controls].", "Goad all creatures you don't control.", "you may goad target creature" and "goad it" after a sentence that named the creature are `Effect::Goad`; "Enchanted creature gets +2/+2 and is goaded.", "has indestructible and is goaded." and "gets +1/+1, has deathtouch, and is goaded." (also "Equipped") are a `Static` of `Attached` whose last modification is `Goaded`. "you scry 2", "you surveil 1" and an effect of the controller that opens with "you" after "Whenever enchanted creature attacks," are the wordings of an Aura trigger. A goad of "each creature that player controls", "that player draws a card" after it, "must be blocked if able" beside "is goaded", "goaded creatures ... can't block", "goaded for the rest of the game" and a goad of the card itself stay with the table. Disrupt Decorum, Psychic Impetus and Shiny Impetus are Verified corpus cards (`tests/it/goad.rs` plays them at two to four seats).
- Abilities of the graveyard (`compile/read/abilities.rs`, `costs.rs`, `effects.rs`, tests in `tests/graveyard.rs`): "{M}: Return this card from your graveyard to the battlefield [tapped]" and "... to your hand" and "Exile this card from your graveyard" as a cost (`ExileSelf`) are `Activated` with `from: Graveyard`; "Exile this creature" alone is the same cost on the battlefield.
