"When ~ is put into a graveyard from anywhere, shuffle it into its owner's library" is a `Triggered` with `from: Graveyard` that the engine also looks for on a permanent leaving the battlefield (`triggers/detect.rs`), and "When this Aura is put into a graveyard from the battlefield, return it to its owner's hand" is the dying pattern; both name the card in the graveyard as `Each([In(Graveyard), This])`, a new object there.
- Manlands and group pumps (`compile/read/becomes.rs`, `group_pump.rs`, tests in `tests/becomes.rs`, `tests/each_pump.rs`): "~ becomes a 3/3 red Elemental artifact creature with flying and vigilance until end of turn. It's still a land." is one `Continuous` effect of the card in the order size, colors, creature types (`SetCreatureTypes`, a capitalized word between the colors and "creature" is one), `AddType` artifact and creature, keywords, `AddAllCreatureTypes` ("with all creature types") and one quoted triggered or activated ability (`GrantAbility`; its period is inside the quotes and no "until end of turn" follows it); the duration may open the sentence ("Until end of turn, ..."), "It's still a land." is optional, and "It can't be blocked this turn." is a second `Continuous` of `CantBeBlocked`. A size of X, a duration that never ends, another subject and a quote without its period stay with the table. "Each other creature you control gets +1/+1 until end of turn" is the effect of the plural wording ("Other creatures you control get ..."), which the renderer writes first. "If you control two or more other lands, ~ enters tapped." is a `Replacement` of `Enters` by `Tapped` whose condition is the count (the reverse of "unless").
- Vehicles and Mounts that an effect animates (`compile/read/becomes.rs`, `statics.rs`, tests in `tests/becomes.rs`, `tests/as_long_as.rs`): "~ becomes an artifact creature until end of turn" and "another target Vehicle you control becomes an artifact creature until end of turn" ("up to one other target ..." too) are one `Continuous` of `AddType` artifact and creature with the card or `Target(0)` as `affected` and no `SetPT`, so the Vehicle keeps the size it prints; "target Mount you control becomes saddled until end of turn" is `AddKeyword(Saddled)`, the designation Saddle grants. The cost is any activated cost ("Pay {E}{E}{E}{E}", "Tap two untapped tokens you control"). "As long as ~ has three or more fire counters on it, it's an artifact creature." is a `Static` of the same `AddType` pair under the counter condition (the renderer writes the pronoun as "it's"). A duration that never ends and a second step stay with the table; `becomes::creature` reads the wording with a size. The same sentence stands after a trigger ("Whenever ~ becomes crewed, up to one other target Vehicle you control becomes an artifact creature until end of turn."); an "up to one" target is also returned ("return up to one target creature that crewed it this turn to its owner's hand", `Bounds { min: 0, max: 1 }`).
- Changes of a target or a group for a while (`compile/read/changes.rs`, tests in `tests/changes.rs`, `becomes.rs`, `group_pump.rs`): the parts after one subject, joined by "and", are one `Continuous` in the order of the words, and the writer says them in the order of the data. The parts are "get +a/+b", "gain" or "lose" keywords (`AddKeyword`, `RemoveKeyword`; "protection from each color" is `Protection` of every colored source), "gain all creature types" (`AddAllCreatureTypes`), "lose all abilities" (`LoseAbilities`), "lose all creature types" (`SetCreatureTypes` of none), "have base power and toughness a/b" (`SetPT`, X allowed) and "become a blue Frog with base power and toughness 1/1" (`SetCreatureTypes`, `SetColors`, `SetPT` in that order, as the layers apply them). The duration is "until end of turn" or "until your next turn" (`UntilYourNextTurn`), last or first ("Until end of turn, ..."). The subject is a target, several targets ("up to four target permanents you control"), a group, or the group of the player the spell targets ("creatures target player controls", "each creature target player controls": a `ControlledBy(Target(0))` predicate beside a player target that the group names). A group may be "non-Elf" (`Not` of a `Subtype`). `becomes::creature` also reads a target land ("Target land you control becomes a 3/3 Elemental creature with haste until end of turn. It's still a land."), an X size and "in addition to its other types" (`AddSubtype` instead of `SetCreatureTypes`), and `becomes::animate` "becomes an artifact in addition to its other types". The engine runs all of them as `Continuous`: the amounts are worked out as the effect begins and the group is the permanents named then.
- The creatures that paid for the source (`selectors::suffix`, `effects::return_any_number`, tests in `tests/crew_record.rs`): "that crewed it this turn" and "that saddled it this turn" after a creature phrase are `Pred::Crewed(This)` and `Pred::Saddled(This)`, as a target ("target creature that crewed it this turn explores") and as a group; "[Then] you may return any number of creatures that saddled it this turn to their owner's hand" is a `Choose` of none or more of them bound as `returned` and a `Move` to the hand (a card of another kind or of another group stays unread). "Mill two cards, then ~ gets +X/+X until end of turn, where X is ..." is a chain whose second step is the pump of the card itself, so the size counts what the mill left.
- Phase-out durations, counts and triggers (`compile/read/w11b_phase.rs`, tests in `tests/w11b_phase.rs`): "... phases out until ~ leaves the battlefield" is `PhaseOut.duration: WhileSourceOnBattlefield`, ". Tap that creature as it phases in this way" is `PhaseOut.tapped`, "It phases out ..." after a single target is that target, "untap all creatures, then those creatures phase out until ~ leaves the battlefield" is an `Untap` and a `PhaseOut` of one group, "for each creature that phased out this way" is `Amount.PhasedOut`, and "whenever ~ phases out", "whenever a creature you control phases in" and "whenever one or more other permanents phase out" are `Trigger.PhasedOut`, `Trigger.PhasedIn` and their `Batch`. Oubliette and Teferi's Imp are corpus cards (`tests/it/w11b_phase_durations.rs` plays them at two to four seats).
- Time counters and exile batches (`compile/read/w15b_time.rs`, tests in `tests/w15b_time.rs`): a keyword line "Vanishing" without a number is `Vanishing(0)`; "with mana value less than or equal to the number of time counters on ~" is `Pred.Compare` of `Amount.Counters` on `This`; "Destroy each X" reads as "Destroy all Xs" and is written back both ways, with "Then sacrifice ~." after it; "one or more [other] cards are put into exile from anywhere" is a `Batch` of `ZoneChange` to exile of nontoken cards; and "it deals damage equal to the number of time counters on it" names the source by "it". Out of Time, The Moment and The War Doctor are corpus cards (`tests/it/w15b_out_of_time.rs` plays them at two to four seats).
- Phase-out sentences, Phasing and devour counts (`compile/read/w7e_phase.rs`, tests in `tests/w7e_phase_out.rs` and `tests/w7e_devoured.rs`): "target creature phases out", "any number of target creatures you control phase out" and "all permanents you control phase out" are `PhaseOut`; "until your next turn, your life total can't change and you gain protection from everything" is the two rules of Teferi's Protection, also after a group phase-out joined by ", and" and under "if the gift was promised" (Perch Protection). `Keyword.Phasing` is read from its keyword line. `Amount.Devoured` (only on a card with Devour) counts the permanents the source devoured as it entered: "for each creature it devoured".
- Permanents that entered this turn (`selectors::suffix`, tests in `tests/entered_this_turn.rs`): "that entered this turn" and "that entered the battlefield this turn" after a phrase are `Pred.EnteredThisTurn` (written back as the first; the second is a variant of `render::variants`), for a target ("put a +1/+1 counter on target creature that entered this turn") and a group ("each green creature that entered this turn"). A single token is named as a target ("target creature token", "target token"), and "create a tapped token that's a copy of target token" is a `CreateToken` of `CopyOf` with `tapped`; Octomancer, Romana II, Oran-Rief, the Vastwood, Novijen and Deathleaper are Verified, and "entered the battlefield under your control this turn" stays unread.
- Removal and return (`compile/read/selectors.rs`, `effects.rs`, `create.rs`, tests in `tests/removal_and_return.rs`): the card types of a phrase may be a list ("artifact, creature, or planeswalker", "all artifacts, creatures, and enchantments"; a "with" qualifier after three or more types stays with the table), two negations take a comma ("nonartifact, nonblack creature") and "another target X" is a target with `Other`. "Return target creature card from your graveyard to the battlefield" is a `Move` onto the battlefield (under its owner, who is you); "When ~ enters, sacrifice it unless you pay {R}" is a `Pay` whose unpaid effect sacrifices the card (also on an attack); "Its controller creates a 3/3 green Beast creature token.", "That creature's controller creates ..." and "Its controller investigates." after a destroy or an exile are a `CreateToken` of `ControllerOf(Target)`. Another owner's graveyard, "tapped", "under your control", "that player" or "its owner" who creates and a count of what the destroy did stay with the table.
- Granted keywords and the skipped step (`compile/read/selectors.rs` and `statics.rs`, tests in `tests/group_statics.rs`): a granted keyword is also `skulk` and `ward` with a mana cost, in a static or a pump ("Other creatures you control have ward {2}."); "Skip your draw step." is the rule `SkipDrawStep(You)`. Another cost of ward and another player's draw step stay with the table.
- Tail wordings (`compile/read/facts.rs`, `actions.rs`, `effects.rs`, `prevention.rs`, `replacements.rs`, `pronouns.rs`, tests in `tests/tail_alone.rs`): the intervening "if you gained N or more life this turn" is a `Condition::Compare` of `TurnTally::LifeGainedTotal` (life is counted in digits; one is the plain "you gained life this turn"); "you may gain N life" and "you may lose N life" are one `May` around the life change; a card that is a person on its card connives as "he" (`Connive` of `This`, written beside the "it" wording); "When this Equipment enters, attach it to target creature you control" is an `Attach` of `This` to a target of the trigger of a card that enters or attacks (`actions::attach`); "Prevent all [combat] damage that would be dealt to this creature (or you)." is a static `Replacement` of `Damage` to the card with `Prevent`, and "If damage would be dealt to this creature, prevent that damage. Remove a +1/+1 counter from this creature." the same with `PreventRemovingCounter`.
- Aura grants (`compile/read/statics.rs`, `effects.rs`, `actions.rs`, `counters.rs`, `pronouns.rs`, tests in `tests/aura_grants.rs`): "Enchanted creature has flying and \"...\"" is an `AddKeyword` and a `GrantAbility` of the quoted activated or triggered ability on `Attached` (also after a bonus: "gets +1/+1 and has lifelink and \"...\""; one keyword only), and the ability belongs to the controller of the host, not of the Aura. "lose(s) all creature types" is `SetCreatureTypes([])`, alone or after a size ("have base power and toughness 3/3 and lose all creature types"). In an ability of an Aura "enchanted creature" is `Ref::Attached` for the objects a verb acts on (tap, untap, destroy, exile, counters put on it) and for `Effect::Fight`, whose second creature is "up to one target" or one target ("When this Aura enters, enchanted creature fights up to one target creature you don't control."). "When enchanted creature dies" is written beside "Whenever", and "they lose 2 life" stands for "that player loses 2 life" in a trigger about a player (`Drew` of `AttachedPlayer`).
- Stay-tapped statics and joined sentences (`compile/read/untap_rules.rs`, `joined.rs`, tests in `tests/untap_rules.rs`): "This artifact doesn't untap during your untap step." and "Enchanted creature doesn't untap during its controller's untap step [unless that player is the monarch]." (also "equipped", "permanent", "land", "artifact"; "unless you're the monarch" for the card itself) are a static `DoesntUntap` on `This` or `Attached`, the monarch as `Not(Monarch(...))`. A sentence of the controller after another ("Tap enchanted creature and you become the monarch.", "..., then you gain 2 life and draw a card.") is read as the two sentences in order (`joined::and_you`, `then_you`), and the writer says the second as "and you", ", then you" or a verb phrase joined with "and". "it" in a trigger on a permanent becoming tapped or untapped is the permanent.
- Held-down taps (`compile/read/c10_uc.rs`, `render/c10_uc.rs`, tests in `tests/c10_uc.rs`): "Tap target creature ... That creature doesn't untap during its controller's untap step for as long as you control this creature." (and "up to four target creatures. Those creatures don't untap during their controllers' untap steps ...") is the tap and a `DoesntUntap` over `Target(0)` with `WhileYouControlSource`; "Tap all creatures target player controls. Those creatures don't untap during that player's next untap step." is the tap of the group and `NextUntapStep` over it; "tap up to X target creatures, where X is <count>." puts the count in the bounds; "~ enters tapped and doesn't untap during your untap step." is the pair of a `Tapped` replacement and a static `DoesntUntap` (`static_pairs`).
- Static grants (`compile/read/selectors.rs`, `statics.rs`, `lands.rs`, tests in `tests/static_grants.rs`): the keywords a static gives are the plain words of `KEYWORDS` (also fear, intimidate, exalted, extort, evolve, persist, undying, wither, flash and shadow), a landwalk by its word ("islandwalk": `Landwalk` of the land subtype) and a keyword with a number (`fabricate 1`, `annihilator 2`, `toxic 1`, `afterlife 1`); "Enchanted land has shroud" is `Attached` as well as "Enchanted creature" (the writer says land, artifact and permanent for every keyword grant of an Aura); "Commander creatures you control" is `Commander` among the card types and "Non-Archon creatures" is `Not(Subtype)`; a subtype written the same in the plural (Merfolk, Kor, Kithkin, Djinn, Moonfolk, Sheep, Deer, Kavu) reads in a group; "During your turn, ..." opens a static as `YourTurn` beside "As long as it's your turn, ..."; "You may play Forests from your graveyard" is the land permission of a basic land type. The ward reminder ("counter it unless that player pays") is not the reminder of a counter, so a clause that grants ward reads.
- Counted, chosen and remade statics (`compile/read/selectors.rs`, `statics.rs`, `amounts.rs`, `aura_forms.rs`, tests in `tests/grants_counted.rs`, `tests/aura_forms.rs`): "for each artifact and/or enchantment you control" is the count of `AnyOf` types (the writer also says "or"), "for each opponent you have" is `Players(Opponents)`, and "gets +10/+10 and loses flying" is a `ModifyPT` and a `RemoveKeyword`. Protection from "the chosen color" is `Protection([ChosenColor])` and from "creatures of the chosen type" is `Protection([Type(Creature), ChosenSubtype])`, read in a static only (a spell's effect has no choice to read); a creature type in the plural ("Rats") is `Protection([Subtype])`. The engine writes the color or type the source chose as it entered into the keyword when the static grants it (`resolve::prim::continuous::chosen_in`), so an Aura's host or a lord's creature holds protection from the color the source chose; a card gives itself the chosen protection without a keyword line. An Aura that remakes its creature ("Enchanted creature is a Treefolk with base power and toughness 0/4 and loses all abilities.") is one list in the order of the layers: `SetColors`, `SetTypes` when the words name a card type (creature, artifact creature), `SetCreatureTypes`, `SetPT`, `LoseAbilities`, the `AddKeyword`s it has, `Goaded`; the writer says it "is ..." first or, with nothing else given, "loses all abilities and is ..." first, and "has indestructible, and it loses all other abilities, card types, and creature types" when keywords follow the loss.
- Cards taken from among the top cards (`compile/read/among.rs`, tests in `tests/among.rs`): "Look at the top five cards of your library. You may reveal a creature card from among them and put it into your hand. Put the rest on the bottom of your library in a random order." is `LookAtTop` bound as `top`, a `Choose` of `kept` from `Each` of `In(Library)`, `Is(Bound(top))` and the filter (`min` 0), a `Reveal` of `kept`, a `Move` of `kept` and a `Move` of `top` (the cards that left are other objects and are passed over). "Reveal the top N cards" adds a `Reveal` of `top` and drops the reveal of `kept`; "You may put a land card from among them into your hand / onto the battlefield [tapped]", "Put up to two land cards from among them ...", "Put a land card from among them ..." (one card, not optional) and "Put all land cards revealed this way into your hand and the rest ..." (a `Move` of the `Each`) are the same shape; the rest goes into the graveyard or on the bottom in any or a random order. The filter is the one of a search (land types and card types as "A or B" or "A, B, or C": "a basic Forest, Plains, or Island card"); a count of X, another library, a chosen type, "Then put the rest", a cast or an exile from among them stay with the table. "You may cast creature spells from the top of your library." (a card type, a subtype, "non" and two joined by "and") is a `MayCast` permission from the library over the cards the group names (`lands::cast_from_the_top`); a rider, "lands and spells" and the other zones stay with the table.
- Mass moves (`compile/read/mass_moves.rs`, tests in `tests/mass_moves.rs`): "Put all creatures on the bottom (or on top) of their owners' libraries.", "Return all nonland permanents to their owners' hands." and "Return all artifact and enchantment cards from your graveyard to the battlefield." are a `Move` of `Each(selector)`; a selector with no zone names the battlefield. A token moved to a library ceases to exist (CR 111.7) and is not put in the order of the cards. "Each player (or opponent) discards their hand" is a `Discard` of the count of cards in those hands, more than any one hand holds, so each hand is taken whole; "Each player discards their hand, then draws seven cards." reads and writes as one sentence (`draws::wheel`); "..., then draws cards equal to the greatest number of cards a player discarded this way" stays with the table.
- Draws and discards (`compile/read/draws.rs`, tests in `tests/draws.rs`): "draw an additional card" and "draw two additional cards" in a draw step are a plain `Draw`, and "each player's" before a step is `StepBegins` of `EachPlayer`, whose "that player" is the player whose step it is (also after "each opponent's"); "You draw a card and you lose 1 life." is the effects of "you" with the subject said again for each verb; "Whenever you attack with two or more creatures" is `GroupAttacks` of the creatures you control; "Whenever an opponent casts their second spell each turn" and "draws their second card" are an `Nth` of that player (also "a player"); "Discard your hand." and "target player / that player discards their hand" are a `Discard` of a `Count` of the cards in that player's hand, and "each player discards their hand" stays with the table.
- `tests/it/compile_corpus.rs` is the ratchet: every ability the compiler reads equals one of the hand-written file (mode labels aside, which the compiler leaves empty), and the corpus cards it reproduces whole (`tests/fixtures/reproduced.txt`) never shrink in number.
- `coverage::analyze(cards, caps) -> Report` judges each distinct card of a deck list: the hand-written rules when the embedded library has them, else the compiler's (rules that fail `check_printed` count as `Manual` with the blocker `rules: invalid`), its `Support` from `Library::support`, and its blockers, which are the unread clauses (kind and shape: numbers and the words one to ten as `N`, a run of mana symbols as `{M}`, the card's name as `~`; of the spell lines of one spell only those that do not read alone, and a cost that counts targets waits for its spell and is no blocker while the spell does not read), the primitives of its rules that `Caps` lacks and the printed gaps. A blocker holds a card back alone when it is the card's only one.
- `mtg-cli coverage [--deck <code or name part>] [--top <n>]` runs it over the stored precons with `mtg_engine::caps()` and prints about 70 lines: the cards by state, distinct and by copies, the five best and worst precons by the share of their copies that are `Verified`, and the `n` (20) blockers that alone hold back the most cards, then the `n` that hold back the most in all.
- `mtg-cli audit [--count 100] [--seed N] [FILE]` lists a seeded sample of the stored cards whose clauses the compiler reads beyond keyword lines: for each clause the oracle text, the reading (`render_variants`) and the JSON, and the unread clauses. The cards are put in the order the seed and their oracle ids give and the first `count` that are read are taken, so the same seed lists the same cards and a smaller count is a prefix of a larger one. A reader compares the lines for a wrong target, amount, owner or timing, a dropped clause and a reading true only for some cards; each disagreement is fixed in the template or the wording is left to the table, with a golden in `compile/read/tests/audit.rs`.
- A line the compiler cannot read becomes `Ability::Manual { reason, span }` and the card `Partial`; if nothing is read the card is `Manual`. Each compiled card is rendered back to English and compared with its oracle text in the golden tests.
- A card the engine does not run whole is cast and resolved without effects for the clauses it leaves out, and its controller does them by hand with the generic logged actions (`engine.md`, Manual actions). `assemble` copies the left-out text into the definition: `CardDefinition.manual` holds a `ManualClause {text, reason}` for each clause the support record names on a face (the text its span covers, so a `Partial` card shows the clauses of its `Manual` abilities and, for an ability the engine lacks a primitive for, the whole face text), and a card with no rules attached (`Manual`, `Stale`) one clause of the whole rules text of each face that has any outside reminder text. A `Verified` card has none. Every seat reads the clauses on the card (`ObjectView.manual`, `protocol.md`) and sees the card's support (`deck-check.md`). The field is left out of the serialized form while empty, so the deck fingerprint of a card without clauses does not change.
- Prior art for the templates: phase.rs's oracle parser (`crates/engine/src/parser` in its repository; MIT or Apache-2.0) and XMage (MIT). Forge and Magarena are GPL-3.0: study only, copy nothing.
- Moves between zones by a choice (`compile/read/zone_moves.rs`, written back by `render.rs` and `mass_moves.rs`): "Return up to two target creature cards from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped." (a type, a subtype, "permanent card", "nonland permanent card", "with mana value N or less", up to N targets; a move to the hand or the battlefield, the owner's), "Put target creature card from a graveyard onto the battlefield under your control." (a graveyard of any player, an opponent's or the defending player's; `Destination::Battlefield.under`), "Return all land cards from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.", "Exile target player's graveyard." and "You may put a land card from your hand onto the battlefield tapped." (a `May` of a `Choose` and a `Move`). "Return target creature card from your graveyard to the battlefield with a -1/-1 counter on it." (`Destination::Battlefield.counters`; a counter the rules give, such as lifelink or finality, stays with the table), "Return six target nonland permanents to their owners' hands." and "Exile two target creatures." (a count above one is `Bounds { min: n, max: n }`). The older two-trigger exile ("When ~ enters, exile target creature." with "When ~ leaves the battlefield, return the exiled card to the battlefield under its owner's control.") is read by `compile/linked.rs` as the one exile `until ~ leaves the battlefield`, the second trigger read into the first. "That many" is read only in a triggered or activated ability, where `Amount::Triggering` is valid.
- Activation restrictions (`compile/read/restrictions.rs`, written back by `activations.rs`, tests in `tests/restrictions.rs` and `activation_moments.rs` of `mtg-testkit`): the sentence "Activate only ..." after the effects of an activated ability is parts joined by "and only" in any order, each one "as a sorcery" (`timing`), "once each turn" (`limit`), "if" and a state of the game, or a moment of the turn: "during your turn" (`YourTurn`), "during your upkeep" (`All [YourTurn, Step(Upkeep)]`), "during an opponent's turn" (`Not(YourTurn)`), "during combat" (`InCombat`), "during the declare blockers step" (`Step(DeclareBlockers)`), "before attackers are declared" (`BeforeAttackers`: the turn has not reached its declare attackers step), also after a comma behind a moment ("during your turn, before attackers are declared"). The conditions read are one `condition`, an `All` when there are several, judged as the ability is activated. A count of the cards of a kind in a graveyard reads after "there are N or more" and "there are at least N" ("Zombie creature cards in your graveyard"). Another moment, "only once" and "only if a card left your graveyard this turn" stay with the table.
- Replacements under a condition and searches that split (`compile/read/instead_pairs.rs`, `exiled_kind.rs`, `library.rs`, written back by `render/instead.rs`): a sentence followed by "If C, instead S2." or "If C, S2 instead." is one `If` of the condition over the second sentence with the first as its `otherwise`, in a spell, a triggered and an activated ability; the sentences name no target (the targets a spell announces cannot depend on the condition), so a kicker or a "this spell was cast from ..." that changes the target stays with the table. The condition is read as after any "if": "this spell was kicked" (`Condition::Kicked`), "this spell's madness cost was paid", "the gift was promised", or how the game stands ("there are seven or more cards in your graveyard", "you control eight or more lands", "you're the monarch"). An ability word before a dash on a line of a spell ("Threshold — ") is flavor and the line reads without it. "Search your library for up to two basic land cards, reveal those cards, put one onto the battlefield tapped and the other into your hand, then shuffle." (also "and the rest", "and put" after the reveal and "Then shuffle." as a sentence) is a `Search`, a `Choose` of one of the cards found (`kept`, the others `rest`), a `Move` for each and the `Shuffle`. A list of cards names "basic land cards and/or Gate cards" as the land cards that are basic or of the land type (`[Land, AnyOf [Basic, Gate]]`), and "and/or" stands for "or" in a plural list of types or subtypes. "Exile target card from a graveyard. If it was a creature card, ..." is an `Exile` that binds the card (`exiled`) and an `If` of `Matches` on the binding, whose consequence is steps joined by "and" (also "and you gain 1 life"). A pick at random in a hand or a library stays with the table.
- Chance, votes and repeats (`compile/read/chance.rs`, `vote.rs`, `repeat.rs`, `compile/tables.rs`, written back by `render/chance.rs`, tests in `tests/chance.rs`, `votes.rs`, `repeats.rs`, `tests/it/chance_tables.rs`): "Flip a coin. If you win the flip, S. If you lose the flip, S." is a `FlipCoin` (the win branch before the lose branch, either or both left out; "flip a coin until you lose a flip" with no branch is a flip with `until`; "Whenever you win a coin flip" and "Whenever a player wins a coin flip" are `CoinFlipWon`; "that player flips a coin" is the player of the event); "Roll a dN." is a `RollDice` whose table the compiler fills from the lines after the clause ("1—9 | S", "20 | S", "15+ | S"): each row is read as a spell without targets and the totals rise, and one row that does not read leaves the roll and every row to the table; `check-rules` reads each row back against its own line (`die_row_texts`). "Starting with you, each player votes for A or B" (or "Each player secretly votes for A or B, then those votes are revealed") is a `Vote` on two one-word names followed by "If A gets more votes, S. If B gets more votes or the vote is tied, S." (an `If` on `Players(Bound(A))` against `Players(Bound(B))`) or by "For each A vote, S. For each B vote, S." (a `RepeatEach` over each name, the voter bound as `voter`). "For each opponent, S" and "For each player, S" are a `RepeatEach` whose steps read no "that player" and no target. "For each opponent, destroy up to one target artifact or enchantment that player controls." is one target specification of `Targetable::PerPlayer { players: Opponents, what }` (the objects of `what` each opponent controls, or owns outside the battlefield; at most one target of each, `Target.PerPlayer` in `Caps`) and the steps act on every target at once (`compile/read/c10_dice_targets.rs`, written back by `render/c10_dice_per_player.rs`, tests in `tests/c10_dice_targets.rs`); "target ... that player controls" without "up to one" and a second "that player" stay unread. "Each opponent sacrifices two permanents of their choice" is a `Sacrifice` with a count.

**Incubate.** The compiler reads "Incubate N." and "incubate X, where X is ..." of "you" (`Effect::Incubate`), also after a step joined by ", then" ("mill two cards, then incubate X"); the Incubator is the predefined token `incubator`, whose file has the Phyrexian back face. The reminder text of incubate names counters but does not keep the clause from being read (`normalize::COUNTER_ACTIONS`). "Its controller incubates X, where X is its mana value" (Excise the Imperfect) and "target player incubates N" are `Incubate` of another player, and Sunfall's "where X is the number of creatures exiled this way" is `Amount.Exiled`. The cast trigger of Brimaz, "a Phyrexian creature or artifact creature spell" (`compile/read/w14b_or_spell.rs`), is a creature that has the subtype or is an artifact (`Type(Creature)` and `AnyOf[Subtype, Type(Artifact)]`), and "if a Phyrexian died under your control this turn" is `Tallied` of `PhyrexiansDiedUnder`. Tests in `tests/w14b_incubate.rs`.

**Expend.** The compiler reads "Whenever you expend N" with N in digits (`Trigger::Expend`), and "an opponent expends" and "a player expends" too; the reminder text is dropped. A word for N stays with the table.
**Clash.** The compiler reads "Clash with an opponent." with an optional "If you win, S." (`Effect::Clash`, `compile/read/w9b_clash.rs`, written back by `render/w9b_clash.rs`), "~ deals N damage to that creature's controller" as the win branch for the one target of the sentences before, and "You S, then clash with an opponent. If you win, repeat this process." as the steps and a clash until a loss whose win branch repeats them. A lose branch, "repeat this process" with no steps and another player who clashes stay with the table.

**Clash wordings.** `compile/read/w11c_clash.rs` (written back by `render/w11c_clash.rs`) reads "Clash with an opponent, then return target creature to its owner's hand. If you win, you may put that creature on top of its owner's library instead." as a clash whose win branch may put the one target on top of its owner's library, then the return, which finds the moved creature gone (CR 400.7); and "If you win, creatures that player controls don't untap during the player's next untap step." as a `Continuous` `DoesntUntap` over the creatures `ControlledBy(Bound("clashed"))` until `NextUntapStep`.

**Clash with a named opponent and clash triggers.** `Clash.with` names the opponent instead of a choice: "clash with defending player" is `with: Defending` (`compile/read/w15c_clash.rs`, written back by `render/w15c_clash.rs`, tests in `tests/w15c_clash.rs`). "Whenever you win a clash" (also "a player", "an opponent") is `TriggerPattern::W15cClashWon { who }` (`Trigger.W15cClashWon`). "you may cast a spell from your hand with mana value 8 or less without paying its mana cost" (the limit after the hand, also after "Then") reads as the free cast of the other order.

**Crimes.** "Whenever you commit a crime" and "whenever an opponent commits a crime" read as `Trigger::W11cCrime { who }` (`Trigger.W11cCrime`), with "This ability triggers only once each turn." as its limit.

## Model-assisted reading

A language model writes the rules of a card and a deterministic checker accepts them only when they are valid, read back as the printed text and have a support state. The text of the card is the only ground truth; the checker never trusts the rules.

- `mtg_cards::proposal::check(json, &PrintedCard, &Library, &names, &Caps) -> Report`; `mtg-cli check-rules [--card NAME] [--bundle] [--verbose] [--write DIR] FILE...` (a file is named `<slug>.json` after its card unless `--card` names it; a bundle is one object from a card name to its rules).
- A proposal may leave out the oracle id, the names, the status and the fingerprints: they come from the card, and the status is derived from what is modelled (a declared status counts for nothing). A clause no ability stands for becomes a `Manual` ability, so the state is `Partial`; abilities are put in printed order. `--write` stores the completed rules in canonical form.
- `mtg-cli promote [--dry-run] [--partial] [--out DIR] FILE...` and `propose --promote [DIR]` (`mtg_cli::w2a_promote`) make corpus cards of proposals: one the checker accepts `Verified` (`Partial` with `--partial`) becomes `cards/<slug>.json` in canonical form, with the card's record from the cached Oracle Cards file, pretty-printed in its key order, as `tests/fixtures/corpus/<slug>.json`. A card with a corpus file, a card the compiler already makes `Verified`, a card an earlier file promotes, rules that name a card outside the corpus (a partner) and a card the cache has no record of are skipped with the reason; a dry run lists both.
- Yield over the 1,261 stored samples: 119 corpus cards (2 more accepted `Verified` name a partner outside the corpus), and the `Verified` precon cards go from 4,022 to 4,141 of 6,429 (64.4%; 80.7% of the copies).

| Check | How | Result |
|---|---|---|
| Valid | `Library::check_generated`: levels 1 to 3 (`Validation`) | The problems, each with its path |
| Reads back | The clauses of the printed text (`compile::compile`) are paired with the abilities by kind; a wording of `render_variants` equal to the clause by `same_text` reads, a keyword reads by the keyword reader, two statics under one condition, a line of Saga chapters and a Backup count as in the compiler, and a level, Class or Station block reads level by level (below) | Each clause: reads, differs (the closest wording is shown), unwritten (an ability stands for it and no writer exists; the construct is named by writing each part alone), manual or open; an ability with no clause is a stray |
| Support | `support` with `mtg_engine::caps()` | `Verified`, `Partial` or `Manual`, with the blockers |

The verdict is `Invalid` (a problem of the first check), `Mismatch` (a clause differs or an ability has no clause), `Unchecked` (valid and nothing contradicts the text, but a construct has no writer) or `Accepted`. Only an accepted proposal is vouched for. The 1,791 hand-written files are the calibration: 1,711 are accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial`, 77 unchecked, none invalid or mismatching (`proposal::no_hand_written_file_is_invalid_or_mismatches_and_the_state_is_the_librarys`). The writers invert the readers, so a card is checked only as far as the compiler's templates reach.

**Writers.** The checker compares the clause with `written_variants`: the wordings of `render_variants`, which the templates read (the compiler's own reads stay on those), and the wordings of the oracle text that no template reads. The latter are `compile/read/event_wordings.rs` ("you" after another player's event: "Whenever an opponent draws their second card each turn, you draw a card."; "Then" opening a sentence; "that creature" for the creature of an event; a characteristic after "equal to": "deals damage equal to its power") and the effect writers in `compile/read/render/`: divided damage (`divided.rs`: "deals 3 damage divided as you choose among one, two, or three targets", "any number of target creatures and/or planeswalkers", "up to X target creatures"), the exile of a graveyard (`graveyards.rs`: "exile target player's graveyard", "each opponent's"), a card put onto the battlefield from the hand (`put_onto.rs`: "you may put a land card from your hand onto the battlefield tapped"), damage by the creature of an event (`damage_by.rs`: "it deals ...") and a pump the player may choose (`may_have.rs`: "you may have other attacking creatures get +X/+X until end of turn"). A token has its keywords as a pump words them ("with protection from black"), `Pay` of life with an amount is "pay X life" with the amount named after it (", where X is its power"), and a card of a subtype in a zone ("Spirit card from your graveyard") and a nonland permanent card (the permanent types without land) have a phrase. Each wording has a golden in `compile/read/tests/written_effects.rs` with the near-misses that do not read back (a wrong player, count, quality or target). Of the 17 proposals written for the sample, none was accepted before the writers and 14 are after: 13 `Verified` corpus cards and Mask of Griselbrand, accepted `Partial` for `Cost.PayLife.Counted` and a corpus card once the engine plays it; Flaming Tyrannosaurus (a spell cast from anywhere other than the hand) stays unchecked.

The loyalty abilities (`render/loyalty.rs`: "+1: ...", "−3: ...", "0: ...", "−X: ..." with the sentences of the effects), an exile whose cards a later step counts (`amounts::for_each_text`: "put a +1/+1 counter on this creature for each creature card exiled this way", "For each artifact or land card exiled this way, create ..."), a card in its graveyard that pays to return (`from_graveyard.rs`: "if this card is in your graveyard, you may pay 1 life. If you do, return it to your hand"), a move to the hand that a basic land type changes to the battlefield (`instead.rs`: Emeria Shepherd) and the groups an earlier step named (`those.rs`: "each of those creatures", "each of them" for the attackers of a batch, "for each opponent you have", "put a +1/+1 counter on ~ and you gain 1 life") have goldens with near-misses in `compile/read/tests/model_writers.rs`. Of the sample of 40 stored precon cards that no file covers (seed 20261024), 14 had expressible rules; none was accepted before these writers and 4 are after (`Partial`: the clauses the DSL cannot say stay open), and of the named cards of the earlier samples 6 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus (Amphin Mutineer, Canoptek Scarab Swarm, Emeria Shepherd, Malanthrope, Master of Death, Monstrosity of the Lake).

**Effect writers (second round).** The writers `render/delayed.rs` (an exile of a target that a delayed trigger returns at the next end step: "Return that card to the battlefield under its owner's control at the beginning of the next end step"), `render/multi.rs` (a clause with several targets is written target by target, each one's effects as sentences of their own, the target of a later sentence named in full), `render/each_target.rs` (counters on two or three targets in one sentence, "put a -1/-1 counter on target creature, two -1/-1 counters on another target creature, and three -1/-1 counters on a third target creature", and a fight between two targets), `render/may_have.rs` (a choice of several steps, "you may draw a card and lose 1 life") and `payments.rs` ("... unless they sacrifice a creature of their choice") give effects their wording. A target that is exiled and bound is "exile target ...", and an amount of a characteristic of what it took is X named at the end ("..., where X is the mana value of the permanent exiled this way", `where_x.rs`). Counters on a player are "target player gets four rad counters", and on the defending player a condition says "as long as defending player has a rad counter". A trigger names a spell "cast from anywhere other than your hand", "your fourth noncreature spell each turn", the return of the card "from your graveyard to your hand" and "each of your postcombat main phases"; `facts.rs` words "you gained 3 or more life this turn" (the digits and the word both) and "a creature died this turn". `shortened.rs` words a Clue token as "investigate", "Return it" after an exile and "life equal to that card's mana value". A list of subtypes and card types ("Plant, Treefolk, or land card"), "without flying or islandwalk", "artifact creature" in the singular, a keyword counter of two words ("double strike counter"), the plural of Treefolk and Aetherborn and the first name of a legendary card ("Legolas" for "Legolas Greenleaf", `compile::first_name`) have a wording too. Each has a golden with near-misses in `compile/read/tests/effect_writers.rs`.

Samples are kept as `crates/mtg-cards/tests/fixtures/proposals/<sample>/<slug>.json`, the rules a model wrote before the checker's report; `mtg-cli check-rules --verbose proposals/<sample>/*.json` re-runs one. Of the 40 stored precon cards that no file covers and the earlier samples left out (`r2l`, seed 20261026: 10 of which the compiler reads nothing, 15 partial with one unread clause, 15 partial with two or more), 33 got rules (7 need a construct the DSL lacks). Before these writers 14 proposals were accepted (2 `Verified`, 12 `Partial`); after the second round of writers 23 are (10 `Verified`, 13 `Partial`, 6 of those with keywords only), and 26 after the writers of the blocks, loyalty abilities and keywords below (11 `Verified`, 15 `Partial`). The 11 `Verified` cards and the 10 of the earlier sample (`r2g`: Eureka Moment, Primaris Eliminator, Juri, Mogis, Nick Valentine, Nightkin Ambusher, Reaper from the Abyss, Roon, Ruinous Intrusion, Legolas Greenleaf) are corpus cards.

**Effect writers (third round).** `render/each_target.rs` and `multi.rs` write counters on targets that tell themselves apart by who controls them in one sentence without "another" ("put three +1/+1 counters on target creature you control and three +1/+1 counters on target creature an opponent controls"); `render/target_counts.rs` a draw for what each of two players controls ("another target player controls"); `render/switched.rs` a switch of power and toughness ("Switch target creature's power and toughness", "of each of any number of target creatures"); `render/revealed.rs` the top card revealed and put onto the battlefield, cast or put on the bottom, and a permanent its owner shuffles into their library before revealing the top card; `render/exiled_until.rs` an opponent who exiles until a card is found, the free cast and the move of the rest; `render/free_casts.rs` a free cast of a spell from the hand with a mana value limit; `render/untapped.rs` "Untap up to three lands"; `render/populated.rs` populate with a keyword the token keeps and a sacrifice at the next end step; `render/player_events.rs` "Whenever you cycle or discard a card"; `render/together.rs` the steps after "If you do" for different players joined by "and"; `render/and_lists.rs` "Merfolk and Druid cards in your graveyard"; and `changes.rs` gives a pump an ability in quotes after its keywords. The player of an event has "their hand" in a count, the sacrificed creature is `Ref::Paid`, and "that player or that planeswalker's controller" is a held-by pair on the first target. Each has a golden with near-misses in `compile/read/tests/target_pair_writers.rs`, `player_event_writers.rs` and `reveal_cast_writers.rs`. Of the 40 stored precon cards of A to L that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2s`, seed 20261029, one ability each), 21 got rules, 18 are accepted `Verified` corpus cards and 3 are valid and wait for the engine (two need `Condition.Performed` after a removal of counters) or for the sentences of a fight with a delayed trigger; before the writers 2 were accepted.

**Ability writers.** Written from the data only, each a small module with table goldens in `compile/read/tests/*_writers.rs`, which also list the near-misses that do not read back: `replacements.rs` writes the replacements of damage ("If a source would deal damage to an opponent, it deals double that damage to that player instead.", prevent, plus N), of tokens, counters and draws ("twice that many ... are put on it instead", "it puts twice that many ... on that permanent instead") and of a move between zones ("If a Warlock you control would die, exile it instead."); `batches.rs` the events of a group ("Whenever one or more other Elves you control enter", "... creature cards leave your graveyard", "... land cards are put into your graveyard from anywhere") and "Whenever you attack with one or more Birds" (also "Whenever you attack") with "each of them" and "those creatures" for the attackers; `choices.rs` the choice of modes ("choose one or both", "choose two", "choose up to one") with a mode's name before its text or not; `static_parts.rs` what a group or an Aura gives part by part (a bonus with the zero signed either way, lost keywords and abilities, base power and toughness, a type or subtype in addition to the others, "is goaded"); `spend.rs` restricted mana for a list of subtypes, a subtype with a card type, "and/or" between plural kinds and the abilities of the same kinds; `activations.rs` the sentence that limits an activation (a condition, "during your turn", "as a sorcery", "once each turn", in any order). A count over all opponents is written "your opponents control", never "an opponent controls", which is true for one opponent. These wordings are part of `render_variants`, so the compiler's own reads are checked against them too.

**Levels, Classes and Stations.** A block of the text (`LEVEL 2-3`, `{3}{U}: Level 2`, `8+ | Flying`) is one clause that reads into several abilities. `compile::block_plan` splits it into the condition its lines work under (the level counters in range, the level of the Class, the charge counters), a head (the level up ability of a Class, the size a level gives, the creature type of a Spacecraft) and the lines under the header; the compiler reads a block from the same plan. `proposal/blocks.rs` takes the head by value, takes the condition off every other ability that works under it (`compile::without_header`, the inverse of the reader, which also finds it beside an own condition) and writes each back as a line: a line of keywords is the keywords granted, any other line is a wording of the ability. A header, size, line or range that differs is a mismatch, an ability under the level that no line is left for is a stray, and an ability with no writer is the construct without one. Goldens are in `tests/it/proposal_blocks.rs` (the 20 hand-written leveler, Class and Spacecraft cards read back, with the near-misses) and `compile/read/tests/levels.rs`.

**Effect writers (third round).** `render/library_steps.rs` ("Look at the top card of target player's library. You may put that card on the bottom of that player's library.", "Exile all cards from target player's library, then that player shuffles their hand into their library."), `render/group_becomes.rs` ("Artifacts you control become artifact creatures with base power and toughness 5/5 until end of turn."), `render/put_onto.rs` ("return a creature card from your graveyard to the battlefield with a finality counter on it", also after "surveil 3, then") and the writers in `render.rs` and `cast.rs` word a counter put on "up to one target creature", counters removed from each object of a group, a restriction of the turn ("Target creature can't attack or block this turn."), the top card of the library exiled whatever a later step calls it, and a search whose card is called by its type. The name a single search binds is bookkeeping and may be any. `facts.rs` words a count taken for each opponent apart ("an opponent controls four or more lands", in a trigger, an activation limit and an "as long as" condition; a count of all opponents together is "your opponents control"), and the keyword reader takes a ward whose cost is in words ("Ward—Sacrifice a permanent with mana value 1 or greater."). Goldens with near-misses are in `compile/read/tests/loyalty_effect_writers.rs`, `return_and_restriction_writers.rs` and `per_opponent_writers.rs`; the loyalty abilities of Ajani, Caller of the Pride, Jace, the Mind Sculptor and Tezzeret the Seeker read back.

**Oracle-only writers.** `compile/read/render/oracle_only/` holds wordings that only the checker accepts (`written_variants`; the compiler reads none of them), each a module that derives the oracle text from the wordings of `variants` by the data of the ability, with table goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/oracle_only_writers.rs`: `conditions.rs` an intervening "if" after the event for a count of the cards of a hand or a graveyard ("if that player has no cards in hand", "if there are four or more card types among cards in your graveyard"); `wheres.rs` "where X is the power of the creature that died" and "where X is that artifact's mana value"; `land_types.rs` "are Forest lands in addition to their other types"; `non_lists.rs` "noncreature artifact or noncreature enchantment"; `each_of.rs` "deals X damage to each of up to three targets"; `either_event.rs` "Whenever you cycle or discard a card"; `entry_counters.rs` "enters with a +1/+1 counter on it for each Zombie card in your graveyard"; `exile_play.rs` "You may play cards exiled this way until the end of your next turn"; `look_split.rs` the top cards put into the hand, on the bottom and exiled (Expressive Iteration). Of the sample of 40 stored precon cards with a name from A to L that no file covers (seed 20261027, short cards first; the proposals are in `crates/mtg-cards/tests/fixtures/proposals/r2o/`), 1 was accepted before these writers and 12 are after, all `Verified` and in the corpus; 28 are not: 26 need a construct the DSL lacks and 2 need a keyword or counter writer.

**Event and subject writers.** `render/twins.rs` writes an ability with the plain event or subject and adds the words where the oracle puts them: "enters from a graveyard" (also "this creature or another permanent enters from a graveyard"), "enters untapped" for the entry of a land with the intervening "if" that it is untapped, "casts a spell from a graveyard", "this creature and land creatures you control get +2/+2" for a group that includes the card, "Each planeswalker you control enters with an additional loyalty counter on it" and "outlaws" for the five outlaw subtypes. `render/otherwise.rs` writes a step on a comparison of the creature of an event with the card and the step taken otherwise ("put a +1/+1 counter on that creature if its power is less than ~'s power. Otherwise, put a +1/+1 counter on ~."), `render/either.rs` a choice of two modes of one target as one sentence ("target creature gets +2/-2 or -2/+2 until end of turn"), a bigger step on a second count ("..., if a creature died this turn, you draw a card. If seven or more creatures died this turn, instead you draw seven cards.") is written from the two steps alone, and the card a trigger exiles after it died is "exile it". The goldens and their near-misses (another zone, count, relation, characteristic, group, target or duration) are in `compile/read/tests/twin_writers.rs` and `step_writers.rs`. Of 40 stored precon cards that start with M to Z and have no file (seed 20261028, the short ones first), 20 were written; 3 were accepted before these writers and 14 are after, each `Verified` and in the corpus (Moldgraf Monstrosity, Oath of Gideon, Olivia, Opulent Outlaw, River Kelpie, Shamanic Revelation, Shaper Parasite, Shelinda, Yevon Acolyte, Stensia Masquerade, Sylvan Advocate, Tallyman of Nurgle, Triarch Praetorian, Vashta Nerada, Warstorm Surge, Witch's Cottage). The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2p/`; what the other 26 need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Oracle-only writers (S to Z).** More modules of `render/oracle_only/`, each derived from the wordings of `variants` by the data of the ability, with table goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/sz_writers.rs` and `sz_more_writers.rs`: a draw by the controller of the creature of an event (`controller_draws.rs`), a cast from one's own graveyard (`your_graveyard.rs`), a search whose card is put onto the battlefield before a sentence of shuffling (`search_put.rs`), "and/or" between two creature types (`and_or.rs`) and "and draw" after counters (`and_draw.rs`), a doubled power (`doubled.rs`), "have this enchantment deal" (`have_deal.rs`), a basic land type given alone (`land_subtypes.rs`), "destroy all Humans" with ", then" (`destroy_all.rs`), a bounce of each object (`each_to_owner.rs`) and with an exception (`except_for.rs`), "Goad that creature." (`goaded.rs`), damage by a creature just put onto the battlefield (`target_damage.rs`) and to the controller of the target (`controller_damage.rs`), an amount less a number (`minus_amount.rs`), the intervening "if" that a card was cast from a zone (`cast_condition.rs`) or that the creature of the event has one size (`sized_condition.rs`), "this creature or another creature you control" (`creature_enters.rs`), two kinds of permanents each named (`each_and_each.rs`), the top cards looked at and sorted (`look_put.rs`), a target other than the source with the duration last (`other_than.rs`), an untap of lands (`untap_lands.rs`), "You create" after another step (`you_create.rs`), cards drawn for a characteristic of a target (`draw_equal.rs`), a count of a spell (`that_spells.rs`), a bonus "for each" card (`for_each_card.rs`), "is put into a graveyard from the battlefield" (`put_graveyard.rs`) and a sacrifice "of their choice" with counters (`of_their_choice.rs`). `render/exiled_cast.rs` words an opponent who exiles until a card is found and the cast of it for the turn; `facts.rs` words "an opponent lost 3 or more life this turn", "you attacked this turn" and "an opponent controls more lands than you"; "non-outlaw" is an adjective, and a creature that can't be blocked keeps its duration ("until your next turn", no longer "this turn"). Of the 63 stored precon cards of S to Z that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2x`, seed 20261102, the cards of one line first), 62 got valid rules and 49 are accepted `Verified`, of which 48 are corpus cards (the first 21 had 2 accepted before the writers). The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2x/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Piles.** `render/oracle_only/c5_dice_piles.rs` writes two piles of the top cards of a library ("Reveal the top five cards of your library. An opponent separates those cards into two piles. Put one pile into your hand and the other into your graveyard."): `LookAtTop`, `Reveal`, a `Choose` of an opponent by the controller (a choice among players binds players), a `Choose` of any number of those cards by that opponent with `rest_as` for the other pile, a `May` that puts the first pile into the hand and the other into the graveyard, and an `If` on `Not(Performed)` with the reverse moves. Fact or Fiction and Sphinx of Uthuun are Verified.

**Rewordings.** Four modules of `render/oracle_only/` (the `add_w4f` hook, applied last) reword the texts the other writers give, each an equally valid printing of the same ability: `w4f_sentences.rs` joins, orders or shortens sentences ("proliferate twice", "you lose 1 life and amass Zombies 1", "Choose one. Activate only as a sorcery." before the modes, "Exile ~ with three time counters on it.", "for the first time each turn" for a trigger limited to once each turn, "... until end of turn and can't be blocked this turn", "Choose a color. ... the chosen color", "Remove X time counters from ~ and exile it", two steps on the mana spent in one sentence, "put your choice of a +1/+1 counter or two charge counters on ..."); `w4f_counts.rs` counts by "for each" ("For each nontoken creature destroyed this way, you create ...", "Add {B} or {G} for each permanent destroyed this way."); `w4f_tokens.rs` names a token before its ability in quotes, calls it "this token" there and leaves out the size its ability sets; `w4f_pronouns.rs` gives the card of a person "he", "him" and "his" or "she" and "her" for "it" and "~". The renderers keep the capital of "Detectives you control" after a leading condition and word the mana of a tapped permanent "that permanent produced", which the compiler reads too. Goldens with near-misses are in `compile/read/tests/w4f_writers.rs`.

**Conditional steps.** `render/oracle_only/w4a_if.rs` writes each `If` of an ability as the oracle does: "If X, Y.", "Y if X.", ". Otherwise, Z.", "If you can't, Z." and "Then if X, Y." after another step; an `If` nested as the other branch is its own sentence ("If it was a creature card, ... If it was a land card, ... Otherwise, ..."), and two branches that create the same token differ by "create ten of those tokens instead" (`w4a_if_instead.rs`). Each `If` stands as a marker draw while the ability is written, and its steps are written in place between two markers, so "it", "that card" and "that land" read as in the ability. `w4a_if_conditions.rs` words the conditions ("you have at least 15 life more than your starting life total", "an opponent has more cards in hand than you", "you control a creature with the greatest power among creatures on the battlefield", "it was a land card", "this spell was cast from a graveyard", "that card's mana value is 4 or greater") and `w4a_if_steps.rs` the steps no renderer writes alone ("untap that land", "put a +1/+1 counter on a creature you control", "you may cast a Hero spell from your hand without paying its mana cost"). An "Otherwise" right after another conditional sentence is never written, since it reads as that sentence's other branch. Goldens and near-misses are in `compile/read/tests/w4a_if_writers.rs`; of the stored proposals, 10 more are accepted `Verified` (9 new corpus cards).

**Sample writers (M to R).** Wordings that only the checker accepts are modules of `render/oracle_only/`: `sacrifices.rs` ("Sacrifice a land.", "Draw two cards, then sacrifice a permanent."), `paid_steps.rs` ("If you do, put a +1/+1 counter on this creature and create a 1/1 blue Tentacle creature token."), `destroyed_this_way.rs` ("you may destroy another target creature. If a creature is destroyed this way, you gain life equal to its toughness."), `trailing_if.rs` (the condition of a step after it: "put a +1/+1 counter on each creature you control if you control a creature with power 5 or greater. Then you gain 10 life if ...", for any subset of the steps) and `target_and.rs` ("destroy up to one target creature and target player mills three cards"). Sentences with a writer of their own are `render/has_counter.rs` ("draw a card if that creature has a +1/+1 counter on it. If it doesn't, put a +1/+1 counter on it."), `compared_to_chosen.rs` ("Choose target creature you control. Destroy all creatures with power less than that creature's power."), `opponent_picks.rs` ("Reveal the top three cards of your library. An opponent chooses one of them. Put that card into your graveyard and the rest into your hand.") and `all_graveyards.rs` ("Put all creature cards from all graveyards onto the battlefield under your control."). The shared writers gain "up to X target ..." and "each of up to three target creatures" for counters, several objects of different kinds as one target ("target artifact, enchantment, or creature with power 4 or greater"), "twice X" tokens, "where X is the number of colors that spell is", the conditions "X is 5 or more" and "it's an Elf", "if you control no Snakes" in an intervening "if", "prevent all damage that would be dealt to you this turn" and "on top of its owner's library" for a card of any graveyard. `where_x` never turns an amount that a condition compares into X (`compared`). The goldens and their near-misses (another object, count, step, condition or place) are in `compile/read/tests/sample_mr_writers.rs`. Of the first 40 stored precon cards that start with M to R, have no file and no earlier sample and are not blocked by an open construct of the roadmap (seed 20261101, single-face cards with one ability first), 36 got rules (`tests/fixtures/proposals/r2w/`; Orim's Thunder, Otrimi, Nethroi and Revenge of Ravens need a construct the DSL lacks); 2 of the first 18 were accepted before these writers, and 26 of the 36 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus (Rot Hulk, Mana Cannons, Make Your Move, Noxious Gearhulk, Perilous Research, Mandate of Abaddon, Rise of the Dark Realms, Ophiomancer, Roiling Regrowth, Pest Infestation, Marcus, Nadir Kraken, Release to Memory, Mayael's Aria, Mister Fantastic, Murmurs from Beyond, Moldgraf Millipede, Necron Deathmark, Necropolis Regent, Noxious Revival, Protection Magic, Razor Hippogriff, Return Upon the Tide, Riot Control, Rogue Refiner, Martial Coup). What the other 10 need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Engine-gap writers.** `render/oracle_only/c6_eg.rs` writes "you may pay that much life" for life of the amount of the event in a payment (Niv-Mizzet, Ghost Counsel), with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/c6_eg_writers.rs`.

**Sample writers (names M to R, third sample).** Modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another pronoun, object, player, count, kind, place, zone or step) in `compile/read/tests/sample_mr3_writers.rs`. `standin.rs` writes an ability around a sentence a writer makes of its effects (the ability is written with one draw, and the sentence of the draw is replaced), which `owner_shuffles.rs` ("Target creature's owner shuffles it into their library.", "The owner of target nonland permanent shuffles it into their library, then draws two cards."), `graveyard_shuffle.rs` ("Shuffle any number of target cards from your graveyard into your library."), `bounce_all.rs` ("Return all nonland permanents target player controls to their owner's hand.") and `owners_graveyards.rs` ("Return all artifact and enchantment cards from all graveyards to the battlefield under their owners' control.") use; `tail_sentences.rs` adds a last sentence to the steps before it ("It deals damage equal to its power to each other creature.", "If a creature is dealt damage this way, it gets +5/+0 until end of turn."). `swaps.rs` is a table of the words a card uses where the renderers use `~` or "it" ("you may have it deal", "Tap it.", "That creature gains trample", "That creature's controller draws", "that player" for the controller of the creature of an event, "If it was an artifact", "sacrifice it unless", "Do this only once each turn."), each entry with the ability it applies to. The others: `all_non.rs` ("all non-Zombie creatures get"), `may_have_player.rs` ("you may have target player lose 3 life"), `energy_words.rs` ("Pay six {E}"), `damage_each_and.rs` ("to each creature without flying and each planeswalker"), `home_pair.rs` (two targets returned in one sentence), `flash_permission.rs` ("You may cast historic spells as though they had flash.", "Any player may cast Sliver spells ...", "As long as ~ is tapped, ..."), `uncounterable_group.rs` ("Creature spells you control can't be countered."), `have_cards.rs` ("if you have four or more creature cards in your graveyard"), `those_cards.rs` ("Put one of those cards into your hand"), `while_control.rs` ("attacks while you don't control another Dinosaur"), `delayed_first.rs` ("At the beginning of the next end step, return it to the battlefield tapped ..."), `channel_discard.rs` ("{U}, Discard this card: Draw a card."), `pay_two_costs.rs` ("you may pay {1} and 1 life") and `poison_pump.rs`. The shared writers gain "until the end of your next turn, you may play that card" after a discard (`discard_play.rs`), a payment after which the card returns from its graveyard (`from_graveyard.rs`), a return of "target creature or land card from a graveyard to its owner's hand" and a cost that returns "another creature you control"; an ability word of up to five words ("Curse of the Walking Pox") is flavor. Of the 637 stored precon cards of M to R that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2bc`, seed 20261123, short single-face cards first), 78 got rules; 49 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus (Mystic Confluence joined with the choice of modes repeated). What the rest needs is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Sample writers (M to R, second pass).** Modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only), each derived from the wordings of `variants`; `twin_json.rs` builds the plain twin of an ability on its JSON. `joined_steps.rs` joins and orders steps ("loses 3 life and you gain 3 life", "deals 1 damage to you and you draw a card", "proliferate, then proliferate again", "draws two cards, then discards two cards", "Draw a card if you control an artifact. Draw a card if you control an enchantment."), `paid_return.rs` a card that pays to return itself from its graveyard, `chosen_color_cast.rs` "a spell of the chosen color", `life_over_starting.rs` "at least 7 life more than your starting life total", `are_and_or.rs` "that are Zombies and/or tokens", `attach_to_it.rs` a return with "attach this Equipment to it", `search_up_to_x.rs` "up to X ..., where X is this creature's power", `discarded_was.rs` "If the discarded card was a creature card", `any_token_maker.rs` the tokens of any player, `per_spell_cast.rs` a token for each spell cast, `converge.rs`, `defending_mills.rs`, `repeat_modes.rs` ("Choose three. You may choose the same mode more than once."), `blink.rs` ("..., then return it to the battlefield under its owner's control"), `pump_and_unblockable.rs`, `no_max_hand.rs`, `triggering_pump.rs` ("that creature gets +2/+0 and gains haste until end of turn"), `as_it_enters.rs` and `shield_group.rs` (combat damage prevented "to Dogs you control"). Table goldens with near-misses (another count, zone, kind, player, group or duration) are in `compile/read/tests/sample_mr2_writers.rs`. Of the stored precon cards of M to R that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2ah`, seed 20261113, the cards whose construct a roadmap item lists left out), 57 got rules; 11 were accepted before these writers and 37 are after, all `Verified` corpus cards. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2ah/`; what the other cards need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Sample writers (names A to F).** Each is a module of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another stat, zone, player, count, kind, group, duration or event) in `compile/read/tests/sample_af_writers.rs`: `energy_amount.rs` ("You get an amount of {E} equal to its mana value."), `cast_graveyard.rs` ("Whenever you cast a spell from your graveyard", a spell you own cast from a graveyard), `copy_quotes.rs` (the ability a token copy has: "When this token enters, it fights ..."), `counter_any.rs` ("Counter target spell, activated ability, or triggered ability."), `unless.rs` ("Then discard a card unless you attacked this turn."), `discard_play.rs` ("you may exile that card from your graveyard. If you do, you may play that card this turn."), `one_or_more_attack.rs` ("Whenever one or more creatures attack"), `put_into_graveyard.rs` ("Whenever a nontoken creature is put into your graveyard from the battlefield", a card you own), `also_create.rs` ("If X is 10 or more, also create X 4/4 ..."), `exile_each.rs` (the top card of each player's library cast for free), `life_counters.rs` ("a +1/+1 counter ... for each 1 life you gained"), `commander_draw.rs`, `controller_may.rs` ("its controller may draw a card", "one of your opponents"), `return_damager.rs` ("return it to its owner's hand") and `double_counters.rs` ("double the number of each kind of counter on any number of target permanents"). `render/may_flicker.rs` writes the choice to exile the source and return it ("you may exile ~. If you do, return it to the battlefield under its owner's control", also as a quoted ability), `attack_pairs.rs` a static ability of a group and the rule that the group attacks as one sentence, `render/delayed.rs` the cards of any number of targets that return ("Return those cards ... under their owner's control"), and a search may put the cards "into your graveyard" (`library.rs`, which the compiler reads too). Of the stored precon cards of A to F that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2y`, seed 20261103, one ability each, in the order of the seed), 29 got rules; 2 were accepted before the writers and 21 are after, each `Verified` and in the corpus (Aggressive Biomancy, Aligned Heart, Ancient Stirrings, Avatar of Slaughter, Buried Alive, Burning Vengeance, Chart a Course, Commander's Insight, Containment Construct, Cradle of Vitality, Deepglow Skate, Disallow, Dissipation Field, Duelist's Heritage, Edric, Spymaster of Trest, Eerie Interlude, Electrosiphon, Estrid's Invocation, Etali, Primal Storm, Field of Souls, Finale of Glory); 6 are valid and wait for a writer or the engine and 2 are invalid for a missing construct. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2y/`; what the rest needs is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The 1,451 hand-written files before were 1,369 accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial` and 79 unchecked; the 1,472 after are 1,390, 3 and 79.

**Sample writers (names S to Z, third pass).** Each is a module of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another player, count, kind, zone, condition or duration) in `compile/read/tests/sz3_writers.rs`. Costs and casts: `sacrifice_unless.rs` ("sacrifice ~ unless you sacrifice a land"), `flash_casts.rs` ("You may cast green creature spells as though they had flash."), `target_costs.rs` ("Spells your opponents cast that target ~ cost {2} more to cast."), `cast_free.rs` (a free cast of an instant or sorcery from the hand in a trigger), `cast_from_hand.rs` ("If this spell was cast from your hand, create ...") and `kicked_enters.rs` ("When ~ enters, if it was kicked, ..."). Events: `battalion.rs` ("Whenever ~ and at least two other creatures attack", a group attack of three creatures you control under the condition that the source attacks), `graveyard_cards.rs` ("Whenever a land card is put into your graveyard from anywhere"), `dealt_damage.rs` ("Whenever you're dealt damage"), `exiled_from.rs` ("Whenever a creature is exiled from the battlefield"), `enters_and_step.rs` ("When this creature enters and at the beginning of your upkeep") and `put_that_card.rs` ("you may put that card onto the battlefield under your control. Do this only once each turn."). Steps: `half_tokens.rs` ("create half X Food tokens, rounded up"), `that_many_tokens.rs` ("Create that many ... tokens" after a return of up to N target cards), `paired_returns.rs` (two targets in one return), `destroy_that.rs` ("destroy that creature" for the creature that dealt the damage), `damage_back.rs` (damage equal to its power both ways), `choose_targets.rs` ("Choose any number of target creatures. Double the number of +1/+1 counters on each of them."), `choice_keywords.rs` ("gains your choice of reach, trample, or haste"), `attach_it.rs`, `spells_cast_damage.rs` ("where X is the number of spells you've cast this turn") and `own_permanent.rs` ("target permanent you own"); `each_of.rs` words "each of up to two target creatures and/or planeswalkers". Statics and replacements: `conditional_rule.rs` ("As long as an opponent controls more lands than you, you may play lands from the top of your library."), `trigger_again.rs` ("If a permanent entering causes a triggered ability ... to trigger, that ability triggers an additional time."), `plus_one_counters.rs` ("that many plus one of each of those kinds of counters"), `entry_sum.rs` (counters for two counts), `prevent_remove.rs` ("prevent that damage and remove a +1/+1 counter from it"), `at_least.rs`, `quote_commas.rs` (the comma inside a quoted ability), `lowercase_until.rs`, `return_this_card.rs`, `color_lists.rs` ("red, green, and white") and `nonlegendary.rs` ("nonland permanents that aren't legendary"). A doubled count is a `Sum` of two equal counts, which `where X is twice the number of ...` words. Of 50 stored precon cards of S to Z that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2bd`, seed 20261124, single faces, the ones the DSL can say first), 44 are accepted `Verified` and 43 of them are in the corpus (The World Tree, Wingmate Roc, Scourge of Valkas, Sludge Monster, Saruman the White Hand, Space Marine Scout, Storvald, Yawgmoth's Vile Offering, Siegfried, Unshakable Tail, Yeva and 32 more; Unbreathing Horde waits for the engine); the proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2bd/`; what the other cards need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Sample writers (names S to Z, fourth pass).** Modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another player, count, kind, zone, step, condition or duration) in `compile/read/tests/c4_sz_writers.rs`. `c4_sz.rs` is a table of writers, each wording the effects of one exact shape as a sentence after the head the renderers write: damage to "that player" and "destroy target nonland permanent that player controls" for the controller of what targeted a Dragon, "there is an additional beginning phase after this phase", "Each other player discards a card. You draw a card for each card discarded this way.", "Exile all other spells and counter all abilities.", the mana value of a commander you own as X, a card returned "at the beginning of the next end step", control of a tapped artifact "until the end of your next turn", "Each creature deals damage to itself equal to its power.", cards exiled from the library of the player of an event with a draw, a counter of four kinds of objects, "create your choice of a Clue token, a Food token, or a Treasure token", tokens that are copies of the card, a discard that sets a bonus ("where X is the discarded card's mana value"), "Then discard two cards unless you discard an artifact card." and a return that goes to the battlefield instead at a graveyard size (the ability word "Threshold —" may be left out of the second clause). `c4_twins.rs` writes a twin of the ability with one part changed and edits its wording: a static that works in the graveyard, a condition on the graveyard of an opponent, a prevention while the card is untapped, "top X cards" for a look, "nonbasic landwalk", a cast and an attack in one trigger, "When you cast your commander", "if no creatures died this turn", "that shares a creature type with it", a creature or a spell "of the chosen type", a mode that mills and puts a land and a mode that untaps the land of the event. `c4_swaps.rs` is a table of guarded swaps ("you may have it deal" for a cycled card, "is put into a graveyard from the battlefield" for a permanent that is not a creature, "that player loses life ... unless they sacrifice", "commander you own", "all permanents except for artifacts and lands", "during your upkeep", "a land you control enters tapped"), each applied to the wordings the swaps before it made. Of the stored precon cards of S to Z that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2cd`, seed 20261134, short texts first), 41 got rules and 39 are accepted `Verified` and 38 are in the corpus (Wolverine Riders, Treasure Nabber, Spellbound Dragon, Skyfire Phoenix, Sphinx of the Second Sun, Sanctum of Eternity, Trailblazer's Boots, Tomb Blade, Shuri, Wonder, Tiana, Species Specialist, Vanquisher's Banner, The Third Doctor, Summary Dismissal, Thirst for Knowledge, Thirst for Meaning, Syphon Mind, Stitch Together, Sister of Silence, Scourglass, Thunderstaff, Titan Hunter, Stinging Study, Slice and Dice, Silumgar's Command and 12 more; Soothsaying waits for the bot games); the proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2cd/`; what the other cards need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The 1,965 hand-written files before are 1,892 accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial` and 70 unchecked; the 2,003 after are 1,930, 3 and 70.

**Sample writers (names S to Z, fifth pass).** Two tables of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only), hooked by `add_c5_sz`, with goldens and near-misses (another count, kind, player, zone, duration or condition) in `compile/read/tests/c5_sz_writers.rs`; each entry is JSON the table parses once into the data types, so it matches whatever defaults a rules file leaves out. `c5_sz_sentences.rs` matches the targets and effects of an ability whole and words them as one sentence after the head the renderers write: "return this card from your graveyard to the battlefield", "draw a card, then put ~ on top of its owner's library", draws for each player who controls a creature with power 4 or greater, "you get that many {E}", cards exiled for life, damage then "destroy each creature you don't control that was dealt damage this turn", Clues that become 6/6 Plant creatures, a creature card chosen at random from an opponent's graveyard, Treasure offered to each opponent, "scry 1, then reveal the top card of your library", "defending player sacrifices a creature of their choice", "populate", counters with an unblockable attacker, "becomes a God in addition to its other types", an exile of all graveyards, lands searched by each player, chosen cards returned for a sacrifice, cards returned to an opponent's hand, a token whose size counts creatures, a land put from a graveyard, Witherbloom Command and Warping Wail, a nonbasic land destroyed and replaced, a mana ability "until your next turn", indestructible "for as long as you control ~" and "investigate twice". `c5_sz_wholes.rs` matches whole abilities whose head, cost or condition the renderers do not write ("if you have exactly thirteen cards in your hand", "Whenever you attack", "Whenever a card is put into an opponent's graveyard from anywhere", "can't be blocked and has shroud", lands that have a mana ability and lose all other abilities, counters for the cards an opponent drew, "You may cast this card from your graveyard.", "Return two lands you control", "Activate only if you've cast two or more spells this turn", "a red source you control", "another creature with a +1/+1 counter on it", "Whenever you create one or more creature tokens", "that's a Skeleton or Pirate", a creature with flying that attacks you and "if it didn't have decayed").
