Of the stored precon cards of S to Z that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2cn`, seed 20261144, the cards a roadmap item names set aside, those the DSL can express first), 54 got rules and 47 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus (Sergeant John Benton, Stone Idol Generator, Temur Sabertooth, Treebeard, Gracious Host, Vile Requiem, Spreading Insurrection, Thraximundar, Triskaidekaphile, Thought Sponge, Sphinx of the Revelation, Xerex Strobe-Knight, Swift Demise, Whispersilk Cloak, Tangletrove Kelp, Uyo, Silent Prophet, Trygon Prime, Tariel, Reckoner of Souls, Tyrite Sanctum, Sensei's Divining Top, Within Range, Shatter the Sky, Vulturous Zombie, Toxicrene, Trostani, Selesnya's Voice, Veteran Explorer, Skaab Ruinator, Vernal Sovereign, Tempting Contract, Suffer the Past, Spurnmage Advocate, Screams from Within, Thrasios, Triton Hero, The Red Terror, Ultimate Nullification, Victimize and 17 more); the proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2cn/`; what the other cards need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The hand-written files grow from 2,130 to 2,177.

**Sample writers (names S to Z, sixth pass).** Three modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only), hooked by `add_c6_sz`, with goldens and near-misses (another count, kind, player, zone, duration or condition) in `compile/read/tests/c6_sz_writers.rs`. `c6_sz_bodies.rs` matches the targets and effects of an ability whole and words them after the head the renderers write: a copy of a spell cast from a graveyard, an Aura revealed and put onto the battlefield or into the hand, "with toughness X or less", "up to X basic land cards", "a basic Plains card" with a choice by the lands of an opponent, a draw "if you have more life than an opponent", an artifact that becomes a creature with its mana value or as a 0/0 with four counters, a mill then a land returned, "add six {R}", damage to fliers then "Tap those creatures", "choose two" over four modes, "each player gains control of all creatures they own", two sacrifices by each opponent, a destroy "and put a +1/+1 counter on ~", "it and Zombies you control", a sacrifice whose failure creates a token, "a Food token or a Treasure token", "blight 1", a draw for the greatest power, a granted combat-damage draw and "It becomes a Mutant". `c6_sz_wholes.rs` matches whole abilities whose head, cost or condition the renderers do not write (the kicked returns and searches, "twenty or more growth counters", "at least 7 life more than your starting life total", "three or more spells this turn", a sacrifice "during your turn", "no Thopters other than ~", counters for red and green creatures, "unless it escaped", "each other player's draw step", "attack with your commander", the attack trigger doubled, the seven-card exile of a planeswalker, "Whenever you attack" with a group pump, "Unearth—Pay eight {E}.", an exile after an ability word in a chapter and a copy that is a 4/4 black Zombie). `c6_sz_and_you.rs` is parametrised: a wording "A. Then B" with B an instruction to the controller also reads "A and you B". Of the stored precon cards of S to Z that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2cz`, seed 20261154, the cards whose compiled reading lacks one clause first), 55 got rules: 13 were accepted with the earlier writers and 51 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus after these (Salvation Colossus, Sevinne's Reclamation, Shard of the Void Dragon, Shuri's Fabricator, Simic Ascendancy, Slinn Voda, the Rising Deep, Songbirds' Blessing, Soothsaying, Speaker of the Heavens, Spectral Deluge, Sporocyst, Stella Lee, Wild Card, Stoic Farmer, Summon: Ixion, Survival Cache, Szarel, Genesis Shepherd, Teval, the Balanced Scale, Thickest in the Thicket, Thopter Assembly, Titan of Industry, Trostani Discordant, Ulasht, the Hate Seed, Uro, Titan of Nature's Wrath, Vault 87: Forced Evolution, Well of Ideas, Wulfgar of Icewind Dale, Xenagos, the Reveler and 24 more); The Scarab God is accepted `Partial`. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2cz/`; what the other cards need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The hand-written files grow from 2,305 to 2,356.

**Sample writers (names A to F, second pass).** `render/af_steps.rs` writes the steps that need a sentence of their own, each a module of `render/af_steps/`: `defender_exiles.rs` ("defending player exiles two permanents they control"), `sacrifice_count.rs` ("Each player sacrifices two creatures of their choice."), `choose_rest.rs` ("Choose up to one creature. Destroy the rest."), `commander_back.rs` ("Put your commander into your hand from the command zone."), `flash_spells.rs` ("You may cast spells this turn as though they had flash."), `shuffle_graveyard.rs` ("Shuffle this artifact and your graveyard into their owner's library."), `reveal_hand.rs` ("... If it's a land card, put it onto the battlefield. Otherwise, put that card into your hand.") and the sentence of a group a target controls ("Tap all creatures target player controls.", "Draw a card for each tapped creature target opponent controls."), which names the target. `render/two_targets.rs` writes one effect between two targets ("Attach target Equipment you control to target creature you control.", "Target creature you control deals damage equal to its power to another target creature."). The modules of `render/oracle_only/af2/` derive the oracle wording from the wordings of `variants`: `owner_hand.rs` ("to their owner's hand"), `subtype_card.rs` ("If it's a Zombie card"), `that_controller.rs` ("that creature's controller may draw a card"), `have_lose.rs` ("you may have that player lose 1 life"), `chosen_inline.rs` ("Destroy all creatures with power greater than target creature's power."), `prevent_all.rs` ("Prevent all damage that would be dealt to creature tokens you control."), `all_others.rs` ("All other creatures get -1/-1 until end of turn."), `every_basic.rs` ("are every basic land type in addition to their other types"), `investigate_times.rs` ("Investigate three times."), `no_control.rs` ("if you don't control a Food") and `that_noun.rs` ("where X is that creature's mana value"). The goldens and their near-misses (another count, kind, group, player, order, zone or duration) are in `compile/read/tests/sample_af2_writers.rs`. Of the 342 stored precon cards of A to F that no file and no earlier sample covers (seed 20261111, short ones first), 58 got rules (`tests/fixtures/proposals/r2af/`); 47 are accepted `Verified` and 45 are in the corpus (Aetherize, Alchemist's Refuge, Angelic Chorus, Angrath's Marauders, Animal Sanctuary, Arcade Cabinet, Armistice, Aura Mutation, Bane of Bala Ged, Barter in Blood, Black Sun's Zenith, Blood Seeker, Blue Sun's Zenith, Borrowing 100,000 Arrows, Brass Squire, Branching Evolution, Broken Wings, Butterbur, Casualties of War, Cemetery Recruitment, City of Brass, Cloud of Faeries, Coiling Oracle, Command Beacon, Confirm Suspicions, Cruel Revival, Crush Contraband, Crystal Chimes, Dawnglare Invoker, Death Mutation, Deathreap Ritual, Dominate, Dryad of the Ilysian Grove, Duneblast, Electrolyze, Elixir of Immortality, Emmara Tandris, Entrancing Melody, Exorcise, Fall of the Hammer, Farewell, Fecundity, Fell the Mighty, Festercreep, Fiery Justice, Fissure Vent, Footbottom Feast); what the rest needs is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The 1,494 hand-written files before were 1,410 accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial` and 81 unchecked; the 1,541 after are 1,457, 3 and 81.

**Effect and oracle-only writers (fourth round).** `render/control.rs` writes a change of control ("Gain control of target creature until end of turn.", "Gain control of target Equipment.", "target opponent gains control of ~", "Untap all creatures and gain control of them until end of turn. They gain haste until end of turn."), `render/token_attach.rs` a token made with the Equipment of the sentence before attached to it ("..., then create a 0/0 black Phyrexian Germ creature token and attach that Equipment to it."), `render/optional_return.rs` an exile the player may undo at once or else at the next end step with counters ("You may return that card to the battlefield under its owner's control. If you don't, at the beginning of the next end step, return that card ... with a vigilance counter and a lifelink counter on it."), `render/hand_returns.rs` "Return all legendary creature cards from your graveyard to your hand." and a mill with the optional return ("Mill three cards, then you may return a creature or land card from your graveyard to your hand."), `render/shuffled_in.rs` "target player shuffles any number of target cards from their graveyard into their library", and `defining.rs` the count "the number of cards in that player's graveyard". The oracle-only modules `each_sweep.rs` ("Destroy each nonland permanent with mana value X or less.", "Destroy each creature that isn't all colors."), `itself.rs` (the card as "it" after its own tap, block or becoming blocked), `other_players.rs` ("each other player sacrifices a creature of their choice"), `put_into.rs` ("enters or is put into a graveyard from the battlefield") and `conditions.rs` ("if ~ is untapped" after a step) add their wordings. Goldens with near-misses are in `compile/read/tests/sample_gl_writers.rs`. Of the 40 stored precon cards of G to L that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2z`, seed 20261104, one ability each, the cards whose construct a roadmap item lists left out), 4 were accepted before these writers and 17 are after, all `Verified` (Kodama's Reach is a corpus card of another round); with the cards beyond the sample that the new writers also accept (Goblin Medics, Insurrection) the round adds 18 corpus cards. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2z/`; what the other cards need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Sample writers (names A to F, third pass).** Modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another amount, kind, player, event, duration, step or group) in `compile/read/tests/sample_ba_writers.rs`: `that_player.rs` ("Whenever a creature an opponent controls dies, that player loses 2 life."), `dealt_damage.rs` ("Whenever you're dealt damage"), `regen_this_way.rs` ("A creature destroyed this way can't be regenerated."), `kicked_trigger.rs` ("If it was kicked" in a triggered ability), `double_self.rs` ("double this creature's power until end of turn"), `energy_each.rs` ("you get {E} for each creature you control"), `energy_words.rs` ("you get six {E}"), `counter_ability.rs` ("Counter target activated ability.", also as an activated ability), `destroy_targets.rs` ("Destroy target artifact, target creature, target enchantment, and target land."), `counters_put.rs` ("Whenever you put one or more +1/+1 counters on this creature"), `counters_equal.rs` ("a number of rad counters equal to its power"), `counter_you_draw.rs` ("put a +1/+1 counter on ~ and you draw a card"), `except_lands_tokens.rs` ("all other permanents except for lands and tokens") and `controller_creates.rs` ("its controller may create a 1/1 colorless Sliver creature token"). The render modules `stolen.rs` ("Gain control of target creature an opponent controls until end of turn. Untap that creature. It gains haste until end of turn."), `held_down.rs` ("That creature doesn't untap during its controller's untap step for as long as you control this creature.") and `becomes_subtype.rs` ("Gain control of target creature. It becomes a Vampire in addition to its other types.") write sentences of several steps, and a discard of "another card" is a trigger wording (`triggers.rs`). Of the stored precon cards of A to F that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2ba`, seed 20261121, short ones first), 80 got rules, 38 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus (Access Denied, Act of Aggression, Aethertide Whale, Aetherwind Basker, Angel of Glory's Rise, Angel of the Dire Hour, Angelic Destiny, April O'Neil, Assault Intercessor, Assemble the Legion, Austere Command, Azorius Guildmage, Beacon of Unrest, Black Widow, Bloodsoaked Champion, Brood Sliver, Captivating Crew, Captivating Vampire, Conclave Mentor, Curator of Mysteries, Cyan, Damn, Darien, Dawn to Dusk, Decimate, Descend upon the Sinful, Desolation Giant, Devilish Valet, Disinformation Campaign, Dread Cacodemon, Dungeon Geists, Dusk Urchins, Eccentric Farmer, Elspeth Tirel, Exemplar of Light, Feed the Swarm, Feral Ghoul, Flickerwisp), and the others are valid and wait for a writer or the engine. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2ba/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). A `PartnerGroup` keyword is printed as `Partner—Character select` (April O'Neil).

**Sample writers (names A to F, fourth pass).** Each is a module of `render/oracle_only/c4_af/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another count, kind, player, group, zone or duration) in `compile/read/tests/c4_af_writers.rs`: `for_each_create.rs` ("For each land you control, create a Treasure token.", "..., for each hatchling counter on it, create a 1/1 black Insect creature token with flying."), `they_gain.rs` ("Put a +1/+1 counter on each legendary creature you control. They gain vigilance until end of turn.", also "Those creatures gain"), `where_per.rs` ("creatures you control get +X/+X until end of turn, where X is the number of cards in your hand", "Destroy each nonland permanent with mana value equal to the number of charge counters on this land."), `permanent_cards.rs` ("permanent card with mana value less than or equal to ~'s power"), `discard_kinds.rs` ("Whenever you discard a creature card, ..."), `graveyard_static.rs` ("As long as this card is in your graveyard and you control a Forest, creatures you control have trample."), `commander_own.rs` ("Commander creatures you own have ...") and `phrases.rs`, a table of swaps each with the rules it applies to ("creatures can't block this turn" for a group, "legendary permanent that's an artifact, creature, or enchantment", "That permanent's controller creates", "That player loses", "you and that player each draw that many cards", "it deals 4 damage divided as you choose", "You may play that card until the end of your next turn", "When a source an opponent controls deals damage to you, sacrifice this enchantment. If you do"). An ability word may end in "!" ("Exterminate! —"). Of the 58 stored precon cards of A to F that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2ca`, seed 20261131, the cards whose rules the existing constructs say first), 10 were accepted before these writers and 30 are after, each `Verified` and in the corpus (Accursed Duneyard, Aragorn, King of Gondor, Archfiend of Ifnir, Ardbert, Warrior of Darkness, Armory Paladin, Awaken the Sky Tyrant, Become the Avalanche, Blast Zone, Bloodtracker, Bone Miser, Bounty Agent, Brass's Bounty, Brawn, Brokers Hideout, Carmen, Cruel Skymarcher, Clan Crafter, Commander Sofia Daguerre, Coral Atoll, Cranial Plating, Crystal, Inhuman Princess, Dalek Drone, Dearly Departed, Denethor, Stone Seer, Diviner Spirit, Dormant Volcano, Double Vision, Eumidian Hatchery, Everglades, Felidar Retreat, Fury); 22 are valid and wait for a writer or the engine, 1 is accepted `Partial` and 5 need a construct the DSL lacks. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2ca/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The hand-written files grow from 1,965 to 1,995.

**Sample writers (names A to F, fifth pass).** The hub `render/oracle_only/c5_af.rs` (checker only; `add_c5_af` in `oracle_only.rs` runs it last) holds modules with table goldens and near-misses (another number, kind, player, zone, condition or group) in `compile/read/tests/c5_af_writers.rs`. `c5_af_twins.rs` is a table of twins: an ability with one part of its JSON swapped or its intervening "if" dropped, whose rendered wording gets the card's words back ("tapped and attacking", "twice X", "up to one target" for an attachment, "of the chosen type", "on top of your library" from a graveyard, "enters untapped", "an Eldrazi Scion", "spell or permanent", "two or more creatures", "for each time it was kicked", "where X is the amount of life you gained this turn", the lands of the defending player or of an opponent, "if its power is 6 or greater", "you may sacrifice it", "Target player searches" and a distribution among any number of targets). The others: `c5_af_look_pick.rs` ("Look at the top seven cards of your library. Put two of them into your hand and the rest on the bottom of your library in any order."), `c5_af_instead.rs` ("If you gained 7 or more life this turn, return that card to the battlefield instead."), `c5_af_difference.rs` ("if you have fewer than seven cards in hand, draw cards equal to the difference"), `c5_af_win.rs` ("When this artifact has twenty or more charge counters on it, you win the game."), `c5_af_returns.rs` ("When enchanted creature dies, return it to the battlefield under your control.") and `c5_af_swaps.rs`, a table of phrase swaps and rewordings each with the rules it applies to ("and it deals", "on that creature", "if it's tapped", "investigate X times", "any number of target", "and five loyalty counters", "other target", "Each of your opponents discards", "Regenerate it.", "Each Fungus and Saproling", "where X is the sacrificed creature's power", "Prevent all noncombat damage", "a number of +1/+1 counters on it equal to" and "When" for a trigger that returns the card). Of the 867 stored precon cards of A to F that no file, no earlier sample and no full compiler reading covers (`r2ck`, seed 20261141, the cards whose compiled reading lacks one clause first), 78 got rules and 69 are accepted `Verified`; 67 are in the corpus (Adric, Mathematical Genius, Aerith, Last Ancient, Aethersquall Ancient, Ajani Unyielding, Alesha, Who Smiles at Death, Banquet Guests, Benevolent Hydra, Celestine, the Living Saint, Chimil, the Inner Sun, Court Hussar, Damia, Sage of Stone, Darksteel Reactor, Dig Through Time, Dusk // Dawn, Everflowing Chalice, Everything Pizza, Expansion // Explosion, Fire Covenant, Frost Fair Lure Fish and 48 more), and Brallin, Skyshark Rider and Cazur, Ruthless Stalker wait for their partners. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2ck/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The hand-written files grow from 2,130 to 2,197.

**Sample writers (names A to F, sixth pass).** The hub `render/oracle_only/c6_af.rs` (checker only; `add_c6_af` in `oracle_only.rs` runs it last) holds two modules with table goldens and near-misses (another number, kind, player, zone, step or group) in `compile/read/tests/c6_af_writers.rs`. `c6_af_twins.rs` is one table of entries, each a list of JSON swaps and a list of word swaps. A twin swaps one part of the ability for a plain stand-in and gives the stand-in's wording, as the checker writes it, the card's words back ("a spell with cascade", "and each player", "you own in exile and in your graveyard", "from a colorless source", "its controller gains control of ~", "can't be blocked this turn except by creatures with haste", "becomes a 4/4 artifact creature until end of turn", "Creature spells you cast this turn can't be countered", "attacks alone", "while it's exiled", "of the chosen type", "for each color of mana spent to cast it", "enters from your graveyard" and "less than or equal to the amount of life you lost this turn"); a twin never holds the part its entry looks for, so the checker's own wordings of it never come back to the entry. A swap keeps the ability (each JSON part is its own stand-in, only a guard) and edits the wordings found so far ("and you scry X", "as you activate this ability", "Choose a color. Add one mana of that color for each charge counter", a granted ability "Until end of turn" and "When you do"). `c6_af_split.rs` words an ability with two targets and two steps, each step on its own target, as the first step with the ability around it and the second as a spell of its own ("... equal to the amount of life you gained this turn. Return up to one target creature card ..."). Of the 654 stored precon cards of A to F that no file, no earlier sample and no full compiler reading covers (`r2cy`, seed 20261151, the cards whose compiled reading lacks one clause first), 75 were looked at, 33 got rules and 26 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus (Abstruse Archaic, Act of Authority, Agility Bobblehead, Alibou, Ancient Witness, Altar of the Goyf, Armix, Filigree Thrasher, Artistic Refusal, Astral Cornucopia, Aurora Phoenix, Belisarius Cawl, Betor, Ancestor's Voice, Breath of Darigaaz, Canoptek Tomb Sentinel, Cleric Class, Crackling Drake, Crystalline Crawler, Cyberdrive Awakener, Death Tyrant, Dinosaurs on a Spaceship, Director Nick Fury, Dirge of Dread, Domri, Anarch of Bolas, Door of Destinies, Endurance Bobblehead, Firja's Retribution and Flayer of the Hatebound). The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2cy/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The hand-written files grow from 2,306 to 2,332.

**Link writers (r2t).** Four more modules of `render/oracle_only/` with goldens and near-misses (another land type, number, life, cost, step, counter kind, link or branch) in `compile/read/tests/performed_writers.rs`: `performed_steps.rs` words the steps after a `Sacrifice`, a `RemoveCounters` or a `Pay` as a spell of their own, so a search of several steps can follow the link ("Sacrifice it. When you do, search ..., put it onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffle and you gain 1 life.", "you may sacrifice a land. If you do, search ... up to two basic land cards ...", "remove all flood counters from ~. If no counters were removed this way, put a flood counter on ~ and draw a card. Otherwise, add {C}{G}{U}."), `sacrifice_search.rs` joins a sacrifice and a search into one sentence ("..., sacrifice this enchantment, search your library for up to two creature cards, put those cards onto the battlefield, then shuffle.") `first_main.rs` calls the precombat main phase "your first main phase" and `without_counter.rs` words a bounce of each object that holds no counter of a kind ("Return each creature without a +1/+1 counter on it to its owner's hand."). Obscura Storefront, Maestros Theater, Bounty of the Luxa, Springbloom Druid, Defense of the Heart and Wave Goodbye are corpus cards the checker accepts `Verified`; `tests/it/performed_after.rs` and `wave_goodbye.rs` play them at two, three and four seats and `tests/it/gap_fuzz.rs` is their fuzz deck.

**Gap writers (r2bi).** `Duration::WhileYouControlSource` is "for as long as you control ~" for an `Effect::Continuous` (`control.rs` words it and "for as long as ~ remains on the battlefield"; the layers derive the control of the source, `engine.md`, Durations). Modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another zone, group, noun, color, count, duration or step) in `compile/read/tests/c4_writers.rs`, `c4_group_writers.rs` and `c4_move_writers.rs`: `c4_group_effects.rs` (control of every object of a selector, "choose target ... For as long as ~ remains on the battlefield, gain control of that permanent, it loses all abilities, and it can't attack or block", a random creature taken with the rest destroyed, "double the power and toughness of each creature you control" as a `RepeatEach` over the group whose `ModifyPT` reads each creature's own stats), `c4_moves.rs` (a creature returned before a sweep, up to X lands from a hand and a graveyard, a search for a land with a basic land type) and `c4_token_counts.rs` (tokens for the creatures attacking you and for the artifacts of the chosen opponent); a devotion counts tokens in `create.rs` and "noncommander" is an adjective. A selector with `AnyOf` of `In(zone)` looks in each of those zones ("from your hand and/or graveyard"). The cards are Mind Flayer, Master Thief, Sower of Temptation, Opportunistic Dragon, Zealous Conscripts, Subjugate the Hobbits, Unnatural Growth, The Nipton Lottery, Time Wipe, Worldsoul's Rage, Nervous Gardener, Arachnogenesis, Curious Herd and Evangel of Heliod; `tests/it/c4_control.rs`, `c4_control_cards.rs` and `c4_move_cards.rs` play them at two, three and four seats and `tests/it/c4_fuzz.rs` is their fuzz deck.
**Changes of control (w6e).** The compiler reads "gain control of" a target (also up to one, or one of each opponent), the enchanted permanent or all of a group, for the turn, for good, "for as long as you control ~" or "for as long as ~ remains on the battlefield", with "Untap that creature. It gains <keywords> until end of turn." after a change for the turn ("those creatures" and "They gain" for a target of each opponent; `compile/read/w6e_control.rs`, written by `render/w6e_control.rs`). `Effect::ExchangeControl { what, duration }` is "exchange control of two target creatures" (one reference of two targets) or "of target land you control and target land an opponent controls" (two references). "You can't activate this ability during combat." is the condition `Not(InCombat)` of an activated ability. A trigger of "When ~ enters and at the beginning of your upkeep" writes back. Goldens and near-misses are in `compile/read/tests/w6e_control.rs`.

**Sample writers (S to Z, second pass).** Each is a module of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another player, count, kind, amount, group, order or event) in `compile/read/tests/sample_sz_two_writers.rs`: `each_sacrifice.rs` ("Each player sacrifices two creatures.", "Each player sacrifices three creatures of their choice. You create three Food tokens.", also "Each other player sacrifices a creature." in `other_players.rs`), `and_create.rs` ("..., put a +1/+1 counter on this creature and create a Treasure token."), `damage_instead.rs` ("~ deals 2 damage to target creature or planeswalker. Delirium — ~ deals 6 damage instead if there are four or more card types among cards in your graveyard."), `that_creature.rs` ("..., put a +1/+1 counter on that creature."), `flash_rule.rs` ("You may cast artifact spells as though they had flash."), `cant_countered.rs` ("Creature spells you control can't be countered."), `leaves_graveyard.rs` ("Whenever an enchantment you control is put into a graveyard from the battlefield, ..."), `spell_controller.rs` ("..., that player loses life equal to that spell's mana value."), `ferocious.rs` ("That creature also gains trample until end of turn if ..."), `each_draw.rs` ("You and target opponent each draw three cards.") and the card as "it" after being tapped for mana (`itself.rs`). The shared writers gain a base size beside an evasion ("has base power and toughness 7/7 and can't be blocked by creatures with power 2 or less", `static_parts.rs`), "Tap another untapped Rogue you control" (`costs.rs`), a search for "an artifact creature card" (`library.rs`), "for each creature that died this turn" (`defining.rs`) and "an opponent casts their first noncreature spell each turn" (`triggers.rs`). The checker shows the names a face calls itself by as `~` inside the written wording too (`proposal/pairing.rs`), so a token named after the card's first name reads back (`tests/it/proposal_names.rs`). Of the stored precon cards of S to Z that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2ai`, seed 20261114, 49 proposals), 12 of the first 25 were accepted before these writers and 41 are after, each `Verified`; 39 are corpus cards (Tuktuk the Explorer needs the calibration test to show a token name as `~`, Shabraz, the Skyshark needs its partner card in the corpus). The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2ai/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Sample writers (names G to L, second pass).** Each is a module of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another kind, count, group, place, player or cost) in `compile/read/tests/sample_gl2_writers.rs`: `died_each.rs` ("create a Treasure token for each nontoken creature that died this turn."), `cant_unless.rs` ("~ can't attack unless you control four or more artifacts.", the restriction under the opposite condition), `counter_pair.rs` ("put a +1/+1 counter on that creature and a +1/+1 counter on ~."), `choose_first.rs` ("Whenever an opponent attacks you, choose target creature attacking you. Put a stun counter on that creature."), `pump_untap.rs` ("creatures target player controls get +2/+2 until end of turn. Untap them."), `sacrifice_it.rs` ("sacrifice it unless you return an untapped Forest you control to its owner's hand."), `put_back_order.rs` ("look at the top three cards of your library, then put them back in any order.") and `destroy_and.rs` ("Destroy all creatures you don't control and all planeswalkers you don't control."). `render/flicker_targets.rs` writes the exile of targets and their return ("Exile two target artifacts, creatures, and/or lands you control, then return those cards to the battlefield under your control."), `pay_phrases.rs` a payment of mana and a sacrifice ("you may pay {2} and sacrifice a token. If you do, ...") or of a permanent returned to the hand, and the shared writers gain "each player gets a poison counter", "X plus 2 damage", "each creature that didn't enter this turn", "its madness cost was paid" (read too) and a card of any graveyard returned "to its owner's hand". Of the stored precon cards of G to L that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2ag`, seed 20261112, taken in the order of the seed), 36 got rules and 27 were left out for a construct the DSL lacks or a roadmap item; 7 were accepted `Verified` before these writers and 21 are after (Gallifrey Stands, Gadrak, the Crown-Scourge, Ghostly Flicker, Grave Scrabbler, Great Oak Guardian, Halimar Depths, Humble Defector, Ichor Rats, In Garruk's Wake, Infectious Inquiry, Jagged-Scar Archers, Jungle Basin, Juniper Order Ranger, Keiga, the Tide Star, Kill! Maim! Burn!, Killer Service, Kolaghan's Command, Let the Galaxy Burn, Liberty Prime, Recharged, Liliana, Death Wielder, Lulu, Stern Guardian), all in the corpus. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2ag/`; what the rest needs is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The 1,602 hand-written files before are 1,517 accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial` and 82 unchecked; the 1,623 after are 1,538, 3 and 82.

**Party, prepare and chosen-player wordings (c14a).** The compiler reads "the life lost this way" (`LifeLostThisWay`: Exsanguinate, Gray Merchant of Asphodel with its "where X is your devotion to black", Malakir Bloodwitch, Blood Tribute), "a player (an opponent) has one or fewer (no) cards in hand" (`c4_hands.rs`), "from your hand" after the spell of a cast trigger (`c4_cast_hand.rs`) and two events of one trigger, "When ~ enters and whenever ..." (`pattern` in `triggers.rs` reads `AnyOf` of the two, `c4_joined_events.rs` writes it); `render.rs` words "life equal to" any count and a token counted by the party ("for each creature in your party"). Writers that only the checker accepts, each with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/c4_party_writers.rs` and `c4_prepare_writers.rs`: the party roles joined by "and" and "and/or" (`c4_party_roles.rs`), counters on a group and "those creatures gain" (`c4_those_creatures.rs`), the damage of a creature repeated to the chosen player (`c4_chosen_damage.rs`), the two replacements of "to the chosen player or a permanent they control" as one sentence (`c4_chosen_pairs.rs`, beside the static pairs), an exile followed by a token copy of the card and "artifact and/or creature card" (`c4_exile_copy.rs`), counters on "each of up to X target creatures" (`c4_each_of_up_to.rs`) and a creature that prepares the card when it deals combat damage this combat (`c4_delayed_prepare.rs`). Twelve hand-written cards: Squad Commander, Burakos, Nalia, Tazri (party), Sawhorn Nemesis, Saskia the Unyielding (chosen player), Dirgur Focusmage, Inspired Skypainter, Lorehold Archivist, Naktamun Lorespinner, Striding Shotcaller and Stensian Sanguinist (prepare); the scenarios are in `mtg-testkit/tests/it/c4_party.rs`, `c4_prepare_triggers.rs` and `c4_combat.rs`, the fuzz deck in `c4_cards.rs`.

**Sample writers (names G to L, third pass).** Each is a module of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another count, player, kind, group, zone or duration) in `compile/read/tests/sample_gl3_writers.rs`: `kicked.rs` ("When ~ enters, if he was kicked, ..." with the card's pronoun, and "you gain 2 life for each time it was kicked"), `entered_under.rs` ("if a creature entered the battlefield under an opponent's control this turn"), `cycle_this.rs` ("When you cycle this card, you may have it deal X damage to target creature, where X is the number of Goblins on the battlefield", "you may have target player lose life equal to ..."), `counter_sacrifice.rs` ("Remove three quest counters from this enchantment and sacrifice it:"), `untapped_cost.rs` ("Tap an untapped legendary permanent you control:"), `group_verbs.rs` ("Regenerate each creature you control.", "All Zombies gain menace until end of turn."), `that_creature.rs` ("put a +1/+1 counter on that creature"), `each_draw.rs` ("You and target opponent each draw a card."), `spell_value.rs` ("with mana value less than or equal to that spell's mana value"), `do_this_once.rs` ("Do this only once each turn."), `attack_player.rs` ("attack a player, target attacking creature gains flying until end of turn") and `destroy_two.rs` ("Destroy target artifact and target enchantment."); the renderer no longer writes "sacrifice an another permanent". A search under "you may" is one `May` over the search, the move and the shuffle, and a search for one card leaves the count at its default. Of the stored precon cards of G to L that no file, no earlier sample and no compiler rule covers (`r2bb`, seed 20261122, shortest text first), 66 got rules, 35 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus (Galepowder Mage, Gargoyle Flock, Gempalm Incinerator, Gempalm Polluter, Goblin Bushwhacker, Golgari Charm, Good-Fortune Unicorn, Hammerhead Tyrant, Healing Technique, Hoard-Smelter Dragon, Honor-Worn Shaku, Hull Breach, Imperial Recovery Unit, Ior Ruin Expedition, Josu Vess, Kaervek the Merciless, Kami of Whispered Hopes, Kederekt Parasite, Keeper of Secrets, Key to the City, Khalni Heart Expedition, Knight of the White Orchid, Knights' Charge, Kor Sanctifiers, Korvold, Krenko, Tin Street Kingpin, Krile Baldesion, Land Tax, Landroval, Lictor, Lightkeeper of Emeria, Liliana's Devotee, Loran of the Third Path, Lord of the Accursed, Loyal Warhound) and 31 are valid and wait for a writer, a construct or the engine; 13 were accepted before the writers. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2bb/`; what the rest needs is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The 1,656 hand-written files before are 1,575 accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial` and 78 unchecked; the 1,691 after are 1,610, 3 and 78.

**Sample writers (names G to L, fourth pass).** Modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another count, player, kind, group, step or duration) in `compile/read/tests/c4_gl_writers.rs`. `c4_swaps.rs` is an ordered table of the words a card uses where the renderers write another, each entry with the ability it applies to and chained on the entries before it: "Each player draws a card, then discards a card.", "It can't be blocked this turn." after a counter, "for each Angel you already control", "other than their hand", the pronoun "he" of a legend, "Put one of them into your hand and the rest on the bottom of your library in any order.", "you may have this creature deal damage ... on it to target player", "you get {E}{E}, then you may pay eight {E}. If you pay, ...", "a 1/1 creature", "Investigate X times." and "If X is 5 or more, you draw a card." with the counters of a creature doubled "on any number of other target creatures". The others: `c4_support.rs` ("When this creature enters, support 2."), `c4_counters_you_put.rs` ("Whenever you put one or more -1/-1 counters on a creature"), `c4_prevent_combat.rs` ("Prevent all combat damage that would be dealt to attacking artifact creatures you control."), `c4_attack_token.rs` ("... token with flying that's tapped and attacking. Exile that token at end of combat.") and `c4_aura_becomes.rs` ("Enchanted permanent is a colorless Clue artifact with "{2}, Sacrifice this artifact: Draw a card" and loses all other abilities."). Of the stored precon cards of G to L that no file, no earlier sample and no compiler rule covers (`r2cb`, seed 20261132, short ones first, the cards of a roadmap leftover set aside), 62 got rules; 14 were accepted before the writers and 31 are after, 30 of them `Verified` corpus cards (Generous Patron, Geier Reach Sanitarium, Geist of Saint Traft, Giada, Gingerbread Cabin, Ghostly Pilferer, Goblin Razerunners, Graham O'Brien, Hapatra, Hellkite Charger, Here Comes a New Hero!, Hibernation Sliver, Honored Dreyleader, Hormagaunt Horde, Imotekh the Stormlord, Imprisoned in the Moon, Ingenious Smith, Iraxxa, Ishkanah, James, Wandering Dad, Kappa Cannoneer, Karoo, Killmonger, Kinetic Ooze, Kumena, Lightning Runner, Lone Revenant, Losheel, Lotus Field, Lovestruck Beast); Kher Keep is accepted and waits for the calibration test to show a token name that holds the card's name as `~`. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2cb/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Sample writers (names G to L, fifth pass).** Modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only) with table goldens and near-misses (another count, kind, player, step, zone, group or duration) in `compile/read/tests/c5_gl_writers.rs`. `c5_gl_twins.rs` is a table of twins: each swaps parts of the ability's compact JSON for parts the renderers write, writes the twin with every writer and swaps the twin's words for the card's ("if ~ has loyalty counters on it, you may move a loyalty counter from ~ onto that planeswalker", "except it's a Tyranid in addition to its other types", "Then that player mills X cards.", "return ~ to the battlefield at the beginning of the next end step", "you may exile any number of", "Then if that creature has three or more +1/+1 counters on it, transform ~.", "each Merfolk creature you control explores", "If you don't, draw a card.", "or Ugin planeswalker card", "that shares a creature type with it", the kicked step of Hunting Wilds, "of the chosen type", a creature put with haste and sacrificed at the next end step, "three mana of that type", "equal to the greatest number of creatures a player controls", "Double equipped creature's power", the forced attacks of Kardur, "~ or equipped creature" with "goad each creature", "You and permanents you control gain hexproof", afflict, "for each 1 life you gained this turn", "Whenever you attack a player" and a prevention after "if you control your commander"). `c5_gl_modes.rs` writes the modes of a triggered "choose one —" one by one, each as the trigger with the mode's steps alone, so the words of a whole ability ("where X is ...") reach a mode; `c5_gl_swaps.rs` swaps words of the ability's own wordings ("Sacrifice ~ and thirteen Blood tokens", "attached to target creature", "also get", "When you attack with") and names a token "tapped and attacking" before its kind. Of the 294 stored precon cards of G to L that no file, no earlier sample and no compiler rule covers (`r2cl`, seed 20261142, short ones first, the cards of a roadmap leftover set aside), 35 got rules; the others need constructs the DSL lacks. 4 were accepted before the writers and 31 are after, all `Verified` corpus cards (Gatewatch Beacon, Genestealer Patriarch, Geth, Ghalta and Mavren, Ghoulish Impetus, Glass-Cast Heart, Glorious Protector, Graaz, Gryff's Boon, Hadana's Climb, Hakbal of the Surging Soul, Harald, Haven of the Spirit Dragon, Heirloom Blade, Heroes' Podium, Hunting Wilds, Icon of Ancestry, Incandescent Soulstoke, Incubation Druid, Investigator's Journal, Jetmir, Junk Jet, Kardur, Komainu Battle Armor, Lazotep Plating, Lazotep Sliver, Legion's Landing, Leonardo, Licia, Long-Range Sensor, Loyal Unicorn). The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2cl/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Sample writers (names G to L, sixth pass).** Three modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only), hooked by `add_c6_gl`, with goldens and near-misses (another count, kind, step, zone, group, cost or duration) in `compile/read/tests/c6_gl_writers.rs`, whose goldens read the abilities of the committed proposals. `c6_gl_wholes.rs` is a table of whole abilities parsed once into the data types and compared whole ("If this spell was cast from a graveyard, copy that spell twice instead.", "Until the end of your next turn, you may play those cards. If this spell was cast from a graveyard, you may play cards this way without paying their mana costs.", Eldrazi Spawn for the cards of an impulse left in exile, "to a player or planeswalker", two token copies of a nontoken card, counters on each creature named ~, Desert lands played from a graveyard, a sacrifice followed by "When you do" and its damage, a cost and an upkeep look for the chosen type, "choose any number instead" for a kicked spell, a payment for a Dinosaur's damage, two of three cards to the hand, a hand discarded for the damage an opponent took, a land that becomes an X/X Hydra, counters distributed for the life gained, copies goaded for the rest of the game, a draw for a discarded land, "Choose an opponent." twice, Living Death and the modes of Incendiary Command and Grab the Reins). `c6_gl_returns.rs` writes a card returned from its graveyard tapped and attacking as you attack with your commander, legendary creatures or creatures, for a choice ("if this card is in your graveyard, you may return it") or a payment ("you may pay {2}{B}. If you do, return this card from your graveyard"); `c6_gl_causes.rs` adds a player drawing a card and a creature dying as causes of a rule that triggers abilities again. `Gingerbrute` is a predefined token. Of the 279 stored precon cards of G to L that no file, no earlier sample, no compiler `Full` reading and no roadmap item covers (`r2dc`, seed 20261162), 26 got rules: 2 were accepted before the writers and 26 are after, all `Verified` corpus cards (Ginger, Queen of Sweets, Glimpse the Impossible, Grab the Reins, Grateful Apparition, Gruff Triplets, Hazezon, Shaper of Sand, Heart-Piercer Manticore, Herald's Horn, Ignite the Future, Incendiary Command, Increasing Vengeance, Inquisitorial Rosette, Inscription of Abundance, Intellectual Offering, Interceptor, Shadow's Hound, Iroas, God of Victory, Itzquinth, Firstborn of Gishath, Jocasta, Automaton Avenger, Keskit, the Flesh Sculptor, Knollspine Dragon, Krang, the All-Powerful, Lair of the Hydra, Lathiel, the Bounteous Dawn, Life of the Party, Living Death, Lord Windgrace; the vanilla Gigantosaurus and Indomitable Ancients and the basic Island are `Verified` without a file); the others need constructs the DSL lacks. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2dc/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The hand-written files grow from 2,318 to 2,344.

**Sample writers (names M to R, fourth pass).** The hub `render/oracle_only/c4_mr.rs` (checker only; `add_c4_mr` in `oracle_only.rs` runs it last, so it edits the wordings of the other modules) holds one module per wording, each derived from the wordings of a plain twin of the ability (`restyled` changes the JSON, `twin_variants` renders it) with table goldens and near-misses (another number, object, player, kind or step) in `compile/read/tests/sample_c4_writers.rs`: `twice_x.rs` ("Each creature gets twice -X/-X until end of turn."), `foretold.rs` ("If this spell was foretold, scry 2."), `converge_draw.rs` ("You draw X cards and lose X life, where X is the number of colors of mana spent to cast this spell."), `copy_returned.rs` ("Copy target instant or sorcery spell, then return it to its owner's hand."), `return_self.rs` ("{2}, Return ~ to its owner's hand:"), `switch_each.rs` ("Switch each creature's power and toughness until end of turn."), `copy_it.rs` ("create a token that's a copy of it" or "of that artifact" for the permanent of an event), `graveyard_return.rs` ("..., you may discard two cards. If you do, return this card from your graveyard to your hand."), `counter_check.rs` ("Then if there are three or more ribbon counters on this creature, remove those counters and untap it."), `until_first.rs` ("..., if you control three or more Dragons, until end of turn, ~ becomes a Dragon ..."), `sacrificed_stats.rs`, `poison_loss.rs` ("each opponent who has three or more poison counters loses 1 life"), `hand_size_draw.rs`, `graveyard_limit.rs` ("Activate only if an opponent has eight or more cards in their graveyard."), `free_cast.rs` ("You may cast target instant or sorcery card from an opponent's graveyard without paying its mana cost."), `devotion_if.rs` ("if your devotion to white and black is seven or greater"), `last_known.rs` ("if its toughness was less than 1"), `command_zone.rs` ("if ~ is in the command zone"), `explores_it.rs` ("it explores") and `power_plus_toughness.rs`. `swaps.rs` is a table of words with the rules each applies to, one entry per line ("its controller" for the controller of an attacker, "equipped creature", "When ~ deals combat damage ..., sacrifice it and create", "When this token dies" inside a token's quote, "choose any number", "Creatures can't be blocked this turn", "if you don't control a Pest creature token"). Of the 555 stored precon cards of M to R that no file and no earlier sample covers (`r2cc`, seed 20261133, shortest text first), 53 got rules and 37 are accepted `Verified` and in the corpus (March of the Canonized, Massacre Girl, Known Killer, Memory Plunder, Merfolk Windrobber, Mind's Desire, Mirrorworks, Momentous Fall, Narset's Reversal, Nogi, Draco-Zealot, Nuclear Fallout, Obsessive Skinner, Old One Eye, Oloro, Ageless Ascetic, Omnath, Locus of the Roil, Ondu Spiritdancer, Painful Truths, Parasitic Impetus, Path of Discovery, Pentavus, Pest Rescuer, Phoenix Down, Phthisis, Phyrexian Atlas, Poison the Cup, Port of Karfell, Prize Pig, Purestrain Genestealer, Rankle, Master of Pranks, Recurring Insight, Reverse the Polarity, Ring of Thune, Ring of Valkas, Rise of the Eldrazi, RMS Titanic, Rootha, Mercurial Artist, Royal Talon Fighter Jet, Rubblehulk). The 1,965 hand-written files before are 1,891 accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial` and 71 unchecked; the 2,002 after are 1,928, 3 and 71. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2cc/`; what the rest needs is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage).

**Sample writers (names M to R, fifth pass).** The hub `render/oracle_only/c5_mr_writers.rs` (checker only; `add_c5_mr` in `oracle_only.rs` runs it last) derives each wording from the wordings the checker writes for a plain twin of the ability (`c5_mr_twin` changes the JSON) or from the ability's parts, with table goldens and near-misses (another number, object, player, kind, zone or step) in `compile/read/tests/c5_mr_writers.rs`: `c5_mr_moves.rs` ("When this creature dies, put it on the bottom of its owner's library.", "each player may discard their hand and draw seven cards", "Exile ~. Return it to the battlefield tapped under its owner's control at the beginning of the next end step.", "is put into a graveyard from the battlefield or is put into exile from the battlefield", "that player exiles the top card of their library. If it's a nonland card, you may cast it without paying its mana cost." and "Return two lands you control to their owner's hand" as a cost), `c5_mr_sizes.rs` ("double target creature's power and toughness until end of turn", "+1/+1 for each card in its controller's hand", "+1/+1 for each land you control and each land card in your graveyard", "If a Dinosaur is dealt damage this way, ~ gets +2/+0 until end of turn.", "deals 4 damage to each of X targets", "up to X target creature cards ..., where X is the number of times ~ was kicked" and "then it deals X damage to any target, where X is the number of Hamsters you control"), `c5_mr_grants.rs` ("Until end of turn, those creatures gain "Whenever this creature deals combat damage to a player, draw a card."", "except it has "At the beginning of your upkeep, if this creature isn't a token, create a token that's a copy of this creature."" and "Equipment you control have equip {0} as long as ..."), `c5_mr_events.rs` ("each player sacrifices a creature or planeswalker of their choice. Each player who can't discards a card.", "At the beginning of the draw step of enchanted creature's controller, that player draws an additional card." and "~ deals 1 damage to that player" or "to that creature's controller" for the controller of the permanent of an event) and `c5_mr_swaps.rs`, a table of words with the rules each applies to ("Creatures can't block this turn", "power less than ~'s", "When ... sacrifice this enchantment. If you do", "return that card to the battlefield", "they create", "That player draws", "You add", "investigate that many times", "creatures attacking your opponents and/or planeswalkers they control", "deals 2 damage to it"). Of the 501 stored precon cards of M to R that no file, no earlier sample and no compiler `Full` reading covers (`r2cm`, seed 20261143, the cards whose rules the DSL can say first, the cards of a roadmap leftover set aside), 49 got rules and 31 are accepted `Verified`, 30 of them in the corpus (Marauding Raptor, Marshal's Anthem, Meteor Blast, Michelangelo, the Heart, Mind's Dilation, Mirko, Obsessive Theorist, Multani, Yavimaya's Avatar, Murderous Rider, Necroskitter, Nezahal, Primal Tide, Nylea's Colossus, Open into Wonder, Order // Chaos, Pain Distributor, Piper Wright, Publick Reporter, Plaguecrafter, Progenitor Mimic, Promise of Bunrei, Psychomancer, Puresteel Paladin, Pyrogoyf, Red Death, Shipwrecker, Righteous Authority, Riku of Two Reflections, Riveteers Overlook, Road of Return, Roar of Resistance, Rolling Hamsphere, Rootcast Apprenticeship, Ruin Grinder); Merry, Warden of Isengard waits for its partner in the corpus. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2cm/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The 2,130 hand-written files before are 2,056 accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial` and 71 unchecked; the 2,160 after are 2,087, 3 and 70.

**Sample writers (names M to R, sixth pass).** The hub `render/oracle_only/c6_mr_writers.rs` (checker only; `add_c6_mr` in `oracle_only.rs` runs it last) derives each wording from the wordings the checker writes for a plain twin of the ability (`c6_mr_twin_all` changes the JSON) or for its parts, with table goldens and near-misses (another player, turn, count, zone, cost, condition or number of modes) in `compile/read/tests/c6_mr_writers.rs`: `c6_mr_events.rs` ("Whenever a creature ... attacks you, its controller ..." from the attack of anyone, "Whenever you cast your first spell during each opponent's turn" from the second spell each turn), `c6_mr_looks.rs` ("Look at the top X cards of your library, where X is ..." from a look at three cards and a draw of X cards, "You may cast a spell with ... from among them without paying its mana cost" from the card put into your hand, "and the other into your graveyard" from the other on the bottom), `c6_mr_moves.rs` ("exile ... and copy it. You may cast the copy without paying its mana cost.", "If you do, [a step] and [the next steps]" joined from the first step and the others as a spell, "Target player returns a ... card from their graveyard to their hand", "from your hand or graveyard onto the battlefield" and "you may play that card without paying its mana cost"), `c6_mr_modes.rs` ("Choose three. You may choose the same mode more than once." with each mode written as a spell of its own in every wording the checker accepts) and `c6_mr_swaps.rs` ("each creature and planeswalker you don't control", "Activate only if there are N or more ... counters on ~" and "deals damage equal to ... to ..." for "deals X damage to ..., where X is ..."). Of the 378 stored precon cards of M to R that no file, no earlier sample, no compiler `Full` reading and no open roadmap item covers (`r2dd`, seed 20261163, 45 cards whose reading lacks one clause and 15 others), 15 got rules: 3 are accepted `Verified` with the writers before and 13 with these, all 13 in the corpus (MacCready, Lamplight Mayor, Marionette Master, Muzzio, Visionary Architect, Noise Marine, Nymris, Oona's Trickster, Oath of Eorl, Obscura Confluence, Of Herbs and Stewed Rabbit, Oracle's Vault, Perception Bobblehead, Redcap Gutter-Dweller, Renegade Bull, Riveteers Confluence); Nikara, Lair Scavenger, Pako, Arcane Retriever and Rory Williams wait for their partners in the corpus. The proposals are in `tests/fixtures/proposals/r2dd/`; what the others need is in the roadmap (M6, Coverage). The 2,331 hand-written files after are 2,258 accepted `Verified`, 3 accepted `Partial` and 70 unchecked.

**Census writers (r2dg).** `mtg-cli check-rules` runs over every proposals directory against a scratch database. The hub `add_c10_cen` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs `c10_cen_land_look.rs` ("look at the top card of your library. If it's a land card, you may put it onto the battlefield tapped": `LookAtTop` bound by a name, then an `If` on that card being a land with a may-move of it tapped) and `c10_cen_power_damage.rs` (a `DealDamage` whose amount is the power of its own source: "~", "it" or "equipped creature deals damage equal to its power to" the renderer's recipient, "that player" or "the player or planeswalker it's attacking"), with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/c10_cen_writers.rs`. The corpus test shows a face's own names as `~` inside a written wording, as the checker does (a token named after the card, a type in the card's name). The 24 corpus cards are Deny the Witch, Desperate Ravings, Gonti's Aether Heart, Mold Shambler, Naya Charm, Necrotic Hex, Shabraz, the Skyshark, Brallin, Skyshark Rider, Tuktuk the Explorer, Time Beetle, Joraga Warcaller, Kher Keep, Mask of Griselbrand, Reiver Demon, Sliver Hivelord, Stitch in Time, The Mouth of Sauron, Too Greedily, Too Deep, Raiders' Karve, Explorer's Scope, Fiendlash, Gleeful Arsonist, Lothlórien Blade and Mage Slayer.

**Tail writers, names M to Z (r2dge).** `render/oracle_only/c11g_wholes.rs` (checker only, hooked by `add_c11g`) matches 39 whole abilities of the stored proposals as data and words each as the card prints it ("When you cycle this card, counter target activated or triggered ability you don't control."); the goldens and one near-miss per ability (another count, kind, player, zone, step, condition or keyword) are in `compile/read/tests/c11g_writers.rs`. An ability word is stripped from a clause only when it stands on the first line, so the name of a mode ("• Disarm — ") stays. The proposals left are model errors (a wrong duration, owner or counted event) or need a primitive: a choice between tapping and untapping one target, a replacement that names the replaced event, and X paid in a cycling cost.

**Delayed triggers and repeated steps (r3w4).** The hub `add_w4d` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs `w4d_later.rs` and `w4d_each.rs`, which share `w4d_parts.rs`. A closing `DelayedTrigger` is worded as a triggered ability of its own, its object swapped for `~` and named back ("it", "that creature", "that spell's", or the target itself when nothing named it before): a step reads as "the next end step" or "your next end step" in front or at the end of its sentence, an event takes "When" or "Whenever" from `once` and "this turn" from `this_turn`, and a keyword granted for good before it is its own sentence ("That creature gains haste."). A closing `RepeatEach` over each player or each opponent is written for you and said of the group in the third person ("Each player searches their library ..., then shuffles"); a step under a condition on that player reads "each opponent who has three or more poison counters loses 3 life" or "Then for each opponent who has less life than you, create ...", and a may-step with a `Performed` follow-up reads "each player may draw a card, then each player who drew a card this way gains 1 life". The goldens and near-misses are in `compile/read/tests/w4d_writers.rs`.

**Trigger heads and conditions (r3w8).** The hub `add_w5a` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs three checker-only modules, with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/w5a_writers.rs`. `w5a_heads.rs` writes the ability with a stand-in event the checker words (the same subject, a plainer event) and swaps in the head derived from the trigger: "Whenever an opponent discards a noncreature, nonland card", "attacks one of your opponents or a planeswalker an opponent controls" (the attacker named back as "that creature"), "becomes the target of a spell", "deals combat damage to a creature", "Whenever you attack with one or more Zombies", "one or more time counters are removed from ~", the command zone as a destination, a land that enters from anywhere other than your hand, and an `AnyOf` whose events say their shared opening words once ("~ deals combat damage to a player or planeswalker", "a creature you control dies or is put into the command zone"); an intervening "if" on the damage dealt is "deals 5 or more damage", and counters of the triggering object an "if it had one or more" condition names are "that many". `w5a_combat.rs` words a destroy or exile of the damaged creature at end of combat, and `w5a_conditions.rs` the conditions "if that player controls more lands than you" and "if another Human entered the battlefield under your control this turn".

**Step leftovers (r3wa).** The hub `add_w5c` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs compositional writers (checker only), with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/w5c_writers.rs`. `w5c_trigger_again.rs` writes `TriggerAgain` as "If <event> causes a triggered ability of a permanent you control to trigger, that ability triggers an additional time.", the event being the head of a trigger on the cause with its verbs in the "-ing" form. `w5c_later.rs` says a delayed trigger at the next end step or at end of combat after its effect ("destroy that creature at end of combat"), and a trigger on damage to a kind of permanent as the one on damage to a player with that kind in its place. `w5c_damage.rs` writes an intervening "if" that compares the damage dealt with a number as "deals 5 or more damage", and a source selector with no kind as "a source you control". `w5c_costs.rs` puts "that target <phrase>" after the caster of a cost change and words it for activated abilities ("Abilities your opponents activate ... cost {2} more to activate."). `w5c_cast_while.rs` writes a permission to cast under a condition as "... as long as <condition>", a count of two kinds also with "and/or". `w5c_player_counters.rs` writes counters put on a player as "you get an experience counter" or, after damage to a player, "that player gets two rad counters", in place of the step so the steps around it read as in the ability.

**Player counters by word.** `compile/read/w7b_player_counters.rs` reads the counters a player gets by word after the subject: "you get two rad counters", "each player gets a rad counter", "target player gets X rad counters", "each opponent gets a poison counter", "they get four rad counters" and "that player gets that many rad counters" in a trigger on damage to a player. Each is a `PutCounters` on the player of a kind a player holds (rad, poison, experience; energy keeps its symbol); the renderer writes "you get" and "that player gets", with "they get" as a variant. The engine plays the rad rule (`docs/engine.md`, Rad counters).
**Local-model shapes (r4b).** The hub `add_w8c` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs compositional writers (checker only) for the shapes the stored `gpt-oss:20b` proposals (`tests/fixtures/proposals/r4a/`) hit, with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/w8c_writers.rs`. Most write a twin of the ability that the renderers word and change the words: `w8c_snow.rs` writes the snow supertype as basic and swaps the word; `w8c_on_top.rs` writes the top of a library as its bottom, also under a lone "you may"; `w8c_kind_controller.rs` writes a target of one card type as a permanent and names both by the type ("that land's controller"); `w8c_opponent_graveyard.rs` writes a card an opponent owns without the owner and says "put into an opponent's graveyard"; `w8c_other_turns.rs` writes `Not(YourTurn)` as `YourTurn` and says "During turns other than yours"; `w8c_spell_colors.rs` writes a spell of any of several colors with the first and says "a spell that's white, blue, black, or red"; `w8c_prevent_next.rs` writes a shield of the next N damage this turn as N damage to the same recipient and turns that sentence into "Prevent the next N damage that would be dealt to <recipient> this turn". The others join sentences the renderers write apart: `w8c_damage_and.rs` two amounts of damage by the source ("deals 4 damage to any target and 2 damage to you"), `w8c_return_both.rs` two returns to the hand ("Return this creature and target creature you control to their owner's hand"), `w8c_then_tap.rs` a tap or untap of the target the earlier effects named ("and untap it", "Untap it."), `w8c_then_discards.rs` a discard by the owner of a returned target ("Then that player discards a card."), and `w8c_still_kind.rs` words a group that becomes creatures of a size as "All lands become 2/2 creatures until end of turn. They're still lands." Of the 117 stored proposals the checker did not accept, 18 are accepted `Verified` and corpus cards after these writers.

**Local-model shapes (r4d).** The hub `add_w12a` runs the next writers for the proposals of `proposals/r4c/` and the r4a leftovers, with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/w12a_writers.rs`. `w12a_that_target.rs` splits the effects of an ability with one plain target into a first part that names the target and later sentences that say it again ("Tap target creature. This creature deals damage equal to its power to that creature.", "Regenerate it.", "That player discards two cards."); `w12a_their_graveyard.rs` writes an exile from a target player's graveyard as the controller's own and puts it in the third person ("Target player exiles all land cards from their graveyard."); `w12a_exactly.rs` writes a count equal to a number as at least that number and says "exactly seven"; `w12a_is_this.rs` writes the source named through a selector (`{"Is": "This"}`, a sacrifice whose filter is the source) with the plain forms; `w12a_it_self.rs` says "it" for the source after a trigger on the source ("Whenever this creature becomes blocked, untap it."); `w12a_next_upkeep.rs` says "the next turn's upkeep" for a delayed trigger at the next upkeep of anyone; and `w12a_minus_zero.rs` signs the zero of a penalty to power ("-2/-0"). Of the 229 stored r4a and r4c proposals, 18 were accepted `Verified` before these writers and 32 are after; the 14 new ones are corpus cards.

**Local-model shapes (r4d-b, r4b-b).** The hub `add_w13a` runs the third wave of writers for the r4a and r4c proposals, with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/w13a_writers.rs`. Each changes the data into a form the renderers word and swaps the words back: `w13a_greater.rs` writes a count greater than a number as at least the next number ("If you control a Giant"); `w13a_those.rs` writes a last step on the group of creatures the step before named on the source and says "those creatures" ("Untap those creatures."); `w13a_their_hand.rs` writes a count of a target player's hand or graveyard as the controller's own and says "that player's hand"; `w13a_either_quality.rs` writes a target with a choice of two colors or of power and toughness with one comparison and says "that's green or white" or "with power or toughness 4 or greater"; `w13a_itself.rs` writes a target that deals damage to itself with a second target and says "deals damage to itself equal to its power"; `w13a_was_color.rs` writes a condition that the target creature is one of two colors with the first alone and says "If that creature was green or white"; `w13a_only_one.rs` writes a static on the creatures you control while you control exactly one with a count of at least one and says "that creature"; `w13a_sacrifice_all.rs` writes a sacrifice of as many as its filter counts for one and puts it in the plural ("sacrifice all non-Ogre creatures you control"); `w13a_named.rs` writes creatures of a name with a subtype no card has and says "Creatures named Rock Lobster". `w13a_shuffle_zone.rs` words a whole zone of the controller shuffled into the library after the head of the ability ("Shuffle your graveyard into your library."). Of the 229 stored proposals, 32 were accepted `Verified` before these writers and 43 are after; 10 of the new ones are corpus cards and Paper Tiger names a card outside the corpus.

**Local-model shapes (r4d-d, r4b-d).** The hub `add_w16a` runs the fourth wave of writers for the r4a, r4c and r4e proposals, with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/w16a_writers.rs`. `w16a_entry_if.rs` writes an entry with counters without its condition and adds the condition after "if" ("This creature enters with a +1/+1 counter on it if you attacked this turn."; a count greater than a number is also said as at least the next number); `w16a_state_trigger.rs` writes a state trigger with the condition as after "as long as" ("When you control no enchantments, sacrifice this creature."); `w16a_static_and.rs` writes the first modification of a static ability and the others apart and joins them where they name the same subject ("Enchanted creature gets +2/+2 and can't attack."); `w16a_comma_then.rs` joins a sentence that opens with "Then" by ", then". The others change the data into a form the renderers word and swap the words back: `w16a_defending.rs` the defending player for each opponent ("defending player discards a card"), `w16a_owner.rs` the owner of the target for its controller ("Its owner gains 4 life."), `w16a_return_cost.rs` the card returned to its owner's hand for its sacrifice as the cost, `w16a_color_or.rs` and `w16a_named_yours.rs` a subtype no card has for a group of one of two colors ("a white or blue creature you control") or for creatures you control with a name ("Each creature you control named Pack Mastiff"), and `w16a_damage_counters.rs` the prevention of damage to the card for counters put on it ("put that many +1/+1 counters on it instead"). `w16a_enchanted_control.rs` writes "You control enchanted ..." for each kind of permanent an Aura enchants, since the Enchant keyword names the kind. Of the 385 stored r4a, r4c and r4e proposals, 43 were accepted `Verified` before these writers and 58 are after; 14 of the new ones are corpus cards and Pack Mastiff names itself, a card outside the corpus.

**Mill triggers and the rad leftovers.** `compile/read/w8b_milled.rs` reads "whenever a player mills a nonland card" and "an opponent mills" into `Milled` and "whenever one or more nonland cards are milled" into a batch of it, with the card named by kind alone. "if this creature is in your graveyard, you may return it to your hand" after the event makes the ability work from the graveyard (`from: Graveyard`), where the card returns itself. `compile/read/w8b_then.rs` reads two imperative sentences the card joins with ", then" ("draw a card, then put a +1/+1 counter on this creature") when the second opens with put, create, mill, scry or surveil, and "you get three rad counters and put three +1/+1 counters on ~", as the sentences in order; the renderer writes the joined wordings as variants. "You gain life rather than lose life from radiation." is `GameRule::RadiationGainsLife(You)`.

**Mill and rad leftovers (w13b).** `compile/read/w13b_mill_rad.rs` reads "whenever a player mills one or more creature cards" into a batch of `Milled` with `per_player`, so the player who milled is a subject and the batch triggers once for each player; "put a +1/+1 counter on each of up to X target creatures, where X is the number of nonland cards milled this way" after a batch of mills as targets bounded by `Amount::Triggering` (the writer names the cards of the trigger); "Do this only once each turn." after a lone may as the trigger limit of once each turn; "you may get two rad counters"; "target land card in a graveyard that was milled this turn" (`Pred::MilledThisTurn`); "You may have this land enter tapped. If you do, you get two rad counters." (`ReplaceBy::TappedForCounters`); "for each rad counter you have" (`Amount::Counters` on you) in a cost change; and "target player loses all rad counters" (a `RemoveCounters` of the count they hold). A word with a capital inside it ("RadAway") is a name where a sentence opens, so it reads as a mode name.

**Single-wording tail, names A to L (r2dge).** The hub `add_c11f` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs `c11f_wholes.rs`, a table of 98 whole abilities of the stored proposals parsed once into the data types and compared whole, each worded as its card prints it; the goldens of `compile/read/tests/c11f_writers.rs` read every entry from its proposal file and swap the event, player, kind, zone, duration or first number for a near-miss that does not read. An entry is taken only when the ability means the text: proposals that drop a part of the text (a planeswalker recipient, an exile after the effect, a timing limit), sum where the text asks for one player, or name another duration stay rejected. A spell ability reads the lines the compiler takes for statics ("Metalcraft — If you control three or more artifacts, exile that creature.") when no wording reads its spell lines alone and one reads all of them, and the X of an `{X}` in the mana of an `Effect::Pay` is the X its paid effects read ("you may pay {X}{R}. If you do, it deals X damage to any target").

**Choices and payments (r3w3).** The hub `add_w4c` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs `w4c_may_pay.rs` (checker only): an ability whose one effect is `May`, `Pay` or a step then `May` is worded as its head (the stand-in draw of `standin.rs`) and "you may S", "S, then you may S", "you may pay {C}. If you do, S", "S unless its controller pays {C}" or "Then that player S unless they discard a land card". When the paid steps use a target the payment is also worded "When you do, S", since a reflexive trigger chooses its targets once the cost is paid (CR 603.12); with no target only "If you do" reads. The steps S are the wordings of the steps as a spell of their own, or the pieces of `w4c_bodies.rs`: a card put from the hand or graveyard onto the battlefield (tapped and attacking that opponent), an exile and return of a target, a returned card with base power and toughness and a keyword until end of turn, base power and toughness equal to the source's, damage to itself, "can't be blocked this turn except by", "counter that spell or ability" and "Those permanents ... their controllers' next untap steps" for up to N targets. Goldens and near-misses (another count, kind, player, zone, cost, duration or keyword) are in `compile/read/tests/w4c_writers.rs`.

**Continuous-effect writers (r3w2).** The hub `add_w4b` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs compositional writers (checker only) that write an ability with a known-good stand-in for one part and swap the stand-in's words for the part's (`w4b_stand.rs`): a static condition of the card under "as long as" (`w4b_conditions.rs`: "it attacked this turn", "you've cast two or more noncreature spells this turn", "~ has four or more counters on it", "an opponent has three or more poison counters"), a keyword the keyword list does not read, protection from each of your opponents and "can't be blocked this turn except by Spirits" (`w4b_grants.rs`), "~ and Drakes you control each get" and "where X is the amount of life you gained this turn" (`w4b_pumps.rs`), a permanent that becomes a creature with its changes in any order and "It's still a land." for a land that keeps its types (`w4b_becomes.rs`, CR 205.1b), an effect without end or "Until your next turn, those creatures can't block" (`w4b_durations.rs`), permanents kept tapped through their controllers' next untap steps (`w4b_untap.rs`) and a card given to a chosen opponent (`w4b_control.rs`). Goldens and near-misses (another number, kind, player, keyword, subtype, duration or condition) are in `compile/read/tests/w4b_writers.rs`.

**Other printed wordings, names M to Z (r3w7).** The hub `add_w4g` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs two checker-only modules, with goldens and near-misses in `compile/read/tests/w4g_variants.rs`. `w4g_amounts.rs` writes "Add an amount of {C} equal to the number of time counters on ~" beside "Add {C} for each time counter on ~", and "Double all [combat] damage that <sources> would deal" for a replacement that doubles the damage of a group of sources to any recipient. `w4g_texts.rs` rewrites the written wordings: a quoted ability that ends its sentence without a second period, "with flying, menace, and "..."", a token "named N with "..."", "with mana value equal to Y" and "Create a number of <tokens> equal to Y" for "X, where X is Y" when X appears nowhere else, "each creature [you control] with a counter on it" and "all opponents' graveyards", "put a counter on it" after "Whenever enchanted (equipped) creature attacks", "When you sacrifice ~", "If this spell was kicked, it deals" when no later "its" follows, and "they control" once "they" names the player of a trigger. The corpus cards are Circle of Power, Necroplasm, Ordeal of Nylea, Orim's Thunder, Persistent Constrictor, Pink Horror, Raphael, the Muscle, Rotating Fireplace, The Thirteenth Doctor, Wake the Dragon, White Sun's Twilight and Will of the Mardu.

**Move, counter and mode writers (r3w5).** Modules of `render/oracle_only/` (checker only), hooked by `add_w4e`, each deriving the sentence from the data of the effects, with table goldens and near-misses (another number, counter kind, player, zone, subtype or condition) in `compile/read/tests/w4e_writers.rs`. `w4e_counters.rs` writes "remove all counters from target permanent" (joined to a next step by "and"), counters distributed among "one or two target creatures", "each of up to two Soldiers you control", several kinds of counters listed on one target ("Choose target creature. Put four +1/+1 counters, a flying counter, and a lifelink counter on that creature.") and "each creature you control that's a Zombie or Mutant". `w4e_moves.rs` writes "one, two, or three target creature cards from graveyards" with "Each of them enters with an additional -1/-1 counter on it", "that player chooses up to two creatures they control, then sacrifices the rest", "defending player loses 1 life for each card in their graveyard", "target permanent phases out", "Return target spell to its owner's hand.", a token that gets counters ("Put X +1/+1 counters on it") and a random discard before the shuffle of a search. `w4e_where_x.rs` writes a count of counters as X named at the end of its sentence ("where X is the number of counters on that creature", "the number of counters it had on it", "the total number of rad counters among players", "the number of growth counters on ~"). `w4e_modes.rs` writes a choice of modes with every wording `written_variants` gives each mode, a label before a dash and "choose one. If you control a commander, you may choose both instead.". `w4e_if.rs` splices an intervening "if" after the event for the conditions `as_long_as` words ("if ~ is tapped", "if ~ has ten or more luck counters on it", also "there are ten or more luck counters on ~"; a count of charge counters is a Station line and gets none) and for "it had a -1/-1 counter on it", "it had counters on it" and "a player lost 4 or more life this turn". `w4e_flash.rs` writes "You may cast sorcery spells and Dragon spells as though they had flash." Over the stored proposals they turn 20 `Unchecked` proposals into accepted `Verified` corpus cards (1,158 to 1,178 accepted `Verified` of 1,456), and the `Verified` precon cards go from 4,194 to 4,214..

**Cast permissions, rules, mana and cost targets (r3w9).** The hub `add_w5b` of `render/oracle_only.rs` runs checker-only writers that take their nouns from known-good stand-ins (`w5b_parts.rs`: a selector named by "Destroy target ...", a graveyard target by "Return target ... from your graveyard to your hand"). `w5b_casts.rs` words a permission to cast that opens the effects: cards exiled from the top of a library ("exile the top seven cards of your library. Until end of turn, you may cast spells from among them", "exile that many cards from the top of your library", "the top card of that player's library. You may cast that card for as long as it remains exiled") or a target card in your graveyard ("you may cast target Zombie creature card from your graveyard this turn", "choose target artifact card in your graveyard. You may cast that card this turn"), with later effects as sentences of their own. `w5b_rules.rs` names the cause of a rule that triggers abilities again as the trigger's event in the "-ing" form ("a creature you control dealing combat damage to a player", "you casting or copying an instant or sorcery spell") and puts a rule's condition after "as long as" ("you control three or more tapped Pirates and/or Vehicles"). `w5b_mana.rs` writes "Add an amount of {B} equal to Y" with Y the words of a gain of life of that amount, "one mana of any type that a Gate you control could produce" and "Abilities your opponents activate that target a Merfolk you control cost {2} more to activate". `w5b_damage.rs` says a condition on the damage dealt in the event ("Whenever a source you control deals 5 or more damage to a player"). Goldens and near-misses (another count, player, zone, duration, kind or condition) are in `compile/read/tests/w5b_writers.rs`.

**Pilot.** Forty stored precon cards that no hand-written file covers, chosen by a seed over the oracle ids (20261023): 10 of which the compiler reads nothing, 15 partial with one unread clause and 15 partial with two or more. A model wrote each card's rules with no new template and no engine change and fixed a rejected file at most twice. The outcomes:

| Pool | Cards | (a) accepted `Verified` | (b) accepted `Partial` | (c) no writer | (d) DSL gap | (e) invalid or mismatching |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Nothing read | 10 | 0 | 0 | 5 | 5 | 0 |
| One unread clause | 15 | 1 | 0 | 6 | 4 | 4 |
| Two or more | 15 | 0 | 0 | 4 | 10 | 1 |
| All | 40 | 1 (2.5%) | 0 | 15 (37.5%) | 19 (47.5%) | 5 (12.5%) |

- Effort: 9 model turns for the 40 cards. 22 cards were written (the other 18 lack a construct, which the enum lists show); 21 of those 22 were valid, 2 after one fix (a cost part is `{"Mana": ...}` in a list) and 1 invalid for a missing construct. Of the 16 proposals in (a) and (c), 14 are `Verified` by the engine and 2, which need `Cost.PayLife.Counted`, `Partial`.
- (a) Winged Boots, stored with its fixture; the mechanics it uses (an Equipment granting flying and ward, Equip) have scenario tests already. The 15 in (c) are valid and kept as notes.
- Without a writer, by cards: `CreateToken` (3, with a chosen player, a counted number or a bound result); `Move`, `Pay` with life, `May` and `DealDamage` (2 each, the damage divided or beside a monarch); one each: `Exile` of a graveyard, `Continuous` on a count, `If` after tokens, a labelled mode of a trigger, restricted `AddMana`, a batch trigger, a static over `Each`, `Goaded`, a replacement of damage or of a zone change, an activated ability with a condition.
