- Wording the writers do not produce (5 in (e), the rules are right): "you draw" after an opponent's event, "each of those creatures", "Whenever you attack with one or more X" with "on each of them", "deals damage equal to its power" and "nonland permanent card" with "this creature's power".
- DSL gaps, by cards: a condition on what happened this turn ("the first time each turn" for any event, "a counter was put on it this turn", "dealt damage to you this turn") (3); one each: exactly X targets (`Bounds.min` is a number, so "X target creatures" has no form), "from anywhere other than the battlefield", a token with characteristic-defining stats, a named option chosen as it enters, Rooms, a total mana value limit on chosen cards, a controller that differs for each affected object, a cost whose X is the discarded card's mana value, "must be blocked if able", an object that becomes foretold, a duration "until you exile another card", counting counters removed, phasing, granting suspend, a choice of one of each type for each player, mana restricted to the chosen color.
- Missing capability: `Cost.PayLife.Counted` (2 cards).

**Proposer.** `mtg-cli propose` does what a pass above does by hand, for any number of cards: it asks a model for the rules of a card and keeps the proposals the checker accepts. The person who runs it supplies the API key and pays.

- `mtg-cli propose (--cards NAME... | --sample N [--seed S] [--letters A-L] [--precons]) [--provider anthropic|openai] [--base-url URL] [--model ID] [--no-json-mode | --schema] [--max-answer-tokens N] [--max-requests N] [--max-input-tokens N] [--examples 3..5 | --solved-examples K [--examples-train-only]] [--out DIR] [--resume] [--dry-run]`. A sample takes stored cards with no hand-written file that the compiler does not make `Verified`, short ones first (buckets of 100 characters, then the seed); `--precons` keeps only the cards the stored precon decks name on any board, in the same order. The default model is `claude-sonnet-5-5`.
- The system prompt is the instructions and `mtg-cli dsl-reference`: the type tree and its vocabulary, generated from the Rust sources of the types (`mtg_cards::reference::dsl_reference`), about 60 KB or 20 thousand tokens, limited to 80 KB by a test; it is the same for every card and carries a cache marker. The card's message holds its name, layout, keywords and, per face, cost, type line, stats and oracle text, then the 3 to 5 hand-written cards nearest by shared words, word pairs, keywords and type line (cards with the same rules count once, long rules weigh less) with their rules.
- Each card: ask, run `proposal::check` in the process, and after an `Invalid` or `Mismatch` send one repair round with the verdict and the first problems or the first clause that reads back differently. `Unchecked` is not repaired: no wording of the model gives the construct a writer.
- Accepted proposals with something modelled (`Verified` or `Partial`) are saved as `<out>/<slug>.json`, the completed rules `check-rules --write` writes; the others as `<out>/rejected/<slug>.json` (the model's last JSON) and `.txt` (the report). `--out` defaults to `proposals/propose` in the cache directory. `--resume` skips cards with any saved file; deleting a file asks again.
- The key is read from `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` only, held in a type whose `Debug` hides it, scrubbed from every error and sent in one header; it is never printed or written. 429 and 5xx answers are retried (four tries, the wait doubles from 2 s, `Retry-After` is honoured); a refused key or model (400, 401, 403, 404) ends the run, and so do five cards in a row without an answer.
- Cost: a request is about 20 thousand input tokens, almost all of it the cached system prompt, and a card takes one request, two with a repair. `--max-requests` (default 100) and `--max-input-tokens` (default 3 million, cached tokens in full) are hard: a request is not sent when it would pass either, judged by an upper estimate of 3 bytes a token, and the run ends with the summary table (accepted `Verified` and `Partial`, `Unchecked`, `Mismatch`, `Invalid`, requests, tokens). `--dry-run` prints the first card's prompt and the estimated size of the run, needs no key and sends nothing.
- `--provider openai` asks an OpenAI-style server (`POST {base}/chat/completions`: Ollama's `/v1`, llama.cpp's `llama-server`) at `--base-url` (default `http://localhost:11434/v1`); `--model` has no default. A key is optional: `MTG_PROPOSE_API_KEY`, sent as a Bearer header with the same care as the Anthropic key. The system prompt is the first message and the same bytes in every request, so the server's prefix cache is reused; the body carries `cache_prompt: true` (llama.cpp) and `response_format: {"type": "json_object"}`, which `--no-json-mode` leaves out for servers that reject it. Only `message.content` is read: a reasoning model such as `gpt-oss` writes its thinking to `message.reasoning`, and the thinking counts against `--max-answer-tokens` (default 16384); an empty answer with `finish_reason: "length"` fails the card with an error that says to raise the limit. The budget, the retries, the repair round and the outputs are those of the Anthropic provider; an answer without `usage` counts its estimate.
- `--schema` sends the JSON schema of a rules file as `response_format: {"type": "json_schema", "json_schema": {"name": "card_rules", "strict": true, "schema": ...}}` in place of the JSON-object mode, so the server's grammar limits the answer to valid shapes; `mtg-cli dsl-schema` prints it. The schema (`mtg_cards::reference::w3a_card_schema`) is built from the same parsed type sources as the reference, with the shapes of the custom deserializers (a `Cost` is a mana string or a list of parts, a fixed `Amount` a bare number), `null` for `Option` fields and the fields the checker fills in left optional. It keeps to the keywords the grammar converters of llama.cpp and Ollama read (`type`, `properties`, `required`, `additionalProperties: false`, `enum`, `items`, `anyOf`, `$ref` to `$defs`, recursion included) and is about 86 KB, limited to 120 KB by a test; it costs no prompt tokens. Every hand-written rules file fits it, and a stored proposal fits it exactly when serde reads it (the redundant `{"Commander": null}` spelling of a unit variant aside). It catches unknown variants, a unit written with a payload or the reverse, an array where a reference goes, two keys in one variant object and broken JSON; it cannot catch a wrong value in a valid shape, such as a player where an object is meant or an empty span. The Anthropic provider ignores the flag and says so.
- `--solved-examples K` (default 0: the hand-written examples above) shows instead the K solved cards nearest to the card: every stored `Verified` non-token card, hand-written or a compiled `card_rules` row, indexed once per run (`mtg_cli::w19b_pairs::retrieve`). The measure is BM25 over the word and word-pair sets of the oracle texts with reminder text dropped, the card's own names as `~`, mana symbols (other than `{T}` and `{Q}`) and numbers masked, plus a boost for each shared Scryfall keyword and card type; long rules weigh less, cards with the same rules count once, ties go by name and the card itself is never its own example. `--examples-train-only` leaves out the held-out cards of the default `export-pairs` split. The examples are part of the card's message, so `--max-input-tokens` counts them.
- `mtg-cli export-pairs --out FILE [--held-out-percent N] [--seed S] [--measure]` (`mtg_cli::w19b_pairs`) writes those solved cards as JSON Lines training pairs: `name`, `oracle_text` and `type_line` as the prompt shows them (faces joined by a blank line and ` // `), `mana_cost`, `rules` in the proposal format, `source` (`corpus` or `compiled`) and `split` (`train` or `held_out`, from a SHA-256 of the seed and the oracle id; 5% held out by default), and prints the counts per source and split. `--measure` adds, with no model, how often the effect, trigger, condition and modification kinds of the K nearest train examples cover those of a held-out card's rules, for K = 3, 5 and 8, against K random examples.
- A server that reads fewer than half the estimated prompt tokens (`usage.prompt_tokens`) cut the prompt to its context window, and the run ends with an error that says to raise it. A server that cannot be reached ends the run after the retries.

**Local model.** The prompt is about 23 thousand tokens, so the server needs a context of at least 32K tokens; `gpt-oss:20b` fits a 16 GB GPU. Nothing is installed permanently: the server comes from the Nix store (removed by `nix-collect-garbage`) and the models live in one directory (removed by `rm -rf`).

```sh
# Terminal 1: Ollama with CUDA, models under the mtg-cli cache, a 32K context.
NIXPKGS_ALLOW_UNFREE=1 nix shell --impure nixpkgs#ollama-cuda -c env OLLAMA_MODELS=$HOME/.cache/mtg-cli/ollama-models OLLAMA_CONTEXT_LENGTH=32768 ollama serve
# Terminal 2: fetch the model into the same directory, then run.
NIXPKGS_ALLOW_UNFREE=1 nix shell --impure nixpkgs#ollama-cuda -c env OLLAMA_MODELS=$HOME/.cache/mtg-cli/ollama-models ollama pull gpt-oss:20b
mtg-cli propose --provider openai --model gpt-oss:20b --sample 20 --out proposals/local
```

**Measured.** A run of 20 sampled cards (seed 20261028) on `gpt-oss:20b`, 91% of it in the VRAM of a 16 GB GPU: 1 accepted `Verified`, 7 `Unchecked`, 1 `Mismatch`, 1 accepted with nothing modelled and 10 `Invalid`, nearly all of them DSL shape errors (an object where a player is expected, an unknown variant, malformed JSON). It takes 23 minutes for 31 requests (11 repair rounds) and 61 thousand output tokens, about 45 tokens a second overall, and 95% of the input tokens come from the prompt cache. Qwen3.8 27B at 4 bits (19 GB) runs 54% on that GPU and times out, so it does not fit.

With `--schema`, Ollama accepts the schema with `gpt-oss:20b` and every answer follows it. Four cards of that run, asked again: Lush Growth goes from `Invalid` (`Mountain` as a card type) to `Unchecked`, The General and Sick and Tired from `Invalid` to `Mismatch`, and Martial Glory stays `Verified`; 6 requests in 82 seconds. The remaining errors are wrong values in a valid shape (`Planeswalker` as the land type, two separate -1/-1 effects for "two target creatures each").

`llama-cpp-cuda` from nixpkgs serves the same API: `llama-server -m MODEL.gguf -c 32768 --port 8080`, then `--base-url http://localhost:8080/v1`. With devenv the same server is a process (untested):

```nix
# devenv.nix; devenv.yaml holds `allowUnfree: true`.
{ pkgs, config, ... }: {
  packages = [ pkgs.ollama-cuda ];
  env.OLLAMA_MODELS = "${config.env.DEVENV_ROOT}/.devenv/ollama-models";
  env.OLLAMA_CONTEXT_LENGTH = "32768";
  processes.ollama.exec = "ollama serve";
}
```

A server on another machine is reached through a tunnel, `ssh -L 11434:localhost:11434 HOST`, so `--base-url` stays `http://localhost:11434/v1` and the server never listens on the network.

## Worked example: Archon of Cruelty

```json
{
  "oracle_id": "aa1a6646-c1e6-4bff-9092-43ee3e137914",
  "name": "Archon of Cruelty",
  "impl_status": "Full",
  "faces": [
    {
      "name": "Archon of Cruelty",
      "oracle_fingerprint": "b68d063ee3b4ff033c607c00f4b59695",
      "abilities": [
        { "Keyword": "Flying" },
        {
          "Triggered": {
            "when": {
              "AnyOf": [
                { "ZoneChange": { "what": ["This"], "to": "Battlefield" } },
                { "Attacks": { "what": ["This"] } }
              ]
            },
            "targets": [{ "what": "Opponent" }],
            "effects": [
              {
                "Sacrifice": {
                  "who": { "Target": 0 },
                  "filter": [{ "AnyOf": [{ "Type": "Creature" }, { "Type": "Planeswalker" }] }]
                }
              },
              { "Discard": { "who": { "Target": 0 } } },
              { "LoseLife": { "who": { "Target": 0 }, "amount": 3 } },
              { "Draw": {} },
              { "GainLife": { "amount": 3 } }
            ]
          }
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

This is `crates/mtg-cards/cards/archon_of_cruelty.json`; a test keeps the two equal. Oracle text: `Flying` / `Whenever this creature enters or attacks, target opponent sacrifices a creature or planeswalker of their choice, discards a card, and loses 3 life. You draw a card and gain 3 life.`

| Feature | In the file |
|---|---|
| One ability, two events | `when: AnyOf [ZoneChange to Battlefield, Attacks]` |
| Target opponent | `targets: [{ what: Opponent }]` |
| Actors differ | `who: Target(0)` for the first three effects; the default `You` for the last two |
| Choice by the target player | `Sacrifice` filters the target's own creatures and planeswalkers; the target player chooses |

| Phase | Engine behaviour |
|---|---|
| Detection | `SpellResolved` or `ZoneChanged { to: Battlefield }` matches the `ZoneChange` arm against the state after the event; `AttackersDeclared` matches `Attacks`. `AbilityTriggered` names the Archon, ability 1, its controller and a snapshot of it. One event triggers once. |
| On the stack | At the next sweep, before a player receives priority: `ChooseTargets` goes to the controller (`triggered`, with no cancel); `AbilityPutOnStack` creates the ability object with the chosen player. |
| Recheck | If the opponent is no longer a legal target (left the game, gained hexproof) the ability fizzles and nothing happens, including the controller's draw and life gain (CR 608.2b). |
| Step 0 `Sacrifice` | `ChooseCards` to the **target** player (not the controller) over their creatures and planeswalkers ("Sacrifice a creature or planeswalker."); with none, or one, nothing is asked. Then `ChoiceMade`, `ZoneChanged { cause: Sacrificed }`, `StepDone`. |
| Step 1 `Discard` | `ChooseCards` from the target's hand ("Discard a card."), addressed and private to them; with an empty hand, or one card, nothing is asked. Then `ChoiceMade` (shown to the other seats without the card), `CardDiscarded`, `StepDone`. |
| Step 2 `LoseLife` | `LifeLost` through the replacement step; `StepDone`. |
| Steps 3, 4 | `Draw` and `GainLife` for the controller; no decisions. `ResolutionEnded { Resolved }`. |
| Afterwards | Triggers caused by the sacrifice, discard and life loss go on the stack at the next sweep (CR 603.3b). |

| The engine needs | Item |
|---|---|
| `Triggered` kind, `AnyOf`, `ZoneChange` and `Attacks` with their events; detection with snapshots | (i) |
| Ability stack object, `AbilityPutOnStack`, journal events, `ChooseTargets` with `Opponent` legality | (c) |
| `ChooseCards` addressed to a non-controller with a response clock | (d) |
| `Sacrifice`, `Discard`, `LoseLife`, `Draw`, `GainLife` with `LifeGained` and `LifeLost` | (f) |
| `Flying` (blocking restriction) | (e) |

## Corpus

`crates/mtg-cards/cards/` holds the corpus cards and the predefined tokens (Treasure, Clue, Food, Gold, Blood, Junk, Powerstone, Vibranium, Map, Mutagen); the tests derive every count from the directory and the fixture.

| Feature | Cards |
|---|---|
| Targets, damage | Lightning Bolt, Burst Lightning (kicker), Fireball (X, even split, cost per extra target), Fire // Ice (split, divided damage, Fuse) |
| Optional costs | Burst Lightning and Gatekeeper of Malakir (kicker, the second with an enters trigger that reads it), Wolfbriar Elemental (multikicker, a Wolf for each payment) (Verified) |
| Impulse draw (Verified) | Professional Face-Breaker (the Treasure it makes in combat and the ability that sacrifices it), Abbot of Keral Keep (prowess, and the card exiled when it enters) and Reckless Impulse (two cards, until the end of the next turn) |
| Required additional costs (Verified) | Thrill of Possibility, Electric Revelation and Laughing Mad (discard a card; the last two with a flashback that pays it again), Cathartic Reunion (discard two), Altar's Reap and Village Rites (sacrifice a creature), Crop Rotation and Harrow (a land), Reshape and Trash for Treasure (an artifact), Withering Boon (pay 3 life), Weigh Down, Makeshift Mauler, Stitched Drake, Headless Skaab, Relentless Skaabs and Skaab Goliath (exile creature cards from the graveyard) |
| Costs with parts beyond mana (Verified) | Capsize (buyback), Dream's Grip (entwine), Voltage Surge ("as an additional cost, you may sacrifice an artifact", read by `AdditionalCostPaid`), Vicious Offering (kicker that sacrifices a creature), Deep Analysis (flashback that pays life), Endurance (evoke that exiles a card from the hand) |
| Recursion keywords (Verified) | Drudge Beetle, Dreg Mangler, Korozda Monitor and Terrus Wurm (scavenge), Dimir Infiltrator, Drift of Phantasms and Muddle the Mixture (transmute), Clutch of Currents, Ruinous Path and Coastal Discovery (awaken), Ominous Harvest and Follow the Bodies (gravestorm), Suntouched Myr and Pentad Prism (sunburst) |
| Graveyard casts and copies (Verified) | Radical Idea, Direct Current and Chemister's Insight (jump-start), Flame Jab and Syphon Life (retrace), Sacred Cat, Tah-Crop Skirmisher and Aven Initiate (embalm), Adorned Pouncer and Proven Combatant (eternalize) |
| Alternative costs and cast permissions (Verified) | Faithless Looting (flashback), Mulldrifter (evoke, with the enters trigger), Cyclonic Rift and Mizzium Mortars (overload), Omniscience (cast from the hand without paying), Vedalken Orrery (as though flash), Fierce Guardianship, Deadly Rollick, Flawless Maneuver and Obscuring Haze (cast free with a commander) and Consign to Dust (strive) |
| Madness and suspend (Verified) | Fiery Temper, Basking Rootwalla and Arrogant Wurm (madness), Rift Bolt, Ancestral Vision and Durkwood Baloth (suspend) |
| Miracle (Verified) | Sister Repentia, Thunderous Wrath, Vanishment, Terminus, Devastation Tide, Banishing Stroke, Redress Fate, Reforge the Soul, Entreat the Angels (with X) |
| Foretell (Verified) | Behold the Multiverse, Saw It Coming and Demon Bolt (instants cast from exile for the foretell cost), Augury Raven (a creature) |
| Costs of activated abilities (Verified) | Flooded Strand (life), Nantuko Husk (a creature sacrificed), Rummaging Goblin (a discard), Soul Shepherd (a creature card exiled from the graveyard), Llanowar Behemoth (a creature tapped), Tumble Magnet (a counter removed), Souldrinker (life for a counter), Lurching Rotbeast (cycling), Copper-Leaf Angel (X lands sacrificed for X counters) and Solstice Zealot (energy gained and paid) |
| Counter, destroy, exile, mass removal | Counterspell, Doom Blade, Swords to Plowshares, Wrath of God (Verified) |
| Pump and control with duration | Giant Growth, Act of Treason (Verified) |
| Library | Rampant Growth, Cultivate, Opt, Brainstorm, Flooded Strand (fetchland), Jace, the Mind Sculptor (planeswalker, Verified) |
| Modes | Izzet Charm |
| Triggers | Mulldrifter (evoke), Eternal Witness, Dockside Extortionist (Treasure), Tireless Tracker (Clue), Hero of Bladehold (attack, tokens, battle cry; Verified), Archon of Cruelty, Delver of Secrets // Insectile Aberration (transform), Felidar Sovereign (intervening if), Rhystic Study, Bonecrusher Giant // Stomp (adventure), Sneak Attack (delayed trigger) |
| Replacement (Verified) | Anointed Procession, Greater Mossdog, Darkblast, Shenanigans and Shambling Shell (dredge), Parallel Lives, Doubling Season (tokens, and counters on a permanent you control), Hardened Scales, Corpsejack Menace (+1/+1 counters on a creature you control), Rhox Faithmender (life), Thought Reflection (draws), Furnace of Rath (damage), Rest in Peace (a graveyard becomes exile) |
| Prevention (Verified) | Fog (all combat damage this turn), Mending Hands (the next 4 damage to any target), Samite Healer (the next 1, from an activated ability with `{T}`), Death Ward (regenerate a target creature) and Drudge Skeletons (`{B}`: regenerate itself) |
| Enters (Verified) | Idyllic Beachfront (tapped), Sodden Verdure (tapped unless two basic lands), Steam Vents (pay 2 life or tapped), Faithful Watchdog (enters with three +1/+1 counters), Hall of Triumph (a colour chosen as it enters, creatures of that colour get +1/+1) and Rally the Ranks (a creature type chosen as it enters, creatures of that type get +1/+1) |
| Static abilities (Verified) | Glorious Anthem, Levitation, Goblin Chieftain (a lord that gives a keyword), Humility (loses abilities, sets power and toughness), Favorable Winds (a keyword in the selector), Jungle Lion (can't block) |
| Layers (Verified) | Nightmare (power and toughness from a count of Swamps, a characteristic-defining ability), Maro (from the cards in hand), Transguild Courier (all colours), Kormus Bell (types, colours and power and toughness of the Swamps); Favorable Winds with Levitation and Goblin Chieftain with Humility are dependencies |
| Rules of the game (Verified) | Darksteel Juggernaut (attacks each combat, power and toughness from a count), Warmonger Hellkite (all creatures attack), Courtly Provocateur (an attack or a block this turn), Supreme Verdict, Carnage Tyrant and Spellbreaker Behemoth (can't be countered, and the creature spells of its controller with power 5 or more), Rampaging Ferocidon (no life gain), Maralen of the Mornsong (no drawing, a search at the draw step), Mindlock Orb (no searching), Goblin Spy (top revealed), Panharmonicon, Teysa Karlov and Naban (triggers again) |
| Abilities from other zones and state triggers (Verified) | Reassembling Skeleton (returns from the graveyard for mana), Soul of Zendikar (exiles itself from the graveyard as a cost), Elemental Masterpiece (discards itself from the hand as a cost), Squee, Goblin Nabob (an upkeep trigger from the graveyard) and Boneyard Scourge (a Dragon dying, with a payment, from the graveyard), Anger (a static ability from the graveyard) and Covetous Dragon (a state trigger) |
| Umbra armor (Verified) | Eel Umbra (flash and a pump), Snake Umbra (a pump and a granted "deals damage to an opponent" trigger), Bear Umbra (a pump and a granted attack trigger that untaps lands) and Octopus Umbra (`SetPT` 8/8 and a granted attack trigger with a "may tap target creature with power 8 or less") |
| Counters (Verified) | Tireless Tracker (a +1/+1 counter on itself when a Clue is sacrificed), Cathars' Crusade (a counter on each creature you control), Inspiring Call (a count and a selector over +1/+1 counters), Caress of Phyrexia (poison counters on a player) |
| Planeswalkers (Verified) | Jace, the Mind Sculptor (look at a library, Brainstorm, bounce, exile a library and shuffle a hand into it), Jace Beleren (each player draws, a target player draws or mills twenty), Ajani, Caller of the Pride (a counter on up to one creature, flying and double strike, a Cat for each life) and Garruk Wildspeaker (untap two lands, a Beast, +3/+3 and trample) |
| Trigger families (Verified) | Skullclamp (the equipped creature dies), Sword of Body and Mind (protection from two colours, combat damage by the equipped creature, a token and "that player" mills), The Thing, Ben Grimm (a batch of damage from Heroes), Lat-Nam Adept (the second card of a turn), Sanguine Bond and Fear of Failed Tests (the amount of the event), Bloodghast (haste while an opponent has 10 or less life, landfall from the graveyard), Seedglaive Mentor (the first time each turn it is targeted by its controller's spell or ability) |
| Monarch followers (Verified) | Archon of Coronation ("As long as you're the monarch, damage doesn't cause you to lose life."), Archivist of Gondor ("your commander" as a source, "that player" in the monarch's end step), M'Baku, Jabari Chieftain ("attacks one of your opponents, if that player is the monarch", `Monarch(Defending)`), Knights of the Black Rose ("if you were the monarch as the turn began", `MonarchAtTurnStart`) Palace Jailer ("until an opponent becomes the monarch") and Regal Behemoth ("while you're the monarch" after the event of a triggered mana ability) |
| Monarch wordings (Verified) | Custodi Lich (a trigger on becoming the monarch, a player sacrifices), Dawnglade Regent ("as long as you're the monarch" before the static), The Spear of Bashenga ("if there is no monarch", an attack at the monarch and a tapped nonland permanent that player controls) and Azure Fleet Admiral ("creatures the monarch controls" cannot block it) |
| Day and night (Verified) | Sunrise Cavalier (a counter on a creature when it changes), Firmament Sage (a card drawn), Brimstone Vandal (1 damage to each opponent), Gavony Dawnguard (look at the top four), The Celestus (the toggle at sorcery speed, life and a loot) and Moonrager's Slash (costs {2} less if it's night); none is in a precon |
| Auras that enchant a player (Verified) | Curse of Predation (a counter on a creature that attacks enchanted player), Curse of the Forsaken (its controller gains life) and Curse of the Nightly Hunt (the creatures enchanted player controls must attack); "creatures enchanted player controls" is `ControlledBy(AttachedPlayer)` |
| Auras (Verified) | Pacifism, Epic Proportions (flash, a pump and a keyword), Control Magic (`SetController` through `Attached`), Squirrel Nest (an Aura on a land that gives it an ability) and Shielded by Faith (`Attach` in a trigger) |
| Exile until the source leaves (Verified) | Banisher Priest, Banishing Light, Cast Out, Conclave Tribunal, White Auracite, Faith Unbroken, Sheltered by Ghosts and Angel of Sanctions: the enters trigger exiles a target "until ~ leaves the battlefield" (`Exile` with `duration: WhileSourceOnBattlefield`) |
| Reveal until a card matches (Verified) | Treasure Hunt (every card revealed goes to the hand), Clifftop Lookout (the land onto the battlefield tapped, the rest on the bottom in a random order), Evolutionary Leap and Foster (the rest on the bottom or into the graveyard): `RevealUntil` binds the matching cards and every card revealed, which stay in the library until a `Move` of the bound set takes them; a card that has moved is no longer the named object, so "the rest" is the revealed set after the match has gone |
| Reveal until X cards match, or a card that may be put (Verified) | Genesis Storm: "You may put that card onto the battlefield. Then put all cards revealed this way that weren't put onto the battlefield on the bottom" reads as `May` around the move of the card found and a move of the revealed set; "reveal X land cards. Put those land cards onto the battlefield tapped" counts X matches (Open the Way stays manual for its restriction on X) |
| Repeat for each, dice and coins (Verified) | Incite Rebellion, Treacherous Terrain, Stormbreath Dragon, Wound Reflection and Archfiend of Despair (a number read for each player), Duskmantle Seer (a body that binds a name in each pass), Mutalith Vortex Beast (a flip inside a repeat for each opponent), Boompile and Goblin Archaeologist (a coin), Earth-Cult Elemental (a table of rows) |
| Doubling counters (Verified) | Kalonian Hydra (each creature the controller has), Primordial Hydra and Big Mother Mouser (the card itself), Vorel of the Hull Clade (each kind on a target): `MultiplyCounters` with `counter` unset for "each kind of counter" and `factor` 3 for "triple" |
| Mana spent to cast (Verified) | Azorius Herald, Patagia Viper and Plaxmanta: "sacrifice it unless {U} was spent to cast it" (`If` of `Not(ManaSpent(Blue))` over a `Sacrifice` of the card) |
| Hideaway (Verified) | Mosswort Bridge (the total power of creatures, a land that enters tapped), Fight Rigging (a creature of power 7 or more after a counter at the beginning of combat), Cemetery Tampering (twenty cards in the graveyard after a mill), Evercoat Ursine (two hideaways, "if there are cards exiled with it", one of them chosen), Smuggler's Buggy (a free cast and "if you do, return this Vehicle", with crew), Windbrisk Heights (three attackers), Spinerock Knoll (7 damage to an opponent), Howltooth Hollow (no cards in any hand), Clive's Hideaway (four legendary creatures) and Rabble Rousing (ten creatures after the tokens); the compiler reproduces all ten |
| Cipher (Verified) | Writ of Return (a creature card returned tapped), Stolen Identity (a token copy of an artifact or creature); `tests/it/cipher.rs` in `mtg-testkit` plays the spell, the combat trigger and the copy at two to four seats. |
| Mutate (Verified) | Cavern Whisperer, Chittering Harvester, Dreamtail Heron, Gemrazer, Glowstone Recluse, Huntmaster Liger, Insatiable Hemophage, Migratory Greathorn, Porcuparrot, Pouncing Shoreshark, Sawtusk Demolisher and Trumpeting Gnarr: `Mutate` with its cost and a trigger on `Mutates` or an ability that reads `Mutations`; Archipelagore (X targets) and Auspicious Starrix (an exile until X permanent cards) keep their triggers with the table |
| Bestow (Verified) | Boon Satyr (flash), Celestial Archon, Ghostblade Eidolon and Chromanticore: `Bestow` with its cost and a static ability on `Attached`; the keyword list after "has" reads with commas ("flying, first strike, vigilance, trample, and lifelink") |
| Level up and Outlast (Verified) | Student of Warfare, Caravan Escort, Coralhelm Commander and Hada Spy Patrol (brackets of a size, keywords and a lord), Kazandu Tuskcaller (a tap ability per bracket), Echo Mage (a copy of a spell, twice at the top level); Abzan Falconer, Tuskguard Captain, Ainok Bond-Kin, Abzan Battle Priest and Mer-Ek Nightblade (outlast, and a keyword for each creature with a +1/+1 counter) |
| Sagas (Verified) | The Birth of Meletis, Binding the Old Gods, Book of Mazarbul, Origin of the Hulk, History of Benalia (a line of two chapters), Vault 75: Middle School (a line of two chapters), Founding of Omashu and Summon: G.F. Ifrit (a creature Saga with two lines of two chapters); Era of Enlightenment, The Modern Age, Befriending the Moths, Okiba Reckoner Raid and Life of Toshiro Umezawa (a transforming Saga: the last chapter exiles it and returns it transformed, the back face is a creature with its own abilities) |
| Saddle (Verified) | Bridled Bighorn (vigilance, a Sheep when it attacks while saddled), Giant Beaver (a counter on a creature that saddled it), Rambling Possum (a pump, then any number of the creatures that saddled it return to their owners' hands), Stubborn Burrowfiend (mill two, then a size that counts the creature cards in the graveyard, the first time it is saddled each turn) |
| Station (Verified) | Galvanizing Sawship (a creature with flying and haste at its one bracket), Lumen-Class Frigate (a lord from 2 charge counters, flying and lifelink from 12), Debris Field Crusher (an activated pump in the last bracket), Hearthhull, the Worldseed (a draw ability, a creature with three keywords and a trigger on sacrificing a land), The Seriema (indestructible for tapped legendary creatures), Uthros Research Craft (a trigger from 3, a pump per artifact at 12), Wedgelight Rammer, Wurmwall Sweeper, Uthros Scanship, Rescue Skiff, Atmospheric Greenhouse and Susurian Dirgecraft (an enters trigger and the last bracket) |
| Classes (Verified) | Wizard Class (no maximum hand size, draw two cards at level 2, a counter whenever you draw at level 3), Stormchaser's Talent (a token as it enters, a return from the graveyard at level 2, a token for each instant or sorcery cast at level 3) |
| Vehicles (Verified) | Smuggler's Copter (an attack or block trigger with a loot), Skysovereign (damage to a target when it enters or attacks), Goliath Truck (counters on another attacking creature), Thopter Fabricator (the second card drawn each turn), each with `Crew N`; Peacewalker Colossus (another target Vehicle becomes an artifact creature), Guidelight Matrix (a Vehicle becomes an artifact creature, a Mount becomes saddled), Tangle Tumbler (tap two untapped tokens), Bespoke Battlewagon (pay four energy) and War Balloon (an artifact creature while it holds three fire counters); Getaway Car (a creature that crewed it returns to its owner's hand), Subterranean Schooner (a creature that crewed it explores) and Mobilizer Mech (up to one other target Vehicle becomes an artifact creature when it becomes crewed) |
| Equipment (Verified) | Bonesplitter (a pump through `Attached`, `Equip {1}`), Swiftfoot Boots (two keywords through `Attached`), Batterskull (living weapon, a pump with two keywords, a bounce to hand) and the Equipment creatures with `Reconfigure`: Lizard Blades (double strike), Bronzeplate Boar (a pump and trample) and Armguard Familiar (a pump and ward, a keyword that brings its own trigger) |
| Legends (Verified) | Arvad the Cursed and Kongming, "Sleeping Dragon" (lords) and Sram, Senior Edificer (a trigger on Aura spells); two of a name meet the legend rule |
| Mana abilities | Llanowar Elves, Sol Ring, Gaea's Cradle, Battlefield Forge (a painland), Lotus Petal |
| Convoke and delve (Verified) | Stoke the Flames and Siege Wurm (convoke), Treasure Cruise, Murderous Cut and Gurmag Angler (delve) |
| Keywords given to spells (Verified) | Inspiring Statuary (nonartifact spells you cast have improvise), Chief Engineer (artifact spells you cast have convoke) and Banish to Another Universe (affinity for historic permanents: an artifact, a legendary permanent or a Saga) |
| Improvise, affinity and typecycling (Verified) | Reverse Engineer, Universal Surveillance, Organic Extinction, Maverick Thopterist, Herald of Anguish and Whir of Invention (improvise), Thoughtcast, Thought Monitor, Memory Guardian, Panther Robot, Spire Golem, Ethersworn Sphinx and Broodstar (affinity), Ash Barrens, Chartooth Cougar, Eagles of the North, Elvish Aberration, Fiery Fall, Generous Ent, Migratory Route, Orchard Strider, Traumatic Visions and Valley Rannet (typecycling) |
| Split second and undaunted (Verified) | Sudden Shock, Sudden Death, Sudden Edict, Krosan Grip, Siege Smash, Stonewood Invocation, Wipe Away, Celestial Crusader and Sulfur Elemental (split second), Sublime Exhalation, Curtains' Call, Seeds of Renewal and Coastal Breach (undaunted) |
| Restricted mana (Verified) | Ancient Ziggurat (creature spells), Mishra's Workshop (artifact spells, three mana), Pillar of the Paruns (multicolored spells), Rosheen Meanderer (costs with {X}), Flamebraider and Ixalli's Lorekeeper (a creature type and its sources), Master of Dark Rites (a list of creature types), Vedalken Engineer (artifacts and their abilities), Troyan (a mana value or an X), Thieving Varmint (spells not owned), Lord of the Forsaken (spells from a graveyard) and The Ringhart Crest (a chosen type or a legendary creature) |
| Mana kept past the step (Verified) | Savage Ventmaw (six mana), Colossal Plow (mana and life), Klauth (mana counted by the attackers, for spells only) and Photon (as much mana as the damage dealt); "Until end of turn, you don't lose this mana as steps and phases end." (`lasts: Turn`) and "Until end of combat, you don't lose this mana as steps end." (`lasts: Combat`) read after the sentence that adds the mana, after "Spend this mana only ..." and after "and you gain N life"; "that much mana of any one color" and "that much {R}" are the amount of the event (`Amount::Triggering`) |
| Mana wordings (Verified) | Unclaimed Territory, Pillar of Origins and Secluded Courtyard (mana for the creature type chosen), Whisperer of the Wilds and Fanatic of Rhonas (Ferocious), Abundant Growth, Gift of Paradise, New Horizons and Weirding Wood (a mana ability an Aura gives), Saltcrusted Steppe, Dreadship Reef, Calciform Pools, Molten Slagheap and Fungal Reaches (X storage counters for X mana of two colors) and Crucible of the Spirit Dragon (mana for Dragons only) |
| Keywords | Serra Angel, Bloodbraid Elf (cascade), Monastery Swiftspear (prowess), Hulking Raptor (ward), Black Knight and Kor Firewalker (protection from a colour) and Mother of Runes (protection from a colour chosen as the ability resolves), Pouncing Jaguar, Goblin War Buggy, Rakdos Headliner (echo with a discard) and Skizzik Surger (echo with a sacrifice), Uktabi Efreet, Morinfen (cumulative upkeep with life) and Phyrexian Soulgorger (with a sacrifice), Young Wolf, Strangleroot Geist and Vengeful Vampire (undying), Putrid Goblin, Safehold Elite and Kitchen Finks (persist), Verified |
| No abilities | Forest, Grizzly Bears |
| `Partial`, `Manual` | Questing Beast (three clauses) |
| Tokens | Treasure (a mana ability, made by Dockside Extortionist), Clue (made by Tireless Tracker) and Food (`{2}` and a sacrifice, paid as any ability with mana); a card that makes a predefined token needs what the abilities of that token need, so its support counts them |

## Cross-check

Vocabulary of other engines against this DSL. Sources: P = phase.rs (MIT or Apache-2.0), X = XMage (MIT), F = Forge scripting vocabulary (GPL-3.0; names only, no code or scripts). Status: `DSL` is in the vocabulary; a letter is the M3 item that adds it; `M6` is added by demand from the coverage dashboard.

| Primitive | In | Status |
|---|---|---|
| `AnyOf` compound triggers (enters or attacks) | P, X | DSL |
| `Pay` ("unless pays", "may pay, if you do") | P, X, F | DSL |
| `DelayedTrigger`, `Rule`, `CostModifier`, `If`, `May`, `Modal` with per-mode targets | P, X, F | DSL |
| `limit` and `from` zone on abilities | P, X, F | DSL |
| A `Modification` for every layer, `CopyOf` token, `WinGame` | P, X | DSL |
| Keyword actions: Fight, Explore, Proliferate, Amass, Connive, Goad, Monstrosity, Adapt, Bolster, Surveil, Time travel, the monarch | P, X, F | DSL (stubs); the engine runs them in (h) |
| Keyword actions: Populate, Learn, Detain, Support, Venture, Manifest, Cloak, Incubate, Suspect | P, X, F | (h); Populate (o), Manifest and Cloak (n) |
| `RevealUntil`, `ExileTop`, `Dig` (look at N and take some is `LookAtTop` + `Choose` + `Move`) | P, X, F | (h) |
| `Exile` with a duration other than the source leaving (until a player's next turn, until an event) | P | (h) |
| Cast permissions (`GameRule::MayCast`: cast from a graveyard, exile or a library, without paying, as though with flash); `Condition::CastWith` | P, X | (g4) |
| `CastFromZone` as an effect (cascade, discover, madness, suspend) | P, X | (g4b), (g4c) |
| Impulse draw (`ExileTop`, `MayCast` with `only` and `play`, `UntilEndOfYourNextTurn`) | P, X | (i4e) |
| `CopySpell`, `BecomeCopy` | P, X, F | (o) |
| Costs: PayLife, Sacrifice, Discard, ExileFromGraveyard, TapPermanents, TapPower (crew), RemoveCounters, ReturnToHand with a fixed amount | P, X, F | the engine announces and pays them (`parts/`, `engine.md`) |
| Costs: PayEnergy, counted amounts (X) | P, X, F | Engine pays them; Solstice Zealot and Copper-Leaf Angel are Verified; (g6b) |
| Costs: RemoveCounters with X, Mill, Reveal, Exert, Unattach, OneOf | P, X, F | (g) |
| `ChooseOption` effect (colour, type, name, player) with a remembered choice | P, X, F | (j) |
| `Condition::Performed` ("if you do") | P, F | after a `May`: the `May` step binds whether it was accepted under the name of its position and the `If` right after it reads that binding (`resolve/prim/{may,if_then}.rs`); a `Sacrifice` and a `RemoveCounters` bind the same name through `resolve/prim/done.rs` (the controller when at least one permanent was sacrificed or one counter taken off, nothing when it did nothing: "Sacrifice it. When you do, ...", "If no counters were removed this way, ..." is `Not(Performed)`); after any other effect the walker names `Condition.Performed.Other` |
| Trigger families: damage, life gained and lost, draw, discard, cycle, tap, untap, tapped for mana, blocks, becomes blocked, a group attacking, counters put and removed, attach | P, X, F | (i4a), done |
| Trigger families: exile, reveal, shuffle, search, copy, counter, activate, transform, token created, monarch, initiative | P, X, F | (i4b) |
| Batch ("one or more"), first time each turn, nth card drawn; state triggers (CR 603.8) | P, X, F | (i4b); state triggers (i3) |
| `Pred` and `Amount` over turn history (entered, attacked, spells cast this turn, life gained), event amount ("that much"), size of a bound set ("that many") | P, X | (i4b) |
| `Amount`: spells cast this turn (Storm) | P, X | (o) |
| Statics: remove abilities or types, grant abilities, change control, can't attack or block | P, X, F | DSL |
| Statics: must attack or block, can't be countered, can't gain life or draw, trigger doubling, can't search, reveal top | P, X, F | DSL |
| Statics: can't be blocked (by a kind of creature) | P, X, F | DSL, engine |
| Statics: no maximum hand size, additional land plays, look at the top card, can be your commander | P, X, F | DSL (stubs); the engine runs them in (h) |
| Choosing a player as a permanent enters | P, X, F | DSL (stub); the engine runs it in (j) |
| Keywords: Crew, Changeling, Devoid, Myriad, Squad, Infect | P, X, F | DSL (stubs); the engine runs each in its keyword task |
| Statics: characteristic-defining flag | P, X | DSL |
| Exchange control | P, X, F | (h) |
| Redirection, `Instead`, shields of events other than damage, replaced events: discard, mill, tap, untap | P, X, F | (j) |
| Skip turn, set or exchange life totals, `LoseGame`, dice, coin, clash, vote, separate into piles | P, X, F | M6 |
| Values: devotion, greatest and total of a characteristic, card types among cards, the monarch, the city's blessing; mana of the chosen colour, of a commander's identity, of what lands could produce | P, X, F | DSL (stubs); the engine reads them in (h) |
| Class levels, opening-hand actions, special actions | P, X | M6 |
| Implicit memory (`RememberCard`, `TrackedSet`, `LastCreated`, `ParentTarget`, `Imprint`) | P, F | not used: `as` and `Bound` name results and are validated |
| Conditions split by context (ability, trigger, static, replacement) | P | not used: one `Condition`, validated per position |

`mtg-cli forge-check --forge <Forge checkout>` sets the stored precon cards against Forge's scripts, which are read at run time and never stored: the verbs Forge names that the rules lack, and the unread tail grouped by Forge API and key (`reference-engines.md`, Cross-check report). Its map from Forge names to the verbs of this DSL is `crates/mtg-cli/src/forge/map.txt`.

## Tasks

The order of the M3 work; `ROADMAP.md` lists the pieces. A structural task changes how abilities, characteristics, events, costs or views are shaped, so it runs alone and before the tasks that fill one stub. The rows after (c) are in build order.

| Item | Delivers | Needs |
|---|---|---|
| (a) DSL | Data model, loader, validation, support records, corpus | |
| (b) Card assembly | `PrintedCard` adapter, `assemble`, `CardDefinition` in `mtg-cards`, corpus cards in the testkit; the deck fingerprint keeps version 1 | (a) |
| (c) Pipeline core | Ability objects, zone-change counter, `Snapshot`, journal and its events, interpreter with one stub file per `Effect` variant, value evaluator, replacement seam, `ChooseTargets` with recheck and fizzle, every new `DecisionKind`, `Answer`, `Event` and redaction arm as types, keyword stubs, engine `Caps` | (b) |
| (e) Keywords | One task each with a table test | (c) |
| (f) Keyword actions | One `Effect` each; discard, mill, sacrifice, the library effects, destroy, exile, counter, tap, untap, winning the game, pay, search and tokens are built | (c) |
| (i) Triggers | Detection, queue, APNAP, delayed, intervening if | (c) |
| (r) Compiler | Core, renderer and goldens, `card_rules` rows, templates for the vocabulary the engine runs, the coverage report, the missing vocabulary added in one pass | (a), (c) |
| (q) Small effects | `If`, `Tap`, `Untap`, `Mill`, `WinGame`, `Flash` | (c) |
| (k) Abilities and layers | One reader of an object's abilities with stable addresses (printed, keyword-provided, granted, lost, by zone), static abilities, every `Modification`, `Rule`, the CR 613 derivation (timestamps, dependencies, characteristic-defining abilities), ward, prowess and battle cry (e2), state triggers and abilities from other zones (i3) | (c) |
| (l) Counters and planeswalkers | Counters on permanents and players with their effects, layer 7c, state-based actions, loyalty abilities | (k) |
| (m) Attachments | `Attach`, Auras (`Enchant`), Equipment (`Equip`, `Reconfigure`), their state-based actions | (k), (l) |
| (j) Replacement | The step, `ChooseReplacement`, one `ReplaceWhen` per replaceable event, enters tapped or with counters, prevention shields, regeneration | (k), (l) |
| (g) Costs and casting | The announcement procedure, X, optional, alternative and additional costs, cost modifiers, cast permissions, costs of activated abilities, complex and restricted mana, `ChooseNumber`, `ChooseCosts`, `DivideAmount` | (k), (j) |
| (i4) Trigger families | Patterns for every event (i4a), attached subjects, batches, first time each turn, event amounts (i4b) | (g), (l), (m) |
| (n) Faces | Multi-face cards, `Effect::Transform`, face-down objects | (g), (k) |
| (o) Copies | Copy effects, spell copies, enters as a copy, populate, storm | (k), (j), (n) |
| (p) Manual cards | A card the engine does not run is cast and resolves without effects; generic logged actions through the primitives | (l), (g) |
| (h) By demand | Keywords and keyword actions the DSL does not declare, sagas, classes, designations: one task each, in the order of the coverage dashboard | the rows above |

(d) Decision kinds are built with the first task that asks for them: `ChooseNumber`, `ChooseCosts` and `DivideAmount` with (g1), `ChooseReplacement` with (j). Their web prompts follow in M5.

The tasks run one at a time in the order of the table; (h) fills stub files and may run in any order. Rules data leads the engine: a compiled card is `Partial` until the engine runs its primitives. From (q) on, a task ends with the templates and goldens for its wordings, and tasks that do not need each other run in the order of the cards the coverage report says they free.

- Scope of (f), the keyword actions of CR 701: draw, discard, mill, shuffle, scry, surveil, fateseal, search, reveal, sacrifice, destroy, exile, counter, create tokens, populate, investigate, fight, explore, proliferate, amass, connive, learn, transform, meld, attach, regenerate, goad, monstrosity, adapt, bolster, support, venture. Arena-only digital actions (conjure, seek, specialize) are out of scope; a card that needs one is `Unplayable` (`deck-check.md`). An action that belongs to a cluster is built there: attach (m), regenerate (j), proliferate (l), transform and meld (n), populate (o); the others are (h).
